📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 近期多项研究聚焦人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的预防、筛查与治疗。一项1b/2期试验显示,新型治疗性HPV疫苗VTP-200虽安全且诱导T细胞应答,但未显著提高高危HPV清除率[10.1093/cid/ciaf658]。同时,系统综述和荟萃分析发现HPV与前列腺癌存在正相关,尤其高危型HPV[10.1002/ijc.70663]。在筛查方面,尿液自采样诊断准确性低于临床采样,但便利性可提高参与度[10.1177/09691413261471634];合并检测策略在低流行区可大幅减少检测量[10.7189/jogh.16.04275]。此外,研究揭示了特定人群的行为风险因素和疫苗接受度障碍,为精准干预提供依据。
  • VTP-200治疗性疫苗在持续高危HPV感染女性中安全且诱导T细胞应答,但12个月时HPV清除率与安慰剂无差异(31.3% vs 33.3%)[10.1093/cid/ciaf658]。
  • 荟萃分析显示,前列腺标本中检测到致癌HPV DNA与前列腺癌风险增加相关(合并OR=2.35),高危型HPV关联更强(OR=2.75)[10.1002/ijc.70663]。
  • 尿液自采样检测高危HPV的敏感性(75%)低于阴道(92%)和宫颈样本(95%),但特异性相当,可作为不愿参与筛查女性的替代方案[10.1177/09691413261471634]。
  • 在HPV低流行区(如孟加拉国,2.10%),合并检测可节省71%-77%的检测量,但可能增加假阴性率[10.7189/jogh.16.04275]。
  • Recent studies on human papillomaviruses (HPV) highlight progress and challenges in prevention, screening, and treatment.
  • The therapeutic vaccine VTP-200 was safe and induced T-cell responses in women with persistent high-risk HPV, but 12-month HPV clearance did not differ from placebo (31.3% vs 33.3%) [10.1093/cid/ciaf658].
  • A meta-analysis found that detection of oncogenic HPV DNA in prostate specimens was associated with increased prostate cancer risk (pooled OR=2.35), with a stronger association for high-risk HPV (OR=2.75) [10.1002/ijc.70663].
  • Urine self-sampling for high-risk HPV detection had lower sensitivity (75%) than vaginal (92%) and cervical samples (95%), but comparable specificity, offering an alternative for women who decline clinician-based screening [10.1177/09691413261471634].
  • In low HPV prevalence settings (e.g., Bangladesh, 2.10%), pooled testing saved 71%–77% of tests but may increase false-negative rates [10.7189/jogh.16.04275].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Clinical infectious diseases : an official publication of the Infectious Diseases Society of America  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-23  |  DOI: 10.1093/cid/ciaf658  |  Date basis: published_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 83, 1, 51-58
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

一项 1b/2 期、随机、安慰剂对照研究,旨在评估 VTP-200(一种黑猩猩腺病毒和改良痘苗安卡拉载体多基因型高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗)在患有低度宫颈病变的女性中的安全性、耐受性、有效性和免疫原性。

A Phase 1b/2, Randomised, Placebo-controlled Study to Evaluate Safety, Tolerability, Efficacy, and Immunogenicity of VTP-200, a Chimpanzee Adenovirus and Modified Vaccinia Ankara-vectored Multigenotype High-risk Human Papillomavirus Vaccine in Women With Low-grade Cervical Lesions.
作者： Karin Hellner, Philippe Simon, Philippe De Sutter, Kadri-Liina Vahula, Paula Briggs, Ülle Kiisla, Michelle Russell, Claire Newton, Kobe Dewilde, Katie Anderson
摘要中文翻译背景:高风险致癌 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 和相关宫颈病变的清除与强大的 T 细胞反应的发展相关。诱导 HPV 抗原特异性 T 细胞的治疗性 疫苗接种 是一种有前途的方法。方法:使用黑猩猩腺病毒 Oxford 1-HPV 和改良痘苗病毒 Ankara-HPV 的 2-媒介 给药策略(两者均编码相同的多抗原 HPV 盒)按 0/28 天的计划给予患有持续性宫颈 hrHPV 和有低度宫颈病变史的参与者。开放标签导入期之后是随机、盲法、安慰剂对照的主要阶段。主要终点是不同剂量方案的安全性;次要终点包括免疫原性以及 12 个月时 hrHPV 和相关病变的清除率。9 名参与者参加了导入试验,99 名参与者以 67:32 的比例分别随机分配到 5 个活性剂量组或安慰剂组。结果:该方案耐受性良好,没有出现 3 级相关的 治疗 紧急不良事件或严重不良反应。所有给药方案均产生抗原特异性 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞反应。合并的活性组和安慰剂组之间的 hrHPV 清除率没有差异(20/64 [31.3%] 与 10/30 [33.3%];P > .99),病灶清除率也没有差异。使用最高剂量的黑猩猩腺病毒 Oxford 1-HPV 观察到 hrHPV 清除率 (60%) 较高的趋势。未证明外周 免疫应答 与清除率之间存在关联。结论:异源、多抗原 HPV 2 成分免疫治疗方案具有良好的耐受性和免疫原性,但在持续 高危型HPV 感染的女性中,并没有导致 hrHPV 或相关宫颈病变的统计学显着清除。试用注册:EudraCT 2019-001890-98;NCT04607850。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高风险致癌 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 和相关宫颈病变的清除与强大的 T 细胞反应的发展相关。诱导 HPV 抗原特异性 T 细胞的治疗性 疫苗接种 是一种有前途的方法。
设计与对象
开放标签导入期之后是随机、盲法、安慰剂对照的主要阶段。9 名参与者参加了导入试验,99 名参与者以 67:32 的比例分别随机分配到 5 个活性剂量组或安慰剂组。
核心方法
使用黑猩猩腺病毒 Oxford 1-HPV 和改良痘苗病毒 Ankara-HPV 的 2-媒介 剂量策略(两者均编码相同的多抗原 HPV 盒)按 0/28 天的计划给予患有持续性宫颈 hrHPV 和有低度宫颈病变史的参与者。
主要结果
该方案耐受性良好,没有出现 3 级相关的 治疗 紧急不良事件或严重不良反应。所有给药方案均产生抗原特异性 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞反应。合并的活性组和安慰剂组之间的 hrHPV 清除率没有差异(20/64 [31.3%] 与 10/30 [33.3%];P > .99),病灶清除率也没有差异。
解释与创新
异源、多抗原 HPV 2 成分免疫治疗方案具有良好的耐受性和免疫原性,但在持续 高危型HPV 感染的女性中,并没有导致 hrHPV 或相关宫颈病变的统计学显着清除。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,虽然 疫苗 是安全的并能诱导 免疫应答,但它并没有显着改善该人群中 hrHPV 的清除或病变消退,表明需要进一步优化治疗性 HPV 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量的限制,并且 hrHPV 清除率缺乏统计学上的显着差异,尽管最高剂量显示出清除率较高的趋势。由于随机设计,证据强度中等,但受到样本量小和缺乏长期随访的限制。

A Phase 1b/2, Randomised, Placebo-controlled Study to Evaluate Safety, Tolerability, Efficacy, and Immunogenicity of VTP-200, a Chimpanzee Adenovirus and Modified Vaccinia Ankara-vectored Multigenotype High-risk Human Papillomavirus Vaccine in Women With Low-grade Cervical Lesions.

Authors: Karin Hellner, Philippe Simon, Philippe De Sutter, Kadri-Liina Vahula, Paula Briggs, Ülle Kiisla, Michelle Russell, Claire Newton, Kobe Dewilde, Katie Anderson
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The clearance of high-risk oncogenic human papillomaviruses (hrHPV) and related cervical lesions is associated with the development of a robust T-cell response. Therapeutic vaccination that induces HPV antigen-specific T cells is a promising approach. METHOD: A 2-vector dosing strategy using Chimpanzee Adenovirus Oxford 1-HPV and Modified Vaccinia virus Ankara-HPV, both encoding the same multiantigen HPV cassette was given on a 0/28-day schedule to participants with persistent cervical hrHPV and a history of low-grade cervical lesions. An open-label lead-in was followed by a randomized, blinded, placebo-controlled main phase. The primary endpoint was the safety of the different dose regimens; secondary endpoints included immunogenicity, and clearance of hrHPV and associated lesions at 12 months. Nine participants were enrolled in the lead-in and 99 were randomized 67:32 to 5 active dose arms or placebo, respectively. RESULTS: The regimens were well-tolerated with no grade 3-related treatment emergent adverse events or serious adverse reactions. All dosing regimens generated antigen-specific CD4+ and CD8+ T-cell responses. There was no difference in hrHPV clearance between the pooled active groups and placebo (20/64 [31.3%] versus 10/30 [33.3%]; P > .99), nor in lesion clearance. A trend toward higher hrHPV clearance (60%) was observed with the highest Chimpanzee Adenovirus Oxford 1-HPV doses. No association between the peripheral immune response and clearance was demonstrated. CONCLUSIONS: A heterologous, multiantigenic HPV 2-component immunotherapy regimen was well-tolerated and immunogenic but did not result in a statistically significant clearance of either hrHPV or the associated cervical lesions in women with persistent high-risk HPV infections. TRIAL REGISTRATION: EudraCT 2019-001890-98; NCT04607850.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The clearance of high-risk oncogenic human papillomaviruses (hrHPV) and related cervical lesions is associated with the development of a robust T-cell response. Therapeutic vaccination that induces HPV antigen-specific T cells is a promising approach.
Design and population
An open-label lead-in was followed by a randomized, blinded, placebo-controlled main phase. Nine participants were enrolled in the lead-in and 99 were randomized 67:32 to 5 active dose arms or placebo, respectively.
Core methods
A 2-vector dosing strategy using Chimpanzee Adenovirus Oxford 1-HPV and Modified Vaccinia virus Ankara-HPV, both encoding the same multiantigen HPV cassette was given on a 0/28-day schedule to participants with persistent cervical hrHPV and a history of low-grade cervical lesions.
Main results
The regimens were well-tolerated with no grade 3-related treatment emergent adverse events or serious adverse reactions. All dosing regimens generated antigen-specific CD4+ and CD8+ T-cell responses. There was no difference in hrHPV clearance between the pooled active groups and placebo (20/64 [31.3%] versus 10/30 [33.3%]; P > .99), nor in lesion clearance.
Interpretation and novelty
A heterologous, multiantigenic HPV 2-component immunotherapy regimen was well-tolerated and immunogenic but did not result in a statistically significant clearance of either hrHPV or the associated cervical lesions in women with persistent high-risk HPV infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that while the vaccine is safe and induces immune responses, it does not significantly improve hrHPV clearance or lesion resolution in this population, indicating a need for further optimization of therapeutic HPV vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its sample size and the lack of statistically significant difference in hrHPV clearance, though the highest dose showed a trend toward higher clearance. The evidence strength is moderate due to the randomized design but limited by the small sample size and lack of long-term follow-up.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

人乳头瘤病毒 与前列腺癌之间的关联:系统评价和荟萃分析

The Association Between Human Papillomavirus and Prostate Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta‐Analysis
作者： Sarah Botting‐Provost, Kristy Ng, Cassandra Laurie, Rodrigo Noorani, Mariam El‐Zein, Eduardo L. Franco, Sarah Botting-Provost, Mariam El-Zein
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在综合 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与前列腺癌之间关联的流行病学证据,解决有关 HPV 在前列腺癌发生中的作用的争议,尽管在前列腺标本中经常检测到 HPV。
设计与对象
对 84 篇文章(1990-2025 年)进行系统回顾和荟萃分析,包括 57 篇横断面研究、15 篇病例对照研究、6 篇巢式病例对照研究和 6 篇 队列研究 研究,以评估 HPV 与前列腺癌之间的关系。
核心方法
研究人员于2025年1月31日对Embase、MEDLINE、Scopus和Cochrane进行了系统检索,由两名独立审稿人筛选文章并提取数据;使用随机效应模型计算汇总风险估计值,并按 HPV 风险水平、基因型和对照组进行亚组分析。
主要结果
The meta-analysis of 39 studies yielded a pooled odds ratio (OR) of 2.35 (95% CI: 1.87–2.95) for HPV and prostate cancer, with a higher OR of 2.75 (95% CI: 1.69–4.46) for high-risk HPV; HPV16, HPV18, and HPV33 showed positive associations, and the strongest association was observed when using normal tissue as controls (OR: 4.81, 95% CI: 1.83–12.69).
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为支持前列腺样本中致癌 HPV DNA 检测与前列腺癌之间的正相关性,尽管他们指出其病因学意义仍不清楚。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明 HPV 在前列腺癌发生中的潜在作用,这可能为 监测 策略和进一步研究 HPV 在前列腺癌发展中的机制提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,受限于研究设计、HPV检测方法的异质性以及无法建立因果关系;大多数研究报告 HPV 患病率 低于 20%,并且 HPV 在前列腺癌中的病因学意义仍不确定。

The Association Between Human Papillomavirus and Prostate Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta‐Analysis

Authors: Sarah Botting‐Provost, Kristy Ng, Cassandra Laurie, Rodrigo Noorani, Mariam El‐Zein, Eduardo L. Franco, Sarah Botting-Provost, Mariam El-Zein
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Human papillomavirus (HPV), the necessary cause of cervical cancer, is associated with an increased risk of other anogenital cancers. While HPV has frequently been detected in prostate specimens, its role in prostate carcinogenesis remains controversial. We synthesized and meta‐analyzed the epidemiological literature on the association between HPV and prostate cancer. We completed a systematic search of Embase, MEDLINE, Scopus and Cochrane on January 31, 2025. Two reviewers independently screened articles and extracted data on study design and population, sample characteristics, HPV and prostate cancer ascertainment methods, and results. We pooled risk estimates using a random effects model and performed subgroup meta‐analyses by HPV risk level, genotype, and control group. Our search identified 1874 unique records. Of 84 included articles (1990–2025), 57 were cross‐sectional, 15 case–control, 6 nested case–control, and 6 were cohort studies. HPV detection methods reported were HPV DNA ( n = 57); HPV serology ( n = 17); subjects' self‐report ( n = 5); chart review, registry or claims data ( n = 4); RNA ( n = 3); and immunohistochemistry ( n = 2). Most studies (58.3%) reported HPV prevalence below 20%. Our meta‐analysis (39 studies) produced a pooled odds ratio (OR) of 2.35 (95% CI: 1.87–2.95). When restricted to high‐risk HPV, the pooled OR was 2.75 (95% CI: 1.69–4.46). HPV16, HPV18, and HPV33 had positive associations with prostate cancer (pooled ORs: 2.28, 95% CI: 1.18–4.40; 2.37, 95% CI: 1.16–4.87; 3.73, 95% CI: 0.46–30.30, respectively) and the association was strongest when using normal tissue as controls (pooled OR:4.81, 95% CI: 1.83–12.69). Our findings support a positive association between the detection of oncogenic HPV DNA in prostate specimens and prostate cancer, although its etiologic significance remains unclear.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to synthesize epidemiological evidence on the association between human papillomavirus (HPV) and prostate cancer, addressing the controversy surrounding HPV's role in prostate carcinogenesis despite frequent detection in prostate specimens.
Design and population
A systematic review and meta-analysis of 84 articles (1990–2025), including 57 cross-sectional, 15 case-control, 6 nested case-control, and 6 cohort studies, was conducted to evaluate the relationship between HPV and prostate cancer.
Core methods
The researchers performed a systematic search of Embase, MEDLINE, Scopus, and Cochrane on January 31, 2025, with two independent reviewers screening articles and extracting data; pooled risk estimates were calculated using a random effects model, with subgroup analyses by HPV risk level, genotype, and control group.
Main results
The meta-analysis of 39 studies yielded a pooled odds ratio (OR) of 2.35 (95% CI: 1.87–2.95) for HPV and prostate cancer, with a higher OR of 2.75 (95% CI: 1.69–4.46) for high-risk HPV; HPV16, HPV18, and HPV33 showed positive associations, and the strongest association was observed when using normal tissue as controls (OR: 4.81, 95% CI: 1.83–12.69).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as supporting a positive association between oncogenic HPV DNA detection in prostate specimens and prostate cancer, though they noted that its etiologic significance remains unclear.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest a potential role for HPV in prostate carcinogenesis, which may inform surveillance strategies and further research into HPV's mechanisms in prostate cancer development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, limited by the heterogeneity of study designs, HPV detection methods, and the inability to establish causality; most studies reported HPV prevalence below 20%, and the etiologic significance of HPV in prostate cancer remains uncertain.
DOI · PubMed · 来源DOI · PubMed · 来源
Journal: Revista da Associacao Medica Brasileira (1992)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-20  |  DOI: 10.1590/1806-9282.20260122  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 72, 6, e20260122
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

自行采集与临床医生采集的 人乳头瘤病毒 样本。

Self-collected versus clinician-collected human papillomavirus samples.
作者： Ramazan Bülbü, Jule Eriç Horasanlı, Arif Caner Erdoğan, Fatih Akkuş
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估自我收集和临床医生收集的样本在 人乳头瘤病毒 检测、细胞学结果和传染原鉴定方面的可比性。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性对照临床试验招募了 124 名 30-65 岁的女性,她们有资格接受常规 宫颈癌 筛查。根据诊所登记顺序,通过系统分配,将参与者分配到临床医生收集的对照组(n = 51)或自行收集的组(n = 73)。
核心方法
通过多重实时聚合酶链式反应进行病毒脱氧核糖核酸分析;细胞学评估遵循Bethesda分类系统。还评估了传染源和参与者的满意度。实验室人员对分组分配不知情。
主要结果
Overall human papillomavirus positivity was 21.8% (27/124), with rates of 27.5% (14/51) in the control group and 17.8% (13/73) in the self-collected group (p=0.200). Cytological abnormalities were detected in 5.5% of self-collected samples versus 0% in the control group (p=0.236). Inflammatory smear findings, Candida (9.8 vs. 8.2%), and Trichomonas vaginalis (0 vs. 1.4%) detection rates were similar between groups. Mean satisfaction score was 12.6±1.62 out of 16 (78.8% positive responses).
解释与创新
自行采集的 人乳头瘤病毒 采样产生的诊断结果与临床医生采集的标本相当。与临床医生采集的样本相比,自行采集的 人乳头瘤病毒 采样显示出相似的 人乳头瘤病毒 检出率。
科研与公卫意义
鉴于其可行性和较高的可接受性,自行收集可以作为 宫颈癌 筛查计划的有效补充方法,特别是对于以前未参与筛查的女性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度评估的具体局限性。

Self-collected versus clinician-collected human papillomavirus samples.

Authors: Ramazan Bülbü, Jule Eriç Horasanlı, Arif Caner Erdoğan, Fatih Akkuş
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: The aim of this study was to evaluate the comparability of self-collected and clinician-collected samples for human papillomavirus detection, cytological findings, and identification of infectious agents. METHODS: This prospective controlled clinical trial enrolled 124 women aged 30-65 years due for routine cervical cancer screening. This prospective comparative study enrolled 124 women aged 30-65 years who were eligible for routine cervical cancer screening. Participants were allocated to either a clinician-collected control group (n=51) or a self-collected group (n=73) via systematic allocation based on clinic registration order. Viral deoxyribonucleic acid analysis was performed by multiplex real-time polymerase chain reaction; cytological evaluation followed the Bethesda classification system. Infectious agents and participant satisfaction were also assessed. Laboratory personnel were blinded to group allocation. RESULTS: Overall human papillomavirus positivity was 21.8% (27/124), with rates of 27.5% (14/51) in the control group and 17.8% (13/73) in the self-collected group (p=0.200). Cytological abnormalities were detected in 5.5% of self-collected samples (three cases of atypical squamous cells of undetermined significance, one case of atypical squamous cells cannot exclude high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion) versus 0% in the control group (p=0.236). Inflammatory smear findings, Candida (9.8 vs. 8.2%), and Trichomonas vaginalis (0 vs. 1.4%) detection rates were similar between groups. Mean satisfaction score was 12.6±1.62 out of 16 (78.8% positive responses). CONCLUSION: Self-collected human papillomavirus sampling yields results diagnostically comparable to clinician-collected specimens. Self collected human papillomavirus sampling demonstrated similar human papillomavirus detection rates compared with clinician-collected specimens. Given its feasibility and high acceptability, self-collection may serve as an effective complementary approach in cervical cancer screening programs, particularly for reaching women who have not previously participated in screening.

中国大学 MSM 肛门 HPV 感染相关行为特征:潜在类别分析

Behavioral profiles associated with anal HPV infection among MSM attending university in China: a latent class analysis
作者： Song Fan, Yingming Wang, Ningjun Ren, Maohe Yu, Lin Ouyang, Huachun Zou, Ouyang Lin
摘要中文翻译目的:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是在参加大学就读的男男性行为者(MSM)中普遍存在的性传播感染(STI)。我们的研究旨在使用潜在类别分析(LCA)确定该人群中肛门HPV感染的不同行为特征,并评估与高风险行为相关的因素。方法:我们于2019年1月至4月在中国大学就读的MSM中招募了参与者,这些参与者参加了"大学学生艾滋病检测干预研究"(UniTest研究)。参与者完成了关于社会人口学和性行为特征的自填式在线问卷。参与者自行采集肛门拭子进行HPV基因分型。LCA根据性行为及相关风险行为将个体分为肛门HPV感染的高风险组和低风险组。结果:LCA在292名大学就读的MSM中确定了两个不同的组。第2组的肛门HPV感染率显著升高(52.90%,P < 0.001),而第1组为25.55%。值得注意的是,第2组的特点是更多地参与临时性行为(74.15% vs. 42.87%)、有非学生性伴侣(77.24% vs. 38.22%)、在过去一年内涉及醉酒性行为(26.40% vs. 10.90%)、新型精神活性物质(NPS)使用性行为史(53.13% vs. 12.88%),以及更高的患病率的HIV检测史(98.80% vs. 这些结果表明,高风险行为特征与观察到的更高的患病率肛门HPV感染率相关。结论:我们的研究确定了中国大学就读的MSM中与肛门HPV感染相关的不同行为特征。有针对性的干预措施应解决物质使用(酒精和NPS)、强调坚持使用安全套的性健康教育,以及在学生健康项目中推广HPV 疫苗接种。校园HPV检测服务代表了一种潜在有价值的服务模式,但在常规实施之前,需要进一步评估其可行性、可接受性以及与现有卫生基础设施的整合情况。试验注册:中国临床试验注册中心,www.chictr.org.cn:ChiCTR1900020645。注册日期:2019.09.11。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是一种在大学男男性接触者 (MSM) 中流行的性传播感染 (STI)。我们的研究旨在利用潜在类别分析 (LCA) 确定该人群中肛门 HPV 感染的不同行为特征,并评估与高危行为相关的因素。
设计与对象
我们于 2019 年 1 月至 4 月在中国开展了大学生 HIV 检测干预研究(UniTest 研究),招募了在校的 MSM。参与者完成了一份关于社会人口统计和性行为特征的自填在线调查问卷,并自行收集肛门拭子进行 HPV 基因分型。
核心方法
LCA 根据性行为和相关危险行为将个体分为肛门 HPV 感染高危组和低危组。
主要结果
LCA 在 292 名就读大学的 MSM 中确定了两个不同的群体。与1类组(25.55%)相比,2类组的肛门HPV感染率显着升高(52.90%,P < 0.001)。2级组的特点是随意性行为的参与度较高(74.15% vs. 42.87%),有非学生性伴侣(77.24% vs. 38.22%),过去一年有醉酒性行为(26.40% vs. 10.90%),新型精神活性物质(NPS)使用性行为史(53.13% vs. 12.88%),以及更高的 患病率 HIV 检测史(98.80% vs.
解释与创新
这些发现表明,较高风险的行为特征与观察到的较高的肛门 HPV 感染 患病率 相关。我们的研究发现了中国大学男男性接触者中与肛门 HPV 感染相关的不同行为特征。
科研与公卫意义
需要针对物质使用(酒精和 NPS)、强调持续使用安全套的性健康教育以及在学生健康计划中宣传 HPV 疫苗接种 等问题采取量身定制的干预措施。校园HPV检测服务代表了一种潜在有价值的服务模式,但其可行性、可接受性以及与现有卫生基础设施的整合需要在常规实施之前进行进一步评估。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计中的具体局限性或偏见。证据强度中等,因为该研究确定了行为关联,但需要进一步评估干预的可行性和可接受性。

Behavioral profiles associated with anal HPV infection among MSM attending university in China: a latent class analysis

Authors: Song Fan, Yingming Wang, Ningjun Ren, Maohe Yu, Lin Ouyang, Huachun Zou, Ouyang Lin
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a prevalent sexually transmitted infection (STI) among men who have sex with men (MSM) attending university. Our study aimed to identify distinct behavioral profiles for anal HPV infection in this population using Latent Class Analysis (LCA) and assess factors associated with high-risk behavior. METHODS: We recruited MSM attending university in the University Student HIV Test Intervention Study (UniTest Study) between January and April 2019 in China. Participants completed a self-administered online questionnaire on socio-demographic and sexual behavior characteristics. Participants self-collected anal swabs for HPV genotyping. LCA classified individuals into high and low-risk groups for anal HPV infection based on sexual and related risk behaviors. RESULTS: LCA identified two distinct groups among 292 MSM attending university. The class 2 group exhibited a significantly elevated anal HPV infection rate (52.90%, P < 0.001) compared to the class 1 group (25.55%). Notably, the class 2 group was characterized by higher engagement in casual sex (74.15% vs. 42.87%), having non-student sex partners (77.24% vs. 38.22%), involvement in drunken sex in the past year (26.40% vs. 10.90%), Novel Psychoactive Substances (NPS) use sex history (53.13% vs. 12.88%), and a higher prevalence of HIV testing history (98.80% vs. These findings indicate that the higher-risk behavioral profile was associated with a higher observed prevalence of anal HPV infection. CONCLUSION: Our study identified distinct behavioral profiles associated with anal HPV infection among MSM attending university in China. Tailored interventions addressing substance use (alcohol and NPS), sexual health education emphasizing consistent condom use, and the promotion of HPV vaccination within student health programs are warranted. On-campus HPV testing services represent a potentially valuable service model, but their feasibility, acceptability, and integration into existing health infrastructure require further evaluation before routine implementation. TRIAL REGISTRATION: Chinese Clinical Trial Registry, www.chictr.org.cn : ChiCTR1900020645. Date of Registration: 2019.09.11.

埃塞俄比亚南部阿勒地区有符合资格女儿的农村父母对 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 的感知促进因素和障碍

Perceived facilitators and barriers against human papillomavirus vaccine among rural parents with eligible daughters in the Alle district, Southern Ethiopia
作者： Selemaye Zenebe, Tomas Yeheyis, Fikru Tadesse, Bedilu Deribe, Gezhagn Geresu, Tsehaynew Kasse, Susanne Unverzagt, Eric Sven Kroeber, Eva Johanna Kantelhardt, Lesley Taylor
摘要中文翻译简介:人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 的接受程度受到家长对 疫苗 认知的影响,但埃塞俄比亚的相关研究很少,大多数都集中在定量方法上。因此,本定性研究旨在探讨家长对 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 接受的看法。目的:探讨埃塞俄比亚南部阿勒地区有符合条件的女儿的农村父母对 人乳头瘤病毒 的感知促进因素和障碍。方法:2023 年 4 月 25 日至 2023 年 5 月 25 日,对埃塞俄比亚南部阿勒地区符合条件的女儿家长进行了一项定性研究,采用焦点小组讨论和深入访谈的方式进行。我们随机抽取了 53 名家长进行焦点小组讨论,并特意抽取了 15 名家长进行深度访谈。数据通过半结构化焦点小组讨论指南和深度访谈问卷收集。访谈资料转录后翻译成英文,并通过Atlas软件7.1.16版进行主题分析。结果:在所有参与者中,66.2%是女性父母,60.2%是新教信徒。调查结果分为两个主要主题:人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 接受的感知促进因素和障碍。所确定的感知促进者正在阻止 宫颈癌,将其视为父母角色和责任的表达,并相信卫生专业人员作为 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 促进者的建议。已确定的 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 感知障碍包括:缺乏认识、缺乏可靠的信息来源、对 疫苗 缺乏信任、误解、对副作用的恐惧以及影响 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 接受的文化和宗教因素。结论:家长确定了接受 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 的各种促进因素和障碍。因此,我们建议政府和相关机构合作解决已发现的挑战,以提高 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的接受度。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
之所以需要这项研究,是因为关于埃塞俄比亚 HPV 疫苗 接受度的研究很少,大多数都侧重于定量方法,其目的是探讨父母对 HPV 疫苗 接受度的看法。
设计与对象
2023年4月25日至2023年5月25日,对埃塞俄比亚南部阿勒地区符合条件的女儿家长进行了一项采用焦点小组讨论和深度访谈的定性研究,方便样本包括53名焦点小组家长和15名受访家长。
核心方法
使用半结构化焦点小组讨论指南和深度访谈问卷收集数据,转录后翻译成英文,并使用 Atlas 软件版本 7.1.16 进行主题分析。
主要结果
在参与者中,66.2% 是女性父母,60.2% 是新教信徒,调查结果分为 HPV 疫苗 接受的促进因素和障碍因素。
解释与创新
作者解释说,父母发现了接受 HPV 疫苗 的各种促进因素和障碍,该研究提供了对埃塞俄比亚农村背景下这些因素的定性见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息,以解决已发现的障碍并利用促进因素,通过政府和卫生机构之间的合作,有可能提高埃塞俄比亚农村地区对 HPV 疫苗 的接受度。
局限与证据强度
由于定性设计和有限的样本量,证据强度适中,这可能会限制普遍性,尽管该研究提供了有价值的针对特定背景的见解。

Perceived facilitators and barriers against human papillomavirus vaccine among rural parents with eligible daughters in the Alle district, Southern Ethiopia

Authors: Selemaye Zenebe, Tomas Yeheyis, Fikru Tadesse, Bedilu Deribe, Gezhagn Geresu, Tsehaynew Kasse, Susanne Unverzagt, Eric Sven Kroeber, Eva Johanna Kantelhardt, Lesley Taylor
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Human papillomavirus vaccine acceptance is influenced by the parental perceptions of the vaccine, but there are few related studies in Ethiopia, and most have focused on quantitative approaches. Therefore, this qualitative study intended to explore parental perceptions about the human papillomavirus vaccine acceptance. OBJECTIVE: To explore the perceived facilitators and barriers against human papillomavirus among rural parents with eligible daughters in the Alle district, southern Ethiopia. METHODS: A qualitative study using focus group discussion and in-depth interviews was conducted from April 25, 2023, to May 25, 2023, among eligible parents of daughters in the Alle district, southern Ethiopia. A convenience sample of 53 parents was recruited for focus group discussions, and 15 parents were purposely sampled for in-depth interviews. The data were collected by a semi-structured focus group discussion guide and in-depth interview questionnaires. The interview data were translated into English after transcription, and thematic analysis was done by Atlas software version 7.1.16. RESULTS: Among the total participants, 66.2% were female parents and 60.2% were protestant religious followers. Findings were split between two major themes: perceived facilitators and barriers to human papillomavirus vaccine acceptance. The perceived facilitators identified were preventing cervical cancer, seeing it as an expression of parental role and responsibility, and believing in the recommendation of health professionals as facilitators of human papillomavirus vaccination. The identified perceived barriers to human papillomavirus vaccination included: a lack of awareness, a lack of reliable information sources, a lack of trust in the vaccine, misconceptions, fear of side effects, and cultural and religious factors affecting the acceptance of human papillomavirus vaccination. CONCLUSION: Parents identified various facilitators and barriers to accepting the human papillomavirus vaccine. Therefore, we recommend that the government and concerned institutions work in collaboration to address the identified challenges to improve the acceptance of human papillomavirus vaccination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed because there are few studies on HPV vaccine acceptance in Ethiopia, with most focusing on quantitative approaches, and it aimed to explore parental perceptions about HPV vaccine acceptance.
Design and population
A qualitative study using focus group discussions and in-depth interviews was conducted from April 25, 2023, to May 25, 2023, among eligible parents of daughters in the Alle district, southern Ethiopia, with a convenience sample of 53 parents for focus groups and 15 parents for interviews.
Core methods
Data were collected using semi-structured focus group discussion guides and in-depth interview questionnaires, translated into English after transcription, and analyzed thematically using Atlas software version 7.1.16.
Main results
Among participants, 66.2% were female parents and 60.2% were Protestant religious followers, with findings split into perceived facilitators and barriers to HPV vaccine acceptance.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that parents identified various facilitators and barriers to accepting the HPV vaccine, with the study providing qualitative insights into these factors in a rural Ethiopian context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted interventions to address identified barriers and leverage facilitators, potentially improving HPV vaccine acceptance in rural Ethiopia through collaboration between government and health institutions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the qualitative design and limited sample size, which may limit generalizability, though the study provides valuable context-specific insights.

Journal: Archives of Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-20  |  DOI: 10.1007/s00705-026-06691-5  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 171, 8, 230

HPV 诊断中的类型特异性敏感性与检测广度:通过巢式 PCR 改进 HPV16/18 的分析检测

Type-specific sensitivity versus detection breadth in HPV diagnostics: improved analytical detection of HPV16/18 by nested PCR
作者： Yago Tomaz Vieira da Silva, Larissa Alves Honorato Ferreira, Samara Fontes de Lima Gomes, Beatriz Helena Dantas Rodrigues De Albuquerque, Maryana Thalyta Ferreira Câmara De Oliveira, Alice Dantas Leite, José Veríssimo Fernandes, Ricardo Ney Oliveira Cobucci, Daniel Carlos Ferreira Lanza, De Albuquerque BHDR
摘要中文翻译摘要 准确检测高危型 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是 宫颈癌 筛查的核心。当前的诊断策略越来越优先考虑广谱检测,以最大限度地提高基因型覆盖率。然而,当临床显性靶标以低拷贝数存在时,理论基因型宽度不一定转化为有效的样本水平检测。在这里,我们使用传统 PCR 作为受控实验框架来评估这种分析权衡。针对 HPV16 和 HPV18 的类型特异性巢式 PCR 检测已开发出来并应用于 41 个宫颈样本。巢式 PCR 显示出比单轮扩增明显更高的相对分析灵敏度,并且比此处测试的广谱共识系统(MY09/11 和 GP5/6)产生更大比例的特异性和可解释的检测。在测试条件下,通过巢式PCR在22/41个样本中检测到HPV16和/或HPV18 DNA,而MY09/11和GP5/6分别检测到11/41和2/41个样本。这些发现表明广泛的基因型覆盖和阳性样本的有效检测是独特的分析特性。在普遍 HPV 靶标丰度较低的情况下,与更广泛但敏感性较低的共识方法相比,高度敏感的基因型重点检测可能会揭示更多的靶标阳性样本。这些结果并非旨在取代经过临床验证的广谱筛查测定,但它们支持在反射测试和未来的多目标 HPV 测定设计中明确保留每个目标的分析灵敏度。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
准确检测高危型 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是 宫颈癌 筛查的核心。当前的诊断策略越来越优先考虑广谱检测,以最大限度地提高基因型覆盖率。
设计与对象
该研究使用传统 PCR 作为受控实验框架,评估了类型特异性灵敏度和检测广度之间的分析权衡。针对 HPV16 和 HPV18 的类型特异性巢式 PCR 检测已开发出来并应用于 41 个宫颈样本。
核心方法
巢式 PCR 显示出比单轮扩增明显更高的相对分析灵敏度,并且比此处测试的广谱共识系统(MY09/11 和 GP5/6)产生更大比例的特异性和可解释的检测。
主要结果
在测试条件下,通过巢式PCR在22/41个样本中检测到HPV16和/或HPV18 DNA,而MY09/11和GP5/6分别检测到11/41和2/41个样本。
解释与创新
这些发现表明广泛的基因型覆盖和阳性样本的有效检测是独特的分析特性。
科研与公卫意义
结果支持在反射测试和未来的多靶点 HPV 检测设计中明确保留每个靶点的分析灵敏度。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 41 个宫颈样本,其结果无意取代经过临床验证的广谱筛查测定。

Type-specific sensitivity versus detection breadth in HPV diagnostics: improved analytical detection of HPV16/18 by nested PCR

Authors: Yago Tomaz Vieira da Silva, Larissa Alves Honorato Ferreira, Samara Fontes de Lima Gomes, Beatriz Helena Dantas Rodrigues De Albuquerque, Maryana Thalyta Ferreira Câmara De Oliveira, Alice Dantas Leite, José Veríssimo Fernandes, Ricardo Ney Oliveira Cobucci, Daniel Carlos Ferreira Lanza, De Albuquerque BHDR
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Accurate detection of high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) is central to cervical cancer screening. Current diagnostic strategies increasingly prioritize broad-spectrum assays to maximize genotype coverage. However, theoretical genotype breadth does not necessarily translate into effective sample-level detection when clinically dominant targets are present at low copy number. Here, we evaluated this analytical trade-off using conventional PCR as a controlled experimental framework. Type-specific nested PCR assays targeting HPV16 and HPV18 were developed and applied to 41 cervical samples. Nested PCR showed markedly higher relative analytical sensitivity than single-round amplification and produced a greater proportion of specific and interpretable detections than the broad-spectrum consensus systems tested here (MY09/11 and GP5/6). HPV16 and/or HPV18 DNA was detected by nested PCR in 22/41 samples, whereas MY09/11 and GP5/6 detected 11/41 and 2/41 samples, respectively, under the tested conditions. These findings indicate that broad genotype coverage and effective detection of positive samples are distinct analytical properties. In settings where prevalent HPV targets occur at low abundance, a highly sensitive genotype-focused assay may reveal more target-positive samples than a broader but less sensitive consensus approach. The results are not intended to replace clinically validated broad-spectrum screening assays, but they support explicit preservation of per-target analytical sensitivity in reflex testing and future multi-target HPV assay design.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Accurate detection of high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) is central to cervical cancer screening. Current diagnostic strategies increasingly prioritize broad-spectrum assays to maximize genotype coverage.
Design and population
The study evaluated the analytical trade-off between type-specific sensitivity and detection breadth using conventional PCR as a controlled experimental framework. Type-specific nested PCR assays targeting HPV16 and HPV18 were developed and applied to 41 cervical samples.
Core methods
Nested PCR showed markedly higher relative analytical sensitivity than single-round amplification and produced a greater proportion of specific and interpretable detections than the broad-spectrum consensus systems tested here (MY09/11 and GP5/6).
Main results
HPV16 and/or HPV18 DNA was detected by nested PCR in 22/41 samples, whereas MY09/11 and GP5/6 detected 11/41 and 2/41 samples, respectively, under the tested conditions.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings indicate that broad genotype coverage and effective detection of positive samples are distinct analytical properties.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results support explicit preservation of per-target analytical sensitivity in reflex testing and future multi-target HPV assay design.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to 41 cervical samples, and the results are not intended to replace clinically validated broad-spectrum screening assays.

喉外科医生和听力医师的职业 人乳头瘤病毒 暴露风险:欧洲喉科协会 (ELS) 和欧洲听力医师联盟 (UEP) 德尔菲联合临床共识声明

Occupational human papillomavirus exposure risks for laryngeal surgeons and phoniatricians: A joint European Laryngological Society (ELS) and Union of European Phoniatricians (UEP) Delphi clinical consensus statement
作者： Shi Ying Hey, Alberto Maria Saibene, Milan R Amin, Jennifer Anderson, Christoph Arens, Simon R Best, Guillermo Campos, Craig Derkay, Gauthier Desuter, Frederik G. Dikkers
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 对上呼吸消化道中的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关疾病(如复发性呼吸道乳头状瘤病 (RRP))进行激光手术治疗时会产生手术烟雾,这可能是喉外科医生、语音科医生、麻醉人员和护士的职业病毒暴露来源。尽管人们的认识不断提高,但保护措施的实施仍然不一致。本研究旨在制定一份循证临床共识声明 (CCS),以指导喉科职业 HPV 风险管理。方法 在欧洲喉科协会 (ELS) 和欧洲语音医师联盟 (UEP) 的支持下进行了修改后的德尔菲共识流程。由 26 名国际专家组成的小组对 18 项声明进行了三轮投票,涉及五个领域:定义和风险认知、个人防护措施、排烟和通风、预防性 疫苗接种 以及进一步研究领域。结果 所有小组成员都完成了全部三轮。18份声明中,1份达成强烈共识,7份达成共识,1份达成接近共识,6份未达成共识。各轮中合并了三份声明。达成共识的关键领域包括认识到手术烟雾是潜在的职业 HPV 暴露风险、标准手术口罩的不足、向可能职业暴露的医护人员提供 HPV 疫苗接种 的重要性,以及需要进一步研究的领域。结论 ELS 和 UEP CCS 联合代表了第一个国际协调行动,旨在解决喉科职业 HPV 暴露问题。虽然在几个关键领域达成了共识,但研究结果强调需要进行有针对性的研究,并在保护措施、教育提供和监管框架方面提高一致性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决喉外科医生和耳科医师的职业 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 暴露风险,重点关注手术烟雾作为复发性呼吸道乳头状瘤病治疗等手术过程中病毒 传播 的潜在来源。
设计与对象
修改后的德尔菲共识流程是在欧洲喉科协会 (ELS) 和欧洲语音医师联盟 (UEP) 的支持下进行的,涉及 26 名国际专家进行了三轮投票。
核心方法
该小组通过迭代投票和建立共识,评估了五个领域(定义和风险感知、个人防护措施、排烟和通风、预防性 疫苗接种 以及进一步研究领域)的 18 项声明。
主要结果
所有小组成员均完成了全部三轮;18份声明中,1份达成强烈共识,7份达成共识,1份达成接近共识,6份未达成共识,3份声明跨轮合并。
解释与创新
这份 ELS 和 UEP 联合共识声明代表了第一个国际协调一致的努力,以解决喉科职业 HPV 暴露问题,强调了达成一致的关键领域和仍然存在的不确定性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要有针对性的研究、一致的保护措施、教育提供和监管框架,以减轻医疗机构中的职业 HPV 暴露风险。
局限与证据强度
尽管德尔菲法提供了一种综合专家意见的结构化方法,但证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于专家共识而不是经验数据。

Occupational human papillomavirus exposure risks for laryngeal surgeons and phoniatricians: A joint European Laryngological Society (ELS) and Union of European Phoniatricians (UEP) Delphi clinical consensus statement

Authors: Shi Ying Hey, Alberto Maria Saibene, Milan R Amin, Jennifer Anderson, Christoph Arens, Simon R Best, Guillermo Campos, Craig Derkay, Gauthier Desuter, Frederik G. Dikkers
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purpose Laser surgical management of human papillomavirus (HPV)-related disease, such as recurrent respiratory papillomatosis (RRP), in the upper aerodigestive tract generates surgical smoke, which may represent a source of occupational viral exposure for laryngeal surgeons, phoniatricians, anesthesia providers and scrub nurses. Despite growing awareness, protective measures remain inconsistently implemented. This study aimed to develop an evidence-informed clinical consensus statement (CCS) to guide occupational HPV risk management in laryngology. Methods A modified Delphi consensus process was conducted under the auspices of the European Laryngological Society (ELS) and the Union of the European Phoniatricians (UEP). A panel of 26 international experts undertook three rounds of voting on 18 statements across five domains: definitions and risk perception, personal protective measures, smoke evacuation and ventilation, preventive vaccination, and areas for further research. Results All panellists completed all three rounds. Of the 18 statements, one reached strong consensus, seven reached consensus, one reached near consensus and six did not reach consensus. Three statements were merged across rounds. Key areas of consensus included the recognition of surgical smoke as a potential occupational HPV exposure risk, the inadequacy of standard surgical masks, the importance of offering HPV vaccination to healthcare workers with potential occupational exposure, alongside areas for further research. Conclusion This joint ELS and UEP CCS represents the first internationally coordinated effort to address occupational HPV exposure in laryngology. While consensus was reached in several key areas, the findings underscore the need for targeted research and greater consistency in protective measures, educational provision and regulatory frameworks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the occupational human papillomavirus (HPV) exposure risks for laryngeal surgeons and phoniatricians, focusing on surgical smoke as a potential source of viral transmission during procedures like recurrent respiratory papillomatosis management.
Design and population
A modified Delphi consensus process was conducted under the auspices of the European Laryngological Society (ELS) and the Union of the European Phoniatricians (UEP), involving 26 international experts across three rounds of voting.
Core methods
The panel evaluated 18 statements across five domains (definitions and risk perception, personal protective measures, smoke evacuation and ventilation, preventive vaccination, and areas for further research) through iterative voting and consensus-building.
Main results
All panellists completed all three rounds; of the 18 statements, one reached strong consensus, seven reached consensus, one reached near consensus, and six did not reach consensus, with three statements merged across rounds.
Interpretation and novelty
This joint ELS and UEP consensus statement represents the first internationally coordinated effort to address occupational HPV exposure in laryngology, highlighting key areas of agreement and remaining uncertainties.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for targeted research, consistent protective measures, educational provision, and regulatory frameworks to mitigate occupational HPV exposure risks in healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on expert consensus rather than empirical data, though the Delphi method provides a structured approach to synthesizing expert opinions.

伊朗德黑兰转诊至三级妇科肿瘤诊所的女性对 人乳头瘤病毒 和 宫颈癌 的态度和知识

Attitudes and Knowledge About Human Papillomavirus and Cervical Cancer among Women Referred to a Tertiary Gynecologic Oncology Clinic, Tehran, Iran
作者： Elham Shirali, Fariba Yarandi, Elham Feizabad, Mariyah Zehra, Saba Sabet, Sara Ramhormozian, Arash Jafarieh, Pershang Nazemi
摘要中文翻译背景和目标:宫颈癌 是全球主要的公共卫生问题。本研究的目的是评估在三级妇科诊所就诊的女性对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 和 宫颈癌 的态度和知识。材料和方法:这项描述性横断面调查对 2023 年至 2024 年德黑兰医科大学附属亚斯综合医院妇科诊所转诊的 139 名女性进行。数据收集通过面对面采访。使用 SPSS(IBM SPSS Statistics for Windows,版本 25)进行数据分析。P<0.05 被认为具有统计学显着性。结果:平均总知识得分为 25.07±5.92,满分为 33 分。参与者表现出很强的基础意识,正确地将 HPV 识别为性传播感染,认识到 HPV 影响两性,并认识到其在导致 宫颈癌 中的作用。然而,对垂直 传播 风险的了解以及缺乏针对 HPV 的特定 抗病毒药物 治疗 的知识很少。教育程度与知识水平显着相关(P=0.01)。事实上,教育程度是知识得分的最强预测因素。结论:在这种临床环境中,女性对 HPV 的基本认知度较高。然而,对于 治疗 选项、垂直 传播 和 疫苗接种 功效仍然存在严重误解。这些发现凸显了针对这些知识差距的教育干预措施的迫切需要,特别是在教育程度较低的女性中。他们还强调需要进行结构化的 HPV 教育和针对文化的宣传活动。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是全球主要的公共卫生问题。本研究旨在评估就诊于三级妇科诊所的女性对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 和 宫颈癌 的态度和知识。
设计与对象
这项描述性横断面调查针对 2023 年至 2024 年转诊至德黑兰医科大学附属亚斯综合医院妇科诊所的 139 名女性进行。
核心方法
数据是通过面对面访谈收集的。使用 SPSS(IBM SPSS Statistics for Windows,版本 25)进行数据分析。
主要结果
知识总分平均分为 25.07±5.92 分(满分为 33 分)。教育程度与知识水平显着相关(P=0.01)。
解释与创新
参与者表现出很强的基础意识,正确地将 HPV 识别为性传播感染,认识到 HPV 影响两性,并认识到其在导致 宫颈癌 中的作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现凸显了针对这些知识差距的教育干预措施的迫切需要,特别是在教育程度较低的女性中。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。由于横截面设计和自我报告偏差的可能性,证据强度中等。

Attitudes and Knowledge About Human Papillomavirus and Cervical Cancer among Women Referred to a Tertiary Gynecologic Oncology Clinic, Tehran, Iran

Authors: Elham Shirali, Fariba Yarandi, Elham Feizabad, Mariyah Zehra, Saba Sabet, Sara Ramhormozian, Arash Jafarieh, Pershang Nazemi
Original abstract (English)
Background & Objective: Cervical cancer is a major public health concern worldwide. This study was conducted with aim to assess the attitudes and knowledge about Human Papillomavirus (HPV) and cervical cancer among women attending a tertiary gynecologic clinic.Materials & Methods: This descriptive, cross-sectional survey was conducted among 139 women referred to the gynecologic clinics of Yas Hospital Complex, affiliated with Tehran University of Medical Sciences, from 2023 to 2024. Data were collected through face-to-face interviews. Data analysis was performed using SPSS (IBM SPSS Statistics for Windows, Version 25). P<0.05 was considered statistically significant.Results: The mean total knowledge score was 25.07±5.92 out of a possible 33 points. Participants demonstrated strong foundational awareness, correctly identifying HPV as a sexually transmitted infection, recognizing that HPV affects both genders, and recognizing its role in causing cervical cancer. However, knowledge of the risks of vertical transmission and the lack of a specific antiviral treatment for HPV was low. Educational attainment was significantly associated with knowledge levels (P=0.01). Indeed, educational attainment was the strongest predictor of knowledge scores.Conclusion: In this clinical setting, basic HPV awareness was high among women. However, substantial misconceptions persisted regarding treatment options, vertical transmission, and vaccination efficacy. These findings highlight an urgent need for educational interventions targeting these knowledge gaps, particularly among women with lower educational attainment. They also underscore the need for structured HPV education and culturally tailored awareness campaigns.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is a major public health concern worldwide. This study aimed to assess the attitudes and knowledge about Human Papillomavirus (HPV) and cervical cancer among women attending a tertiary gynecologic clinic.
Design and population
This descriptive, cross-sectional survey was conducted among 139 women referred to the gynecologic clinics of Yas Hospital Complex, affiliated with Tehran University of Medical Sciences, from 2023 to 2024.
Core methods
Data were collected through face-to-face interviews. Data analysis was performed using SPSS (IBM SPSS Statistics for Windows, Version 25).
Main results
The mean total knowledge score was 25.07±5.92 out of a possible 33 points. Educational attainment was significantly associated with knowledge levels (P=0.01).
Interpretation and novelty
Participants demonstrated strong foundational awareness, correctly identifying HPV as a sexually transmitted infection, recognizing that HPV affects both genders, and recognizing its role in causing cervical cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight an urgent need for educational interventions targeting these knowledge gaps, particularly among women with lower educational attainment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study. The evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and potential for self-reporting bias.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

非 16/18 高危型HPV 感染女性阴道生态失调与宫颈病变的关联:一项针对中国东部地区 2,264 名女性的横断面研究

Association of vaginal dysbiosis with cervical lesions among women with non-16/18 high-risk HPV infections: a cross-sectional study of 2,264 women in Eastern China
作者： Shu-xing Yuan, Dan Wu, Kexin Hu, Yongxinag Li
摘要中文翻译背景:旨在调查阴道生态失调是否与感染非 16/18 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 基因型的宫颈病变风险增加相关,并为风险分层 宫颈癌 筛查策略提供信息。方法:这项横断面研究纳入了 2023 年 6 月至 2024 年 10 月期间在嘉兴市妇幼保健院就诊的 2,264 名女性。进行了 HPV 基因分型、阴道微生态评估和 Thinprep 细胞学检测(TCT)。HR-HR-HPV 感染被分类为 HPV16/18 阳性或非 16/18 HR-HPV 感染。使用逻辑回归模型来评估关联性,并根据年龄进行调整。使用乘积项的 Wald 检验来评估乘法交互作用。应用 Benjamini-Hochberg FDR 校正进行多重比较。结果:HPV 患病率 总体检出率为 49.87%,HR-HPV 检出率为 36.13%。在正常 (OR = 11.45) 和失调 (OR = 4.40) 阴道微生物群中,HR-HPV 感染与宫颈病变显着相关(均 P < 0.001)。分层分析显示,生态失调组中 HPV58 和 HPV59 的点估计值(OR 分别为 10.98 和 9.51)高于正常组(OR 分别为 7.50 和 6.44)。然而,正式的乘性交互作用检验并未达到统计显着性(Wald P 分别为 0.526 和 0.559;两者的 FDR 调整 q = 0.559)。FDR 校正后,没有 HPV 亚型与 ASC-US 保持显着相关。结论:在这项横断面研究中,阴道菌群失调与非 16/18 HR-HPV 感染(尤其是 HPV58 和 HPV59)女性的宫颈病变特别相关,在存在菌群失调的情况下,点估计值升高。然而,正式的乘性交互作用检验并未达到统计显着性。这些发现是探索性的和假设性的;由于横截面设计,无法推断因果关系。在考虑任何临床应用之前,需要进行前瞻性研究来验证观察到的关联。我们的结果应谨慎解释,并且不支持对当前筛查实践的改变。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
旨在调查阴道生态失调是否与感染非 16/18 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 基因型的宫颈病变风险增加相关,并为风险分层 宫颈癌 筛查策略提供信息。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究纳入了 2023 年 6 月至 2024 年 10 月期间在嘉兴市妇幼保健院就诊的 2,264 名女性。
核心方法
进行了 HPV 基因分型、阴道微生态评估和 Thinprep 细胞学检测 (TCT)。使用逻辑回归模型来评估关联性,并根据年龄进行调整。使用乘积项的 Wald 检验评估乘法交互作用,并应用 Benjamini-Hochberg FDR 校正进行多重比较。
主要结果
HPV 患病率 总体检出率为 49.87%,其中 HR-HPV 检出率为 36.13%。在正常 (OR = 11.45) 和失调 (OR = 4.40) 阴道微生物群中,HR-HPV 感染与宫颈病变显着相关(均 P < 0.001)。分层分析显示,生态失调组中 HPV58 和 HPV59 的点估计值(分别为 OR = 10.98 和 9.51)高于正常组(分别为 OR = 7.50 和 6.44),但正式的乘性相互作用检验并未达到统计显着性(分别为 Wald P = 0.526 和 0.559;两者的 FDR 调整 q = 0.559)。FDR 校正后,没有 HPV 亚型与 ASC-US 保持显着相关。
解释与创新
在这项横断面研究中,阴道菌群失调与非 16/18 HR-HPV 感染女性的宫颈病变特别相关,特别是 HPV58 和 HPV59,在存在菌群失调的情况下,点估计值升高。然而,正式的乘性交互作用检验并未达到统计学显着性,并且研究结果是探索性的和假设生成的;由于横截面设计,无法推断因果关系。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能会为风险分层的 宫颈癌 筛查策略提供信息,特别是对于非 16/18 HR-HPV 感染和阴道生态失调的女性,但在考虑任何临床应用之前,需要进行前瞻性研究来验证观察到的关联。结果应谨慎解释,并且不支持对当前筛查实践的改变。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计限制了因果推断,研究结果的探索性需要在前瞻性研究中进行验证。由于样本量大和严格的统计调整,证据强度中等,但缺乏显着的相互作用测试和横截面设计降低了建立因果关系的信心。

Association of vaginal dysbiosis with cervical lesions among women with non-16/18 high-risk HPV infections: a cross-sectional study of 2,264 women in Eastern China

Authors: Shu-xing Yuan, Dan Wu, Kexin Hu, Yongxinag Li
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: To investigate whether vaginal dysbiosis is associated with an increased risk of cervical lesions infected with non-16/18 high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) genotypes and to inform risk-stratified cervical cancer screening strategies. METHODS: This cross‐sectional study included 2,264 women attending Jiaxing Maternal and Child Health Hospital between June 2023 and October 2024. HPV genotyping, vaginal microecological assessment, and Thinprep cytologic test (TCT) were performed. HR- HR-HPV infections were classified as HPV16/18-positive or non-16/18 HR-HPV infections. Logistic regression models were used to evaluate associations, adjusting for age. Multiplicative interaction was assessed using Wald tests for product terms. Benjamini‐Hochberg FDR correction was applied for multiple comparisons. RESULTS: The overall HPV prevalence was 49.87%, with HR-HPV detected in 36.13%. HR‐HPV infection was significantly associated with cervical lesions in both normal (OR = 11.45) and dysbiotic (OR = 4.40) vaginal microbiota (both P < 0.001). Stratified analyses showed higher point estimates for HPV58 and HPV59 in the dysbiosis group (OR = 10.98 and 9.51, respectively) than in the normal group (OR = 7.50 and 6.44, respectively). However, formal multiplicative interaction tests did not reach statistical significance (Wald P = 0.526 and 0.559, respectively; FDR‐adjusted q = 0.559 for both). No HPV subtype remained significantly associated with ASC‐US after FDR correction. CONCLUSIONS: In this cross‐sectional study, vaginal dysbiosis is specifically associated with cervical lesions among women with non‐16/18 HR‐HPV infections, particularly HPV58 and HPV59, which showed elevated point estimates in the presence of dysbiosis. However, formal multiplicative interaction tests did not reach statistical significance. These findings are exploratory and hypothesis‐generating; causality cannot be inferred due to the cross‐sectional design. Prospective studies are needed to validate the observed associations before any clinical application can be considered. Our results should be interpreted with caution and do not support changes to current screening practice.

Question and background
To investigate whether vaginal dysbiosis is associated with an increased risk of cervical lesions infected with non-16/18 high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) genotypes and to inform risk-stratified cervical cancer screening strategies.
Design and population
This cross‐sectional study included 2,264 women attending Jiaxing Maternal and Child Health Hospital between June 2023 and October 2024.
Core methods

Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform risk-stratified cervical cancer screening strategies, particularly for women with non-16/18 HR-HPV infections and vaginal dysbiosis, but prospective studies are needed to validate the observed associations before any clinical application can be considered. The results should be interpreted with caution and do not support changes to current screening practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross‐sectional design limits causal inference, and the exploratory nature of the findings requires validation in prospective studies. The evidence strength is moderate due to the large sample size and rigorous statistical adjustments, but the lack of significant interaction tests and the cross-sectional design reduce confidence in establishing causality.

消息框架视频干预对母亲 疫苗 对女儿 HPV 疫苗接种 信心的影响:宫颈上皮内瘤变母亲的准实验研究

Effects of a Message‐Framing Video Intervention on Maternal Vaccine Confidence Regarding HPV Vaccination for Daughters: A Quasi‐Experimental Study Among Mothers With Cervical Intraepithelial Neoplasia
作者： Jie Gong, Suyun Dong, Jinghui Sun, Meihua Cong, Juan Xie
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 增加 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率至关重要,需要有效的 疫苗接种 策略。这项研究评估了消息框架 (MF) 视频干预对增强诊断为宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 的母亲的 疫苗 信心的影响,针对其女儿的 HPV 疫苗接种。方法本次干预研究于2024年1月至6月在某大学附属医院妇科宫颈门诊进行,以邻近社区的CIN患者为对象。有 9 至 14 岁女儿、未接种疫苗且母亲被诊断患有 CIN 的母亲参加了这项活动。他们被分配接受标准健康教育或有针对性的信息框架视频干预。结果数据由经过培训的研究人员在 10 周干预期后收集,重点关注母亲对其女儿 HPV 疫苗接种 的了解、态度和行为。结果 总共分析了 101 名患者。与接受标准健康教育的人相比,接受信息框架视频干预的人对 HPV 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和行为显着改善(Z = -3.376,P = 0.001),并且 疫苗 犹豫得分较低(Z = -2.924,P = 0.003)。然而,各组之间安排 疫苗 预约的比率差异不具有统计学意义(χ 2 = 0.934,P = 0.334)。结论 消息框架视频干预通过提高 CIN 患者为其女儿接种 HPV 疫苗的知识和意愿,显着增强了 疫苗 的信心。需要进一步研究来探索干预措施对实际 疫苗接种 率的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
增加 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率至关重要,需要有效的 疫苗接种 策略。这项研究评估了信息框架视频干预对增强被诊断患有宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 的母亲的 疫苗 信心的影响,针对其女儿的 HPV 疫苗接种。
设计与对象
这项介入研究于2024年1月至6月进行,在一所大学附属医院的妇科宫颈诊所进行,针对来自邻近社区的CIN患者。有 9 至 14 岁女儿、未接种疫苗且母亲被诊断患有 CIN 的母亲参加了这项活动。
核心方法
他们被分配接受标准健康教育或有针对性的信息框架视频干预。结果数据由经过培训的研究人员在 10 周干预期后收集,重点关注母亲对其女儿 HPV 疫苗接种 的了解、态度和行为。
主要结果
总共分析了 101 名患者。与接受标准健康教育的人相比,接受信息框架视频干预的人对 HPV 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和行为显着改善(Z = -3.376,P = 0.001),并且 疫苗 犹豫得分较低(Z = -2.924,P = 0.003)。然而,各组之间安排 疫苗 预约的比率差异不具有统计学意义(χ2 = 0.934,P = 0.334)。
解释与创新
消息框架视频干预通过提高 CIN 患者为其女儿接种 HPV 疫苗的知识和意愿,显着增强了 疫苗 的信心。需要进一步研究来探索干预措施对实际 疫苗接种 率的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,消息框架干预措施可以有效提高高危人群对 疫苗 的信心,可能有助于增加 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率。这种方法对于患有 CIN 的母亲可能特别有价值,因为她们可能提高了对 HPV 相关风险的认识。
局限与证据强度
该研究是准实验性的,并没有证明各组之间实际的 疫苗接种 预约安排存在显着差异。由于研究设计和缺乏实际 疫苗接种 率的长期随访数据,证据强度中等。

Effects of a Message‐Framing Video Intervention on Maternal Vaccine Confidence Regarding HPV Vaccination for Daughters: A Quasi‐Experimental Study Among Mothers With Cervical Intraepithelial Neoplasia

Authors: Jie Gong, Suyun Dong, Jinghui Sun, Meihua Cong, Juan Xie
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Increasing HPV vaccination coverage is pivotal, necessitating effective vaccination strategies. This research assessed the impact of a message‐framing (MF) video intervention on boosting vaccine confidence among mothers diagnosed with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN), targeting HPV vaccination for their daughters. Methods Conducted from January to June 2024, this interventional study took place at the gynecological cervical clinic of a university‐affiliated hospital and targeted CIN patients from adjacent communities. Mothers with daughters aged 9–14 years, who were unvaccinated and whose mothers were diagnosed with CIN, participated. They were allocated to receive either standard health education or a targeted message‐framing video intervention. Outcome data were collected by trained researchers after a 10‐week intervention period, focusing on maternal knowledge, attitudes, and behaviors toward their daughters’ HPV vaccination. Results A total of 101 patients were analyzed. Those exposed to the message‐framing video intervention exhibited significantly improved knowledge, attitudes, and behaviors towards HPV vaccination ( Z = −3.376, P = 0.001), and demonstrated lower vaccine hesitancy scores ( Z = −2.924, P = 0.003) compared to their counterparts receiving standard health education. However, differences in the rates of scheduling vaccine appointments were not statistically significant between the groups ( χ 2 = 0.934, P = 0.334). Conclusions The message‐framing video intervention significantly enhanced vaccine confidence by improving the knowledge and willingness of CIN patients to vaccinate their daughters against HPV. Further studies are required to explore the intervention's influence on actual vaccination rates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Increasing HPV vaccination coverage is pivotal, necessitating effective vaccination strategies. This research assessed the impact of a message‐framing video intervention on boosting vaccine confidence among mothers diagnosed with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN), targeting HPV vaccination for their daughters.
Design and population
Conducted from January to June 2024, this interventional study took place at the gynecological cervical clinic of a university‐affiliated hospital and targeted CIN patients from adjacent communities. Mothers with daughters aged 9–14 years, who were unvaccinated and whose mothers were diagnosed with CIN, participated.
Core methods
They were allocated to receive either standard health education or a targeted message‐framing video intervention. Outcome data were collected by trained researchers after a 10‐week intervention period, focusing on maternal knowledge, attitudes, and behaviors toward their daughters’ HPV vaccination.
Main results
A total of 101 patients were analyzed. Those exposed to the message‐framing video intervention exhibited significantly improved knowledge, attitudes, and behaviors towards HPV vaccination (Z = −3.376, P = 0.001), and demonstrated lower vaccine hesitancy scores (Z = −2.924, P = 0.003) compared to their counterparts receiving standard health education. However, differences in the rates of scheduling vaccine appointments were not statistically significant between the groups (χ2 = 0.934, P = 0.334).
Interpretation and novelty
The message‐framing video intervention significantly enhanced vaccine confidence by improving the knowledge and willingness of CIN patients to vaccinate their daughters against HPV. Further studies are required to explore the intervention's influence on actual vaccination rates.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that message‐framing interventions can be effective in improving vaccine confidence among high-risk populations, potentially contributing to increased HPV vaccination coverage. This approach may be particularly valuable for mothers with CIN, who may have heightened awareness of HPV-related risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was quasi-experimental and did not demonstrate significant differences in actual vaccination appointment scheduling between groups. The evidence strength is moderate due to the study design and the lack of long-term follow-up data on actual vaccination rates.

非价 HPV 疫苗接种 在先前感染的女性中诱导强烈的全身和粘膜 HPV16 中和抗体反应

Nonavalent HPV vaccination induces robust systemic and mucosal HPV16-neutralizing antibody responses in previously infected women
作者： Victoria López-Codony, Álvaro de Andrés-Pablo, Marta López-Querol, Sara Tous, Gonzalo Peón, Maria Eulàlia Fernández-Montolí, Carlos Ortega-Expósito, Yolanda Pérez, Anna Ferrer-Artola, Josep Maria Sole-Sedeno
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的中和抗体反应通常在血清中进行评估,而感染部位的粘膜免疫仍然不太清楚,特别是在 HPV 阳性个体中。我们评估了近期 HPV16/18 宫颈检测呈阳性的女性在感染非价 HPV 疫苗接种 后的全身和粘膜 HPV16 中和抗体反应。方法 在这项纵向 RIFT-HPV 研究中,39 名之前未接种疫苗且在过去 12 个月内进行过 HPV16/18 宫颈检测呈阳性的成年女性接受了非价 HPV 疫苗 (Gardasil-9) 的三剂方案。在 疫苗接种 之前和期间收集配对血清、宫颈和口腔样本。使用增强型绿色荧光蛋白假病毒粒子中和测定 (EGFP-PBNA) 测量针对 HPV16 假病毒粒子的中和活性。结果 疫苗接种 显着增加血清、宫颈和口腔样本中 HPV16 中和抗体反应,无论基线 HPV16 阳性如何。血清 EC 50 检测从基线时的 33% 增加到三剂后的 100%,而宫颈和口腔检测分别从一剂后的 8% 和 10% 增加到三剂后的 100% 和 80%。当检测条件超过生理病毒暴露水平时,宫颈HPV16假病毒粒子中和度从疫苗接种前的36%逐渐增加到疫苗一剂、两剂和三剂后的61%、68%和78%,分别。全身和粘膜中和反应呈正相关。EGFP-PBNA 表现出高重现性 (ICC = 0.98),并且与参考方法具有良好的一致性。结论 无论当前 HPV16 阳性如何,非价 HPV 疫苗接种 都会诱导强烈的全身和粘膜 HPV16 中和抗体反应。这些发现表明,在先前感染的女性中,非价 传播 后粘膜 HPV16 中和免疫力增强,并支持进一步研究其在降低病毒感染性和 传播 方面的潜在作用。试验注册:ClinicalTrials.gov,NCT05334706。注册日期:2022 年 4 月 19 日。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估最近进行 HPV16/18 阳性宫颈检测的女性在非价 HPV 疫苗接种 后的全身和粘膜 HPV16 中和抗体反应,因为对 HPV 疫苗 的中和抗体反应通常在血清中评估,而粘膜免疫的特征仍然较少,特别是在 HPV 阳性个体中。
设计与对象
这是一项纵向 RIFT-HPV 研究,对象是 39 名之前未接种疫苗、在过去 12 个月内宫颈检测呈 HPV16/18 阳性的成年女性,她们接受了三剂非价 HPV 疫苗 (Gardasil-9)。
核心方法
在 疫苗接种 之前和期间收集配对血清、宫颈和口腔样本。使用增强型绿色荧光蛋白假病毒体中和测定 (EGFP-PBNA) 测量针对 HPV16 假病毒体的中和活性,测定条件超过生理病毒暴露水平。
主要结果
无论基线 HPV16 阳性程度如何,疫苗接种 均显着增加血清、宫颈和口腔样本中的 HPV16 中和抗体反应。血清 EC 50 检测从基线时的 33% 增加到三剂后的 100%,而宫颈和口腔检测分别从一剂后的 8% 和 10% 增加到三剂后的 100% 和 80%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,无论当前 HPV16 阳性如何,非价 HPV 疫苗接种 都会诱导强烈的全身和粘膜 HPV16 中和抗体反应,这是一个新颖的发现,因为它证明了 疫苗 对先前感染的女性的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持粘膜 HPV16 中和免疫在降低病毒感染性和 传播 方面的潜在作用,这可以为未来 HPV 监测、诊断 和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个中心和相对较小的样本量,但证据充足,因为它基于配对样本的纵向设计和可重复的测定方法(ICC = 0.98)。

Nonavalent HPV vaccination induces robust systemic and mucosal HPV16-neutralizing antibody responses in previously infected women

Authors: Victoria López-Codony, Álvaro de Andrés-Pablo, Marta López-Querol, Sara Tous, Gonzalo Peón, Maria Eulàlia Fernández-Montolí, Carlos Ortega-Expósito, Yolanda Pérez, Anna Ferrer-Artola, Josep Maria Sole-Sedeno
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Neutralizing antibody responses to human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines are typically assessed in serum, while mucosal immunity at sites of infection remains less well characterized, particularly in HPV-positive individuals. We evaluated systemic and mucosal HPV16-neutralizing antibody responses following nonavalent HPV vaccination in women with a recent HPV16/18-positive cervical test. Methods In this longitudinal RIFT-HPV study, 39 previously unvaccinated adult women with an HPV16/18-positive cervical test done in the previous 12 months received a three-dose schedule of the nonavalent HPV vaccine (Gardasil-9). Paired serum, cervical, and oral samples were collected before and during vaccination. Neutralizing activity against HPV16 pseudovirions was measured using an enhanced green fluorescent protein pseudovirion-based neutralization assay (EGFP-PBNA). Results Vaccination significantly increased HPV16-neutralizing antibody responses in serum, cervical, and oral samples, irrespective of baseline HPV16 positivity. Serum EC 50 detection increased from 33% at baseline to 100% after three doses, while cervical and oral detection increased from 8% and 10% after one dose, to 100% and 80% after three doses, respectively. With assay conditions exceeding physiological viral exposure levels, cervical HPV16 pseudovirion neutralization progressively increased from 36% before vaccination to 61%, 68% and 78% after one, two and three vaccine doses, respectively. Systemic and mucosal neutralizing responses were positively correlated. The EGFP-PBNA demonstrated high reproducibility (ICC = 0.98) and good concordance with the reference method. Conclusions Nonavalent HPV vaccination induced strong systemic and mucosal HPV16-neutralizing antibody responses, regardless of current HPV16 positivity. These findings demonstrate enhanced mucosal HPV16-neutralizing immunity following nonavalent vaccination in previously infected women and support further investigation of its potential role in reducing viral infectivity and transmission. Trial registration: ClinicalTrials.gov, NCT05334706. Registered on 19 April 2022.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the systemic and mucosal HPV16-neutralizing antibody responses following nonavalent HPV vaccination in women with recent HPV16/18-positive cervical tests, as neutralizing antibody responses to HPV vaccines are typically assessed in serum, while mucosal immunity remains less characterized, especially in HPV-positive individuals.
Design and population
This was a longitudinal RIFT-HPV study conducted in 39 previously unvaccinated adult women with an HPV16/18-positive cervical test within the previous 12 months, who received a three-dose schedule of the nonavalent HPV vaccine (Gardasil-9).
Core methods
Paired serum, cervical, and oral samples were collected before and during vaccination. Neutralizing activity against HPV16 pseudovirions was measured using an enhanced green fluorescent protein pseudovirion-based neutralization assay (EGFP-PBNA), with assay conditions exceeding physiological viral exposure levels.
Main results
Vaccination significantly increased HPV16-neutralizing antibody responses in serum, cervical, and oral samples, irrespective of baseline HPV16 positivity. Serum EC 50 detection increased from 33% at baseline to 100% after three doses, while cervical and oral detection increased from 8% and 10% after one dose to 100% and 80% after three doses, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that nonavalent HPV vaccination induced strong systemic and mucosal HPV16-neutralizing antibody responses, regardless of current HPV16 positivity, which is a novel finding as it demonstrates the vaccine's effectiveness in previously infected women.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the potential role of mucosal HPV16-neutralizing immunity in reducing viral infectivity and transmission, which could inform future strategies for HPV surveillance, diagnosis, and prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single center and a relatively small sample size, but the evidence is strong as it is based on a longitudinal design with paired samples and a reproducible assay method (ICC = 0.98).

韩国 发病率 的趋势和 发病率 相关疾病的医疗费用:来自国民健康保险索赔的证据

Trends in incidence and medical costs of human papillomavirus–related diseases in Korea: evidence from National Health Insurance claims
作者： Ji-Eun An, Chelim Cheong, Junwoo Jo, Su-Yeon Yu
摘要中文翻译目的:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是宫颈癌、肛门癌和口咽癌的主要原因。尽管韩国自 2016 年以来实施了国家 HPV 发病率 计划,但全国范围内有关 HPV 相关疾病的 发病率 趋势和医疗费用的证据仍然有限。方法:这项基于人群的回顾性研究分析了韩国国民健康保险服务索赔数据。以2022年韩国人口为标准,计算2015年至2022年宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN1和CIN2/3)、尖锐湿疣(GW)以及宫颈癌、口咽癌和肛门癌的年龄标准化发病率(ASI)率。使用带有分段对数线性(泊松)模型的连接点回归来分析 ASI 的趋势。对于具有和不具有统计显着性连接点的疾病,分别使用预先识别和后识别的特定区段年度百分比变化(APC)和平均年度百分比变化(AAPC)来报告趋势。使用线性回归评估 2015 年至 2021 年总医疗费用的趋势。结果:ASI 趋势因性别和疾病类型而异。2015 年至 2022 年期间,女性中的 宫颈癌 发病率 显着下降(AAPC;-4.2%,p<0.001)。Joinpoint 回归确定了 2019 年女性和男性 GW 的显着拐点。女性 GW 发病率 在 2015-2019 年期间有所增加(APC;3.7%,p=0.010),然后在 2019-2022 年期间显着下降(APC;-14.0%,p<0.001)。男性 GW 发病率 在 2015-2019 年期间显着增加(APC;11.3%,p<0.001),然后在 2019-2022 年期间没有显示出统计上显着的变化(APC;1.4%,p=0.290)。结论:这些描述性研究结果提供了韩国 HPV 相关疾病负担的人群水平证据,但无法确定 疫苗接种、筛查或其他预防性干预措施的因果归因,因此需要继续进行 监测 与个人水平的数据链接。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是宫颈癌、肛门癌和口咽癌的主要原因。尽管韩国自 2016 年以来实施了国家 HPV 发病率 计划,但全国范围内有关 HPV 相关疾病的 发病率 趋势和医疗费用的证据仍然有限。
设计与对象
这项基于人群的回顾性研究分析了韩国国民健康保险局的索赔数据。
核心方法
以2022年韩国人口为标准,计算2015年至2022年宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN1和CIN2/3)、尖锐湿疣(GW)以及宫颈癌、口咽癌和肛门癌的年龄标准化发病率(ASI)率。使用带有分段对数线性(泊松)模型的连接点回归来分析 ASI 的趋势。使用线性回归评估 2015 年至 2021 年总医疗费用的趋势。
主要结果
ASI 趋势因性别和疾病类型而异。2015 年至 2022 年期间,女性中的 宫颈癌 发病率 显着下降(AAPC;-4.2%,p<0.001)。女性 GW 发病率 在 2015-2019 年期间有所增加(APC;3.7%,p=0.010),然后在 2019-2022 年期间显着下降(APC;-14.0%,p<0.001)。男性 GW 发病率 在 2015-2019 年期间显着增加(APC;11.3%,p<0.001),然后在 2019-2022 年期间没有显示出统计上显着的变化(APC;1.4%,p=0.290)。
解释与创新
这些描述性研究结果提供了韩国 HPV 相关疾病负担的人群水平证据,但无法确定 疫苗接种、筛查或其他预防性干预措施的因果归因,因此需要继续进行 监测 与个人水平的数据链接。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过记录 HPV 相关疾病和医疗费用的趋势,为 监测 和预防策略提供信息,强调需要持续监测以评估干预措施的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其描述性性质和无法建立因果归因而受到限制,因为它依赖于没有个人层面联系的索赔数据。由于基于人口的数据集很大,但观察设计存在固有的局限性,证据强度中等。

Trends in incidence and medical costs of human papillomavirus–related diseases in Korea: evidence from National Health Insurance claims

Authors: Ji-Eun An, Chelim Cheong, Junwoo Jo, Su-Yeon Yu
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a major cause of cervical, anal and oropharyngeal cancers. Although Korea has implemented a national HPV vaccination programme since 2016, nationwide evidence on incidence trends and medical costs of HPV-related diseases remains limited. METHODS: This retrospective population-based study analysed Korean National Health Insurance Service claims data. Age-standardised incidence (ASI) rates of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN1 and CIN2/3), genital warts (GWs), and cervical, oropharyngeal and anal cancers from 2015 to 2022 were calculated using the 2022 Korean population as the standard. Trends in ASI were analysed using Joinpoint regression with a piecewise log-linear (Poisson) model. For diseases with and without statistically significant Joinpoints, trends were reported using pre-identified and post-identified segment-specific annual percentage changes (APCs) and the average annual percentage change (AAPC), respectively. Trends in total medical costs from 2015 to 2021 were evaluated using linear regression. RESULTS: ASI trends differed by sex and disease type. Cervical cancer incidence among females declined significantly during 2015-2022 (AAPC; -4.2%, p<0.001). Joinpoint regression identified significant inflection points in 2019 for both female and male GWs. Female GW incidence increased during 2015-2019 (APC; 3.7%, p=0.010) and then decreased significantly during 2019-2022 (APC; -14.0%, p<0.001). Male GW incidence increased significantly during 2015-2019 (APC; 11.3%, p<0.001) and then showed no statistically significant change during 2019-2022 (APC; 1.4%, p=0.290). CONCLUSIONS: These descriptive findings provide population-level evidence on HPV-associated disease burden in Korea but cannot establish causal attribution to vaccination, screening or other preventive interventions, necessitating continued surveillance with individual-level data linkage.

Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a major cause of cervical, anal and oropharyngeal cancers. Although Korea has implemented a national HPV vaccination programme since 2016, nationwide evidence on incidence trends and medical costs of HPV-related diseases remains limited.

中国北京 42,379 名女性 患病率、高危型HPV 感染的基因型分布和病毒载量特征:一项回顾性研究

Prevalence, genotype distribution, and viral load characteristics of high-risk HPV infection among 42,379 women in Beijing, China: a retrospective study
作者： Ziyi Jia, Jia Zhou, Meng Qiu, Nan Guo, Xiuli Ma
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景持续高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是导致宫颈癌的必要原因,其患病率和基因型分布存在较大的地区差异。本研究旨在了解北京地区女性HR-HPV感染特征,为当地预防策略提供特定区域的流行病学证据。方法回顾性分析2016年3月至2024年12月在北京中医药大学东直门医院接受宫颈HPV基因分型的42,379例女性,采用荧光PCR技术检测出15种HR-HPV基因型。对七个年龄组的特定年龄感染模式进行了评估,并将病毒载量分为三个级别。根据 COVID-19 暴发 的时间进展,研究期分为三个阶段:大流行前、大流行和大流行后。结果 HR-HPV 患病率 总体率为 19.91% (8,438/42,379)。HPV52(20.17%)、HPV58(12.61%)和HPV16(11.84%)是最常见的基因型。以单型感染为主(75.09%),多型感染占24.91%,其中以HPV52+HPV58、HPV52+HPV16、HPV52+HPV51最为常见。年龄分布呈现双峰模式,峰值出现在20-29岁(38.53%)和60-69岁(29.55%)。中等病毒载量最常见(40.08%);HPV31和HPV16的高病毒载量比例最高,而HPV52则表现出“高患病率-低病毒载量”模式。高病毒载量比例随着感染复杂性的增加而增加。全年 患病率 范围为 17.9% 至 22.0%,2020 年最低。在大流行阶段,总体 患病率 分别为 20.53%、19.40% 和 19.88%,而多种感染率从 27.39% 连续下降至 21.65%。结论 北京女性HR-HPV感染以HPV52、HPV58、HPV16为主,年龄分布呈双峰分布,病毒载量具有基因型特异性。与大流行相关的总体 患病率 下降和多种类型感染的持续下降强调了大流行后时代有针对性的 疫苗接种 和筛查策略的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
持续高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是导致宫颈癌的必要原因,而其患病率和基因型分布则表现出显着的地区差异。本研究旨在了解北京地区女性HR-HPV感染特征,为当地预防策略提供特定区域的流行病学证据。
设计与对象
这项回顾性分析包括 2016 年 3 月至 2024 年 12 月在北京中医药大学东直门医院接受宫颈 HPV 基因分型的 42,379 名女性。研究期间根据 COVID-19 暴发 的时间进展分为三个阶段:大流行前、大流行和大流行后。
核心方法
使用荧光 PCR 检测到 15 种 HR-HPV 基因型。对七个年龄组的特定年龄感染模式进行了评估,并将病毒载量分为三个级别。
主要结果
总体 HR-HPV 患病率 为 19.91% (8,438/42,379)。HPV52(20.17%)、HPV58(12.61%)和HPV16(11.84%)是最常见的基因型。以单型感染为主(75.09%),多型感染占24.91%,其中以HPV52+HPV58、HPV52+HPV16、HPV52+HPV51最为常见。
解释与创新
与大流行相关的总体 患病率 下降和多种类型感染的持续下降强调了大流行后时代有针对性的 疫苗接种 和筛查策略的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为北京 HR-HPV 感染的 流行病学 提供了重要的见解,可以为制定特定区域的预防和控制策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于一家医院的数据,这可能会限制普遍性。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Prevalence, genotype distribution, and viral load characteristics of high-risk HPV infection among 42,379 women in Beijing, China: a retrospective study

Authors: Ziyi Jia, Jia Zhou, Meng Qiu, Nan Guo, Xiuli Ma
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is a necessary cause of cervical cancer, while its prevalence and genotype distribution show substantial regional variation. This study aimed to characterize HR-HPV infection among women in Beijing and provide region-specific epidemiological evidence for local prevention strategies. Methods This retrospective analysis included 42,379 women who underwent cervical HPV genotyping at Dongzhimen Hospital, Beijing University of Chinese Medicine, from March 2016 to December 2024. Fifteen HR-HPV genotypes were detected using fluorescent PCR. Age-specific infection patterns were assessed across seven age groups, and viral load was categorized into three levels. The study period was divided into three phases based on the temporal progression of the COVID-19 outbreak: pre-pandemic, pandemic, and post-pandemic. Results The overall HR-HPV prevalence was 19.91% (8,438/42,379). HPV52 (20.17%), HPV58 (12.61%), and HPV16 (11.84%) were the most common genotypes. Single-type infections predominated (75.09%), while multiple-type infections accounted for 24.91%, with HPV52 + HPV58, HPV52 + HPV16, and HPV52 + HPV51 being the most frequent combinations. Age distribution showed a bimodal pattern, with peaks at 20–29 years (38.53%) and 60–69 years (29.55%). Moderate viral load was most common (40.08%); HPV31 and HPV16 had the highest proportions of high viral loads, while HPV52 displayed a "high prevalence–low viral load" pattern. High viral load proportion increased with infection complexity. Annual prevalence ranged from 17.9% to 22.0%, with the lowest rate in 2020. Across pandemic phases, overall prevalence was 20.53%, 19.40%, and 19.88%, while multiple-type infection rates declined consecutively from 27.39% to 21.65%. Conclusions HR-HPV infection in Beijing women is characterized by predominance of HPV52, HPV58, and HPV16, a bimodal age distribution, and genotype-specific viral load profiles. The pandemic-associated decline in overall prevalence and sustained decrease in multiple-type infections underscore the importance of targeted vaccination and screening strategies in the post-pandemic era.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is a necessary cause of cervical cancer, while its prevalence and genotype distribution show substantial regional variation. This study aimed to characterize HR-HPV infection among women in Beijing and provide region-specific epidemiological evidence for local prevention strategies.
Design and population
This retrospective analysis included 42,379 women who underwent cervical HPV genotyping at Dongzhimen Hospital, Beijing University of Chinese Medicine, from March 2016 to December 2024. The study period was divided into three phases based on the temporal progression of the COVID-19 outbreak: pre-pandemic, pandemic, and post-pandemic.
Core methods
Fifteen HR-HPV genotypes were detected using fluorescent PCR. Age-specific infection patterns were assessed across seven age groups, and viral load was categorized into three levels.
Main results
The overall HR-HPV prevalence was 19.91% (8,438/42,379). HPV52 (20.17%), HPV58 (12.61%), and HPV16 (11.84%) were the most common genotypes. Single-type infections predominated (75.09%), while multiple-type infections accounted for 24.91%, with HPV52 + HPV58, HPV52 + HPV16, and HPV52 + HPV51 being the most frequent combinations.
Interpretation and novelty
The pandemic-associated decline in overall prevalence and sustained decrease in multiple-type infections underscore the importance of targeted vaccination and screening strategies in the post-pandemic era.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide critical insights into the epidemiology of HR-HPV infection in Beijing, which can inform the development of region-specific prevention and control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on data from a single hospital, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

'...but look how many lives would have been saved if this was available then' 探讨未来人乳头瘤病毒自我采样的可接受性,以及苏格兰智力障碍人群进行宫颈癌筛查的障碍与促进因素

'...but look how many lives would have been saved if this was available then' Exploring the acceptability of future Human Papillomavirus self-sampling, and the barriers and facilitators of cervical cancer screening for those with intellectual disabilities in Scotland.
作者： Catherine B White, Lesley M McGregor
摘要中文翻译背景:苏格兰智障女性 (ID) 的 宫颈癌 筛查出勤率约为 30%,远低于总人口 69% 的比率和世界卫生组织建议的 70% 目标。HPV 自我采样可以为这一服务不足的人群提供更方便的筛查选择。本研究旨在了解患有 ID 的女性对 宫颈癌 筛查以及 HPV 自我采样的可接受性的看法和经验。方法:利用焦点小组对参与苏格兰第三部门社区团体的智障女性及其护理人员进行了一项定性研究。2024 年 9 月至 10 月期间举办了四次焦点小组,共有 13 名参与者(11 名患有智力障碍的女性,2 名护理人员)。使用反思性主题分析对数据进行分析。结果:出现了五个主题:(1) 国家卫生服务 (NHS) 沟通是痛苦的根源;(2) 医疗保健提供者和智障妇女之间的权力失衡:感觉被忽视、被忽视和失控;(3) 护理不足造成的持久后果;(4)过去的创伤塑造了未来的回避;(5) 自我抽样作为赋权的途径。参与者表现出对自我采样的高度接受度,将其视为克服传统筛查障碍的潜在解决方案。结论:这项研究揭示了当前 宫颈癌 针对 ID 女性的筛查服务存在显着的系统性缺陷,同时强调了自我采样的变革潜力。成功的实施需要全面的可获取信息、加强的医疗保健专业培训和创伤知情护理方法。自我采样可以显着推进健康公平目标,同时改善这一服务不足人群的医疗保健结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
苏格兰智障女性 (ID) 的 宫颈癌 筛查出勤率约为 30%,远低于总人口 69% 的比率和世界卫生组织建议的 70% 目标。HPV 自我采样可以为这一服务不足的人群提供更方便的筛查选择。
设计与对象
一项使用焦点小组的定性研究是针对参加苏格兰各地第三部门社区团体的智障女性及其照顾者进行的。2024 年 9 月至 10 月期间举办了四次焦点小组,共有 13 名参与者(11 名患有智力障碍的女性,2 名护理人员)。
核心方法
使用反思性主题分析对数据进行分析。
主要结果
出现了五个主题:(1) 国家医疗服务体系 (NHS) 沟通是痛苦的根源;(2) 医疗保健提供者和智障妇女之间的权力失衡:感觉被忽视、被忽视和失控;(3) 护理不足造成的持久后果;(4)过去的创伤塑造了未来的回避;(5) 自我抽样作为赋权的途径。参与者表现出对自我采样的高度接受度,将其视为克服传统筛查障碍的潜在解决方案。
解释与创新
这项研究揭示了当前 宫颈癌 针对 ID 女性的筛查服务存在重大系统性缺陷,同时强调了自我采样的变革潜力。自我采样可以显着推进健康公平目标,同时改善这一服务不足人群的医疗保健结果。
科研与公卫意义
成功的实施需要全面的可获取信息、加强的医疗保健专业培训和创伤知情护理方法。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

'...but look how many lives would have been saved if this was available then' Exploring the acceptability of future Human Papillomavirus self-sampling, and the barriers and facilitators of cervical cancer screening for those with intellectual disabilities in Scotland.

Authors: Catherine B White, Lesley M McGregor
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cervical cancer screening attendance among women with intellectual disabilities (ID) in Scotland is approximately 30%, significantly below the general population rate of 69% and the WHO's recommended 70% target. HPV self-sampling could provide a more accessible screening option for this underserved population. This study aimed to understand the views and experiences of women with ID regarding cervical cancer screening and the acceptability of HPV self-sampling. METHODS: A qualitative study using focus groups was conducted with women with ID who attended third-sector community groups across Scotland, along with their carers. Four focus groups were held between September and October 2024, with a total of 13 participants (11 women with ID, 2 carers). Data were analysed using reflexive thematic analysis. RESULTS: Five main themes emerged: (1) National Health Service (NHS) communication as a source of distress; (2) power imbalances between health care providers and women with ID: feeling unheard, dismissed and out of control; (3) enduring consequences of inadequate care; (4) past trauma shapes future avoidance; and (5) self-sampling as a pathway to empowerment. Participants demonstrated high acceptability of self-sampling, viewing it as a potential solution to overcome barriers to traditional screening. CONCLUSION: This research reveals significant systemic inadequacies in current cervical cancer screening services for women with ID, whilst highlighting self-sampling's transformative potential. Successful implementation requires comprehensive accessible information, enhanced health care professional training and trauma-informed care approaches. Self-sampling could significantly advance health equity goals whilst improving health care outcomes for this underserved population.
View research seven-element analysis
