与美容皮肤漂白相关的皮肤鳞状细胞癌:组织学特征和 人乳头瘤病毒 的可能作用

Cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma associated with cosmetic skin bleaching: Histological characteristics and the possible role of human papillomavirus
作者： B. Seck, Z. Attiya, M.T. Ndiaye, F. Ly, M. Diallo
摘要中文翻译背景:与化妆品皮肤漂白相关的皮肤鳞状细胞癌(cSCC)的发生和临床特征已在文献中有详细记载。然而,其组织病理学特征尚未见报道。本研究旨在描述在与化妆品皮肤漂白相关的鳞状细胞癌病例中观察到的组织学异常,并探讨潜在的影响因素。方法:我们重新分析了 2006 年 11 月至 2021 年 10 月期间在达喀尔不同病理实验室诊断的所有 cSCC 病例的存档石蜡包埋组织块。结果:我们收集了 19 例 cSCC 病例,全部发生于女性,平均年龄 58 岁。平均而言,这些女性从事美容皮肤漂白已有 20 年。其中大部分(14例)使用含有对苯二酚和丙酸氯倍他索混合物的漂白产品。所有癌均分化良好,其中17例(89.5%)为侵袭性。与cSCC相关的组织学异常包括所有病例中伴有淋巴浆细胞浸润的严重纤维化、18例(94.7%)毛细血管扩张、9例(47.4%)外源性黄褐斑沉积物和1例日光性弹力组织增生。此外,在 10 例(52.6%)病例中发现了挖空细胞,这是 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染的组织学标志物。结论:与化妆品皮肤漂白相关的 cSCC 表现出独特的组织病理学模式,伴有频繁的空细胞增多 (52.6%)。这些发现支持多因素 发病机制,其中 HPV 可能充当辅助因子。未来需要使用 PCR 和原位杂交进行研究来阐明这种潜在的关联。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述与美容皮肤漂白相关的皮肤鳞状细胞癌(cSCC)的组织学异常,并探索潜在的影响因素,因为组织病理学特征尚未报道。
设计与对象
我们重新分析了 2006 年 11 月至 2021 年 10 月期间在达喀尔不同病理实验室诊断的所有 cSCC 病例中存档的石蜡包埋组织块,收集了 19 例平均年龄为 58 岁的女性病例。
核心方法
未来需要使用 PCR 和原位杂交进行研究来阐明这种潜在的关联。
主要结果
所有19例病例均发生于平均年龄58岁的女性,她们平均从事美容皮肤漂白20年;14 使用含有氢醌和丙酸氯倍他索的产品。所有癌均分化良好,89.5%具有侵袭性,并表现出组织学异常,包括严重纤维化(100%)、毛细血管扩张(94.7%)、外源性黄褐斑(47.4%)和挖空细胞(52.6%)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,与化妆品皮肤漂白相关的 cSCC 表现出一种独特的组织病理学模式,伴有频繁的空细胞增多,支持多因素 发病机制,其中 HPV 可能充当辅助因子。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明 HPV 在与皮肤漂白相关的 cSCC 发病机制 中具有潜在作用,强调需要使用 PCR 和原位杂交进行进一步研究以澄清这种关联。
局限与证据强度
由于回顾性设计和对存档组织块的依赖,证据强度中等,这可能会限制充分建立因果关系或将发现推广到该特定人群之外的能力。

Cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma associated with cosmetic skin bleaching: Histological characteristics and the possible role of human papillomavirus

Authors: B. Seck, Z. Attiya, M.T. Ndiaye, F. Ly, M. Diallo
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The occurrence and clinical characteristics of cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma (cSCC) associated with cosmetic skin bleaching have been well documented in the literature. However, the histopathological characteristics have not yet been reported. This study aimed to describe the histological abnormalities observed in cases of cSCC associated with cosmetic skin bleaching and to explore the potential contributing factors. METHODS: We re-analysed archived paraffin-embedded tissue blocks from all cases of cSCC diagnosed in different pathology laboratories in Dakar between November 2006 and October 2021. RESULTS: We collected 19 cases of cSCC, all occurring in women, with a mean age of 58 years. On average, the women had been practising cosmetic skin bleaching for 20 years. Most of them (14 cases) used bleaching products containing a mixture of hydroquinone and clobetasol propionate. All carcinomas were well-differentiated, and 17 cases (89.5%) were invasive. Histological abnormalities associated with the cSCC included intense fibrosis with a lymphoplasmacytic infiltrate in all cases, telangiectasias in 18 cases (94.7%), exogenous ochronosis deposits in 9 cases (47.4%), and solar elastosis in one case. In addition, koilocytes, histological markers of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, were identified in 10 cases (52.6%). CONCLUSION: cSCC associated with cosmetic skin bleaching exhibit a distinctive histopathological pattern with frequent koilocytosis (52.6%). These findings support a multifactorial pathogenesis in which HPV may act as a cofactor. Future studies using PCR and in situ hybridisation are needed to clarify this potential association.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe the histological abnormalities in cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma (cSCC) associated with cosmetic skin bleaching and explore potential contributing factors, as the histopathological characteristics had not yet been reported.
Design and population
We re-analysed archived paraffin-embedded tissue blocks from all cases of cSCC diagnosed in different pathology laboratories in Dakar between November 2006 and October 2021, collecting 19 cases in women with a mean age of 58 years.
Core methods
Future studies using PCR and in situ hybridisation are needed to clarify this potential association.
Main results
All 19 cases occurred in women with a mean age of 58 years, who had practised cosmetic skin bleaching for an average of 20 years; 14 used products containing hydroquinone and clobetasol propionate. All carcinomas were well-differentiated, 89.5% invasive, and exhibited histological abnormalities including intense fibrosis (100%), telangiectasias (94.7%), exogenous ochronosis (47.4%), and koilocytes (52.6%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that cSCC associated with cosmetic skin bleaching exhibits a distinctive histopathological pattern with frequent koilocytosis, supporting a multifactorial pathogenesis in which HPV may act as a cofactor.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest a potential role of HPV in the pathogenesis of cSCC related to skin bleaching, highlighting the need for further studies using PCR and in situ hybridisation to clarify this association.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the retrospective design and reliance on archived tissue blocks, which may limit the ability to fully establish causality or generalise findings beyond this specific population.

通过汇总测试进行高效高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 筛查:基于中低收入国家真实世界数据的模拟研究

Efficient high-risk human papillomavirus screening through pooled testing: a simulation study based on real-world data in low- and middle-income countries
作者： Fengming Pan, Marcel JW Greuter, Jaap AR Koot, Heather Cubie, Naheed Nazrul, Carolyn Nakisige, Jogchum Beltman, Geertruida H de Bock, Jurjen van der Schans
摘要中文翻译背景:世界卫生组织建议高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 检测作为 宫颈癌 筛查的主要方法。将样品组合在一个池中可以减少与大量商业可用测试相关的费用。这项研究使用来自两个低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC) 的真实数据模拟了合并 hrHPV 检测的潜在测试节省影响。方法:我们使用真实世界实验室数据模拟了孟加拉国 (hrHPV 患病率 2.10%) 和乌干达 (hrHPV 患病率 22.52%) 的两阶段和三阶段 hrHPV 合并。测量包括每个阶段的测试数量、测试总数以及保存的测试百分比。我们进行了敏感性分析,以评估池中每个额外样本 1.00% 的敏感性和特异性损失对假阴性率 (FNR) 的影响。结果:在孟加拉国,与单独测试相比,两阶段策略(每个池 8 个样本)和三阶段策略分别节省了 71.21% 和 77.21% 的测试。在乌干达,与单独测试相比,两阶段策略(每个池三个样本)将测试数量减少了 15.34%。假设由于合并而导致敏感性和特异性损失,在两阶段策略下,当 hrHPV 患病率 较低时,FNR 较高 (FNR = 0.012-0.134)。结论:合并 hrHPV 检测有可能减少使用的检测数量并增强资源有限环境中的筛查能力。值得注意的是,较低的 hrHPV 患病率 可以实现更有效的汇总检测。尽管可能会损失敏感性和特异性,但合并 hrHPV 检测可以为中低收入国家带来好处并值得进一步验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
世界卫生组织建议将高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 检测作为 宫颈癌 筛查的主要方法,并将样本合并到一个池中可以减少与大量商用检测相关的费用。
设计与对象
这项研究使用来自两个低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC) 的真实数据模拟了合并 hrHPV 检测的潜在测试节省影响。
核心方法
我们使用真实实验室数据模拟了孟加拉国 (hrHPV 患病率 2.10%) 和乌干达 (hrHPV 患病率 22.52%) 的两阶段和三阶段 hrHPV 合并,进行了敏感性分析,以评估池中每个额外样本敏感性和特异性损失 1.00% 对假阴性率 (FNR) 的影响,并测量了每个阶段的测试次数、测试总数、以及保存的测试的百分比。
主要结果
在孟加拉国,与单独测试相比,两阶段策略(每个池 8 个样本)和三阶段策略分别节省了 71.21% 和 77.21% 的测试次数;在乌干达,两阶段策略(每个池三个样本)与单独测试相比,减少了15.34%的测试数量;假设由于合并而导致敏感性和特异性损失,在两阶段策略下,当 hrHPV 患病率 较低时,FNR 较高 (FNR = 0.012-0.134)。
解释与创新
合并 hrHPV 检测有可能减少所使用的检测数量并增强资源有限环境中的筛查能力,值得注意的是,较低的 hrHPV 患病率 可以实现更有效的合并检测。
科研与公卫意义
合并 hrHPV 检测可以通过降低检测成本和提高筛查能力为低收入和中等收入国家带来好处,尽管可能会损失敏感性和特异性,但仍需要进一步验证。
局限与证据强度
该研究是基于真实世界数据的模拟,由于合并可能会损失敏感性和特异性,因此证据强度中等,这可能会影响假阴性率,特别是在低 患病率 设置中。

Efficient high-risk human papillomavirus screening through pooled testing: a simulation study based on real-world data in low- and middle-income countries

Authors: Fengming Pan, Marcel JW Greuter, Jaap AR Koot, Heather Cubie, Naheed Nazrul, Carolyn Nakisige, Jogchum Beltman, Geertruida H de Bock, Jurjen van der Schans
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The World Health Organization recommends high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) testing as the primary method for cervical cancer screening. Combining samples in a pool can reduce the expense associated with large numbers of commercially available tests. This study simulated the potential test-saving impact of pooled hrHPV testing using real-world data from two low- and middle-income countries (LMICs). METHODS: We simulated a two- and three-stage hrHPV pooling in Bangladesh (hrHPV prevalence 2.10%) and Uganda (hrHPV prevalence 22.52%) using real-world laboratory data. Measurements included the number of tests per stage, the total number of tests, and the percentage of tests saved. We performed sensitivity analysis to assess the impact of a 1.00% loss of sensitivity and specificity per additional sample in the pool on the false-negative rate (FNR). RESULTS: In Bangladesh, the two-stage strategy (eight samples per pool) and the three-stage strategy saved 71.21% and 77.21% of tests, respectively, compared to testing individually. In Uganda, the two-stage strategy (three samples per pool) reduced the number of tests by 15.34% compared to testing individually. Assuming loss of sensitivity and specificity due to pooling, the FNR was higher (FNR = 0.012-0.134) when hrHPV prevalence was low under the two-stage strategy. CONCLUSIONS: Pooled hrHPV testing has the potential to reduce the number of tests used and enhance screening capacity in resource-limited settings. Notably, a lower prevalence of hrHPV allows for more efficient pooled testing. Despite the potential loss of sensitivity and specificity, pooled hrHPV testing could offer benefits for LMICs and warrant further verification.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The World Health Organization recommends high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) testing as the primary method for cervical cancer screening, and combining samples in a pool can reduce the expense associated with large numbers of commercially available tests.
Design and population
This study simulated the potential test-saving impact of pooled hrHPV testing using real-world data from two low- and middle-income countries (LMICs).
Core methods
We simulated a two- and three-stage hrHPV pooling in Bangladesh (hrHPV prevalence 2.10%) and Uganda (hrHPV prevalence 22.52%) using real-world laboratory data, performed sensitivity analysis to assess the impact of a 1.00% loss of sensitivity and specificity per additional sample in the pool on the false-negative rate (FNR), and measured the number of tests per stage, the total number of tests, and the percentage of tests saved.
Main results
In Bangladesh, the two-stage strategy (eight samples per pool) and the three-stage strategy saved 71.21% and 77.21% of tests, respectively, compared to testing individually; in Uganda, the two-stage strategy (three samples per pool) reduced the number of tests by 15.34% compared to testing individually; assuming loss of sensitivity and specificity due to pooling, the FNR was higher (FNR = 0.012-0.134) when hrHPV prevalence was low under the two-stage strategy.
Interpretation and novelty
Pooled hrHPV testing has the potential to reduce the number of tests used and enhance screening capacity in resource-limited settings, and notably, a lower prevalence of hrHPV allows for more efficient pooled testing.
Scientific and public-health significance
Pooled hrHPV testing could offer benefits for low- and middle-income countries by reducing test costs and increasing screening capacity, warranting further verification despite potential loss of sensitivity and specificity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a simulation based on real-world data, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the potential loss of sensitivity and specificity with pooling, which could affect false-negative rates, particularly in low-prevalence settings.

重新连线的 DDR-TGF-β-β-连环蛋白-PD-L1 轴可加速 人乳头瘤病毒 驱动的癌症的进展并塑造治疗方法

Rewired DDR-TGF-β-β-catenin-PD-L1 axis accelerates progression and shapes therapy in human papillomavirus-driven cancer
作者： Wei Liu, Shanmei Chen, Liwei Wang, Hua Xiang, Siyi Tao
摘要中文翻译了解 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 致癌机制对于制定预防和治疗策略以及克服 HPV 相关癌症的治疗耐药性至关重要。这些挑战可能源于 高危型HPV 颠覆 宿主 肿瘤抑制因子(如 p53 和 Rb)的能力,并通过同源重组缺陷(HRD)和多网络失调驱动肿瘤发生。从机制上讲,HPV 对 宿主 DNA 损伤反应 (DDR) 产生环境依赖性影响。在附加型复制过程中,E6/E7 激活 DDR 并将 BRCA1/RAD51 招募到复制灶以支持病毒复制,而不会显着损害 宿主 HR 修复。然而,在病毒整合后,持续的 E6/E7 表达会驱动 HRD 并将 DNA 修复转向容易出错的末端连接,从而产生基因组不稳定,从而加剧恶性转化。这些改变在 宫颈癌 中最为明确,而 HPV 阳性非 宫颈癌 中的证据变化更大,需要进一步针对具体情况进行验证。与此同时,HPV E6/E7 拮抗转化生长因子-β (TGF-β) 介导的肿瘤抑制,并可能通过 FAT 非典型钙粘蛋白 4 (FAT4) 下调,参与 Wnt/β-连环蛋白信号传导。由此产生的核 β-连环蛋白升高会诱导程序性死亡配体 1 (PD-L1) 表达,从而促进免疫逃避、干性和侵袭性。值得注意的是,虽然 DDR-TGF-β-β-连环蛋白-PD-L1 轴得到 HPV 相关癌症的大量证据支持,但该通路内的某些联系是从非 HPV 模型或一般通路生物学推断出来的,并在正文中明确指出。凭借残留的 p53 活性,HRD 可能会赋予对 DNA 损伤剂的初始敏感性,但经常会产生耐药性,这种模式让人想起在 HPV 相关癌症的免疫疗法中观察到的初始反应,然后是获得性耐药。整合这些机制见解,我们提出了针对宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 的消融疗法(例如消融、光动力疗法、手术),以及针对晚期或耐药性疾病,提出了一种将基因毒性疗法与 DDR 抑制剂相结合、靶向 DDR-TGF-β-β-catenin-PD-L1 轴的合成致死框架,以及 抗病毒药物 方法。然而,所提出的治疗策略应谨慎解释,因为其证据基础因肿瘤类型而异,需要进一步研究。

问题与背景
了解 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 致癌机制对于制定预防和治疗策略以及克服 HPV 相关癌症的治疗耐药性至关重要。
设计与对象
这些挑战可能源于 高危型HPV 颠覆 宿主 肿瘤抑制因子(如 p53 和 Rb)的能力,并通过同源重组缺陷(HRD)和多网络失调驱动肿瘤发生。
核心方法

主要结果

整合这些机制见解,我们提出了针对宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 的消融疗法(例如消融、光动力疗法、手术),以及针对晚期或耐药性疾病,提出了一种将基因毒性疗法与 DDR 抑制剂相结合、靶向 DDR-TGF-β-β-catenin-PD-L1 轴的合成致死框架,以及 抗病毒药物 方法。
局限与证据强度
然而,所提出的治疗策略应谨慎解释,因为其证据基础因肿瘤类型而异,需要进一步研究。

Rewired DDR-TGF-β-β-catenin-PD-L1 axis accelerates progression and shapes therapy in human papillomavirus-driven cancer

Authors: Wei Liu, Shanmei Chen, Liwei Wang, Hua Xiang, Siyi Tao
Original abstract (English)
Understanding Human papillomavirus (HPV) oncogenic mechanisms is essential for developing preventive and therapeutic strategies and overcoming therapy resistance in HPV-related cancers. These challenges may arise from the ability of high-risk HPV to subvert host tumor suppressors such as p53 and Rb, and to drive oncogenesis through homologous recombination deficiency (HRD) and multi-network dysregulation. Mechanistically, HPV exerts context-dependent effects on the host DNA damage response (DDR). During episomal replication, E6/E7 activate DDR and recruit BRCA1/RAD51 to replication foci to support viral replication without significantly compromising host HR repair. Upon viral integration, however, sustained E6/E7 expression drives HRD and shifts DNA repair toward error-prone end joining, generating genomic instability that fuels malignant transformation. These alterations are most clearly established in cervical cancer, whereas evidence in HPV-positive non-cervical cancer is more variable and requires further context-specific validation. In parallel, HPV E6/E7 antagonize transforming growth factor-β (TGF-β)-mediated tumor suppression and, potentially through FAT Atypical Cadherin 4 (FAT4) down-regulation, engage Wnt/β-catenin signaling. The resultant elevation of nuclear β-catenin induces programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) expression promotes immune evasion, stemness, and invasiveness. Of note, while the DDR-TGF-β-β-catenin-PD-L1 axis is backed by substantial evidence in HPV-related cancers, certain connections within this pathway are extrapolated from non-HPV models or general pathway biology and are explicitly denoted as such in the main text. With residual p53 activity, HRD may confer initial sensitivity to DNA-damaging agents, but resistance frequently develops—a pattern reminiscent of the initial response followed by acquired resistance observed with immunotherapies in HPV-related cancers. Integrating these mechanistic insights, we propose ablative therapies (e.g., ablation, photodynamic therapy, surgery) for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN), and for advanced or resistant disease, a synthetic-lethality framework combining genotoxic therapies with DDR inhibitors, targeting DDR-TGF-β-β-catenin-PD-L1 axis, and antiviral approaches. The proposed therapeutic strategies, however, should be interpreted with caution, as their evidence base varies across tumor types and warrants further investigation.

肺炎球菌和 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 在预防耳鼻喉科疾病中的作用:对儿科实践的启示

The Role of Pneumococcal and Human Papillomavirus Vaccination in Preventing Otolaryngological Diseases: Implications for Pediatric Practice
作者： Carla Ungaro, Flavia Oliva, Giuseppe Ricciardiello, Teresa Abate, Marina Tesorone
摘要中文翻译疫苗接种 是儿科耳鼻喉科一级预防的基石,在减轻儿科传染病负担方面发挥着关键作用,特别是急性中耳炎 (AOM) 和 HPV 相关感染。肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗 (PCV) 的引入和广泛使用与儿童 AOM 的 流行病学 的实质性变化相关,特别是通过减少疫苗血清型疾病和复杂形式,同时导致病原体分布的变化,非 疫苗 血清型和非肺炎球菌细菌的作用增加,需要持续的流行病学研究监测。青少年和年轻人的预防性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 可有效预防 疫苗 型 高危型HPV 的口腔感染,这是 HPV 相关口咽癌 发病机制 中的必要步骤。临床和流行病学研究表明,在接种疫苗的个体中,抗体反应强劲,口腔 HPV 患病率 显着降低。尽管尚无 发病率 减少口咽癌的直接证据,但 疫苗接种 可能会因此减少未来 HPV 相关口咽癌的负担。长期 监测 对于确认这一潜在益处仍然至关重要。实现 疫苗 最佳覆盖的障碍包括获取机会不平等、疫苗接种 吸收不完全以及对 HPV 相关口腔疾病的认识有限。总之,疫苗接种 通过减少复发性 AOM、严重并发症和手术干预,为儿科耳鼻喉科提供直接和临床相关的益处。HPV 疫苗接种 还可以减少 疫苗 型口腔 HPV 感染,因此可能有助于减轻未来 HPV 相关口咽癌的负担。实现高 疫苗接种 覆盖率,加上持续的流行病学监测,对于最大限度地提高个人和公众健康效益以及为具有更广泛血清型覆盖率的 疫苗 的开发提供信息至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨肺炎球菌和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 在预防耳鼻喉科疾病中的作用,特别关注急性中耳炎 (AOM) 和 HPV 相关感染,这些感染是儿科传染病负担的重要因素。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了临床和流行病学证据,证明肺炎球菌和 HPV 疫苗接种 对不同人群和时间范围内的儿科耳鼻喉疾病(包括 AOM 和 HPV 相关感染)的影响。
核心方法
这些方法涉及临床和流行病学研究的综合,评估接种疫苗的个体中的抗体反应和口腔 HPV 患病率 的减少,以及与肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗 (PCV) 相关的 AOM 流行病学 的变化。
主要结果
肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗 (PCV) 的引入和广泛使用与儿科 AOM 的 流行病学 的实质性变化相关,特别是通过 疫苗 血清型疾病和复杂形式的减少,同时通过非 疫苗 血清型和非肺炎球菌细菌的作用增加,导致病原体分布的变化。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 疫苗接种 在儿科耳鼻喉科中的直接和临床相关益处,特别是在减少复发性 AOM、严重并发症和手术干预方面,这代表了对其对特定疾病影响的新认识。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,实现高 疫苗接种 覆盖率,加上持续的流行病学监测,对于最大限度地提高个人和公共健康效益以及为具有更广泛血清型覆盖率的 疫苗 的开发提供信息至关重要。
局限与证据强度
证据基于观察性研究和抽象总结,这在建立因果关系方面可能存在局限性,并且由于缺乏直接的临床试验数据,研究结果得到了中等置信度的支持。

The Role of Pneumococcal and Human Papillomavirus Vaccination in Preventing Otolaryngological Diseases: Implications for Pediatric Practice

Authors: Carla Ungaro, Flavia Oliva, Giuseppe Ricciardiello, Teresa Abate, Marina Tesorone
Original abstract (English)
Vaccinations are a cornerstone of primary prevention in pediatric otolaryngology and play a critical role in reducing the burden of pediatric infectious diseases, particularly acute otitis media (AOM) and HPV-related infections. The introduction and widespread use of pneumococcal conjugate vaccines (PCVs) have been associated with substantial changes in the epidemiology of pediatric AOM, particularly through reductions in vac-cine-serotype disease and complicated forms, while contributing to changes in pathogen distribution with an increased role of non-vaccine serotypes and non-pneumococcal bac-teria, requiring continuous epidemiological surveillance. Prophylactic human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in adolescents and young adults effectively prevents oral infection with vaccine-type high-risk HPV, a necessary step in the pathogenesis of HPV-related oropharyngeal carcinoma. Clinical and epidemiological studies demonstrate robust antibody responses and significant reductions in oral HPV prevalence among vaccinated individuals. Although direct evidence of a reduction in oropharyngeal cancer incidence is not yet available, vaccination may consequently reduce the future burden of HPV-related oropharyngeal cancers. Long-term surveillance remains essential to confirm this potential benefit. Barriers to optimal vaccine coverage include unequal access, incomplete vaccination uptake, and limited awareness of HPV-related oral disease. In conclusion, vaccinations provide direct and clinically relevant benefits in pediatric otolaryngology by reducing recurrent AOM, severe complications and surgical interventions. HPV vaccination also reduces vaccine-type oral HPV infection and may consequently contribute to reducing the future burden of HPV-related oropharyngeal cancers. Achieving high vaccination coverage, coupled with continuous epidemiological monitoring, is essential to maximize individual and public health benefits and to inform the development of vaccines with broader serotype coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the role of pneumococcal and human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in preventing otolaryngological diseases, particularly focusing on acute otitis media (AOM) and HPV-related infections, which are significant contributors to pediatric infectious disease burden.
Design and population
The study is a review of clinical and epidemiological evidence demonstrating the impact of pneumococcal and HPV vaccination on pediatric otolaryngological diseases, including AOM and HPV-related infections, across various populations and timeframes.
Core methods
The methods involved a synthesis of clinical and epidemiological studies that evaluated antibody responses and reductions in oral HPV prevalence among vaccinated individuals, as well as changes in the epidemiology of AOM associated with pneumococcal conjugate vaccines (PCVs).
Main results
The introduction and widespread use of pneumococcal conjugate vaccines (PCVs) have been associated with substantial changes in the epidemiology of pediatric AOM, particularly through reductions in vaccine-serotype disease and complicated forms, while contributing to changes in pathogen distribution with an increased role of non-vaccine serotypes and non-pneumococcal bacteria.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the direct and clinically relevant benefits of vaccinations in pediatric otolaryngology, particularly in reducing recurrent AOM, severe complications, and surgical interventions, which represents a novel understanding of their impact on specific diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that achieving high vaccination coverage, coupled with continuous epidemiological monitoring, is essential to maximize individual and public health benefits and to inform the development of vaccines with broader serotype coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on observational studies and abstract-level summaries, which may have limitations in establishing causality, and the findings are supported by a moderate level of confidence due to the lack of direct clinical trial data.

The greatest challenge in human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is no longer scientific but rather the need to redefine the next era of HPV vaccination to address broader public health implications.

巴勒斯坦成年女性对 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的认识、事实知识、态度和可接受性:一项横断面研究

Awareness, factual knowledge, attitudes, and acceptability of human papillomavirus vaccination among adult women in Palestine: a cross-sectional study
作者： Alhareth M. Amro, Leen M. Safadi, Shahd R. Fahoum, Salahaldeen Deeb, Anas K. Assi, Lamar Aabed, Sadeel Suwan, Rayan Abdalhadi Abdullah, Jomana Sbehat, Raghda Amro
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是 宫颈癌 的一个主要可预防原因,但在许多情况下,对 HPV 疫苗接种 的认识和吸收仍然不够理想。关于巴勒斯坦妇女 HPV 相关意识、事实知识和 疫苗 可接受性的证据仍然有限。
设计与对象
2025 年 10 月至 2026 年 2 月期间,对居住在巴勒斯坦 18 岁以上的女性进行了一项横断面在线调查。
核心方法
一项横断面在线调查评估了社会人口特征、HPV 和 HPV 疫苗 意识、事实知识、态度、疫苗 的吸收、接受或推荐 疫苗接种 的意愿以及感知到的障碍。进行描述性统计、亚组分析和多变量逻辑回归。
主要结果
Among 424 adult women, 220 (51.9%) had heard of HPV and 146 (34.4%) had heard of the HPV vaccine. The mean factual knowledge score was 4.03 ± 2.72 out of 12, and 90 participants (21.2%) had higher factual knowledge. Self-reported HPV vaccine uptake was low (13.0%), whereas willingness to receive the vaccine was higher (55.2%).
解释与创新
巴勒斯坦成年女性对 HPV 相关的认识有限,事实知识较少,而且自我报告的 HPV 疫苗 摄取率也较低。意愿和接受之间的差距,加上作为报告的障碍的意识和安全问题占主导地位,表明需要进行适合文化的教育、提供者主导的咨询以及改善获得可靠的 HPV 疫苗 信息的机会。
科研与公卫意义
鉴于横截面设计和在线便利抽样,应谨慎解释研究结果。该研究强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施,以提高巴勒斯坦人对 HPV 的认识和 疫苗 的吸收。
局限与证据强度
由于该研究的局限性,证据强度中等,但为巴勒斯坦的 HPV 疫苗接种 屏障提供了有价值的见解。

Awareness, factual knowledge, attitudes, and acceptability of human papillomavirus vaccination among adult women in Palestine: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Alhareth M. Amro, Leen M. Safadi, Shahd R. Fahoum, Salahaldeen Deeb, Anas K. Assi, Lamar Aabed, Sadeel Suwan, Rayan Abdalhadi Abdullah, Jomana Sbehat, Raghda Amro
Original abstract (English)
Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a major preventable cause of cervical cancer, yet awareness and uptake of HPV vaccination remain suboptimal in many settings. Evidence regarding women's HPV-related awareness, factual knowledge, and vaccine acceptability in Palestine remains limited. This study assessed awareness, factual knowledge, attitudes, self-reported uptake, willingness, and perceived barriers related to HPV infection and HPV vaccination among adult women in Palestine. Methods A cross-sectional online survey was conducted between October 2025 and February 2026 among women aged ≥18 years residing in Palestine. The questionnaire assessed sociodemographic characteristics, HPV and HPV vaccine awareness, factual knowledge, attitudes, vaccine uptake, willingness to receive or recommend vaccination, and perceived barriers. A 12-item factual knowledge score was calculated, and higher factual knowledge was defined as ≥7 correct responses. Descriptive statistics, subgroup analyses, and multivariable logistic regression were performed. Results Among 424 adult women included in the final analysis, 220 (51.9%) had heard of HPV and 146 (34.4%) had heard of the HPV vaccine. The mean factual knowledge score was 4.03 ± 2.72 out of 12, and 90 participants (21.2%) had higher factual knowledge. Self-reported HPV vaccine uptake was low (13.0%), whereas willingness to receive the vaccine was higher (55.2%). Lack of awareness (55.8%) and concern about vaccine safety (22.8%) were the most frequently reported barriers among unvaccinated women. In expanded adjusted analyses, prior HPV awareness, prior HPV vaccine awareness, and more supportive HPV-vaccination attitudes were independently associated with higher factual knowledge. Higher factual knowledge and supportive attitudes were associated with self-reported vaccine uptake, while willingness among unvaccinated women was most strongly associated with supportive vaccination attitudes. Conclusion Adult women in Palestine demonstrated limited HPV-related awareness and low factual knowledge, together with low self-reported HPV vaccine uptake. The gap between willingness and uptake, combined with the predominance of awareness and safety concerns as reported barriers, suggests a need for culturally appropriate education, provider-led counseling, and improved access to reliable HPV vaccine information. Findings should be interpreted cautiously given the cross-sectional design and online convenience sampling.

Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a major preventable cause of cervical cancer, yet awareness and uptake of HPV vaccination remain suboptimal in many settings. Evidence regarding women's HPV-related awareness, factual knowledge, and vaccine acceptability in Palestine remains limited.
Design and population
A cross-sectional online survey was conducted between October 2025 and February 2026 among women aged ≥18 years residing in Palestine.
Core methods
A cross-sectional online survey assessed sociodemographic characteristics, HPV and HPV vaccine awareness, factual knowledge, attitudes, vaccine uptake, willingness to receive or recommend vaccination, and perceived barriers. Descriptive statistics, subgroup analyses, and multivariable logistic regression were performed.
Main results
Among 424 adult women, 220 (51.9%) had heard of HPV and 146 (34.4%) had heard of the HPV vaccine. The mean factual knowledge score was 4.03 ± 2.72 out of 12, and 90 participants (21.2%) had higher factual knowledge. Self-reported HPV vaccine uptake was low (13.0%), whereas willingness to receive the vaccine was higher (55.2%).
Interpretation and novelty
Adult women in Palestine demonstrated limited HPV-related awareness and low factual knowledge, together with low self-reported HPV vaccine uptake. The gap between willingness and uptake, combined with the predominance of awareness and safety concerns as reported barriers, suggests a need for culturally appropriate education, provider-led counseling, and improved access to reliable HPV vaccine information.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings should be interpreted cautiously given the cross-sectional design and online convenience sampling. The study highlights the need for targeted interventions to improve HPV awareness and vaccine uptake in Palestine.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the study's limitations but provides valuable insights into HPV vaccination barriers in Palestine.

智利中北部地区公共卫生系统中的患病率 人乳头瘤病毒

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile
作者： Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
摘要中文翻译目标 此前,我们发现在私人医疗系统就诊的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的 患病率 较高。因此,本研究的目的是确定在公共卫生系统就诊的大量 队列研究 女性中 HR-HPV 的频率,并比较这些结果。方法 使用 qPCR 和巴氏技术对 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本进行分析。结果 2020-2024年HR-HPV感染总体患病率为13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),2020年、2021年分别为13.07%、12.64%、14.80%、14.24%和14.15%。分别为 2022 年、2023 年和 2024 年。在25岁以下女性中,评估期间HR-HPV的患病率为34.03%,而25至44岁女性为16.35%,45至64岁女性为10.47%,64岁以上女性为11.52%。子宫颈抹片检查显示,HPV 阴性女性的 NILM 结果为 89.71%,而 HPV 阳性女性的 NILM 结果为 74.59%。HPV阴性女性中LSIL和HSIL的发生率分别为0.14%和0.07%;然而,在 HPV 阳性女性中,这些值分别增加到 4.73% 和 4.43%。HPV 阴性女性中只有 0.006% 可能患有癌症,而 HPV 阳性女性中则有 0.46% 可能患有癌症。结论 中北部地区公共卫生系统女性 HR-HPV 感染 患病率 似乎与智利之前报告的相似,但低于同一地区私营医疗系统女性报告的 患病率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
需要进行这项研究来确定就读公共卫生系统的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的频率,因为之前的研究发现私人卫生系统中的 患病率 较高。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究分析了智利中北部地区 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本。
核心方法
使用 qPCR 和 Papanicolaou 技术对样本进行分析,以检测 HR-HPV 感染和细胞学异常。
主要结果
HR-HPV 感染的总体 患病率 为 13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),与年龄较大的群体相比,25 岁​​以下女性的感染率更高(34.03%)。HPV 阳性女性的 LSIL(4.73% vs. 0.14%)和 HSIL(4.43% vs. 0.07%)发生率显着高于 HPV 阴性女性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,公共卫生系统中的 HR-HPV 患病率 与之前报告的全国比率相似,但低于同一地区的私人卫生系统,突显了医疗系统之间 HPV 感染率的潜在差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过提供按年龄分层的 监测 数据为公共卫生 监测 和资源分配提供信息,这可以指导公共卫生系统中 HPV 相关疾病的有针对性的筛查和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势在于其大样本量和纵向设计,但其局限性包括潜在的选择偏差,因为仅包括参加妇科检查的女性,并且由于报告仅具有摘要性质,证据并不充分。

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile

Authors: Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE Previously, we found a high prevalence of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the private health system. Therefore, the objective of this study was to determine the frequency of HR-HPV in a large cohort of women attending the public health system and to compare these findings. METHODS A total of 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique. RESULTS The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection between 2020 and 2024 was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with annual values of 13.07%, 12.64%, 14.80%, 14.24%, and 14.15% in 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, and 2024, respectively. In women under 25 years of age, the prevalence of HR-HPV was 34.03% during the evaluated period, compared to 16.35% in women aged 25 to 44 years, 10.47% in those aged 45 to 64 years, and 11.52% in women over 64 years of age. Pap smears showed 89.71% NILM results in HPV-negative women, compared to 74.59% in HPV-positive women. The rates of LSIL and HSIL were 0.14% and 0.07% in HPV-negative women, respectively; however, these values increased to 4.73% and 4.43% in HPV-positive women, respectively. Probable carcinoma was found in only 0.006% of HPV-negative women, but in 0.46% of HPV-positive women. CONCLUSIONS The prevalence of HR-HPV infection in women attending the public health system in the central-northern region appears to be similar to that previously reported in Chile, but lower than that reported in the same region for women attending the private health system.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to determine the frequency of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the public health system, as previous research had found a high prevalence in the private health system.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study analyzed 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 in the central-northern region of Chile.
Core methods
Samples were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique to detect HR-HPV infection and cytological abnormalities.
Main results
The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with higher rates in women under 25 years (34.03%) compared to older age groups. HPV-positive women had significantly higher rates of LSIL (4.73% vs. 0.14%) and HSIL (4.43% vs. 0.07%) than HPV-negative women.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HR-HPV prevalence in the public health system was similar to previously reported national rates but lower than in the private health system in the same region, highlighting potential disparities in HPV infection rates between healthcare systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings inform public health surveillance and resource allocation by providing age-stratified prevalence data, which can guide targeted screening and prevention strategies for HPV-related diseases in the public health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's strength lies in its large sample size and longitudinal design, but limitations include potential selection bias as only women attending gynecological check-ups were included, and the evidence is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the report.

尼日利亚奥贡州阿贝奥库塔南地方政府区青少年女孩接受 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 的相关因素

Factors Associated with Human Papillomavirus Vaccine Acceptance Among Adolescent Girls in Abeokuta South Local Government Area, Ogun State, Nigeria
作者： Eunice Oluwakemi Ogunmodede, Dolapo Emmanuel Ajala, Sunmibola Esther Oluseye, Rafiat Omotayo Ishola, Ifeoluwa Elizabeth Alao, Taiwo Omotayo Dosumu, Ronke Gbonjubola Ajala, Grace Folasade Olayanju, Adetunmise Oluseyi Olajide
摘要中文翻译本研究评估了尼日利亚奥贡州阿贝奥库塔南地方政府区选定公立中学少女中与 HPV 疫苗 接受度相关的因素。方法 对奥贡州阿贝奥库塔南地方政府区两所公立中学通过多阶段抽样技术选出的 380 名少女进行横断面研究。结果受访者的平均年龄为15.46±94岁。结论 虽然青春期女孩对HPV 疫苗接种的接受度相对较高,但对HPV及其疫苗的了解仍然不够理想。其他人正在查看类似的内容 数据可用性 当前研究期间使用和分析的数据集可根据合理要求从相应作者处获取。尼日利亚妇女接受 宫颈癌 筛查的障碍:系统评价。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 针对成人的建议和资助计划:有针对性的文献综述。人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 撒哈拉以南非洲青少年女学生的吸收率和决定因素:系统评价和荟萃分析。人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 在尼日利亚中北部一所高等教育机构的学生中:一项关于与其意识、吸收和支付意愿相关的社会人口因素的横断面研究。摘要 背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是一个主要的公共卫生问题,也是全球女性中 人乳头瘤病毒 的主要原因。HPV 疫苗接种 仍然是一种有效的预防策略;然而,低收入和中等收入国家青少年对 疫苗 的接受度仍然不够理想。使用结构化自填问卷收集数据。使用 IBM SPSS 版本对数据进行分析...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究评估了尼日利亚奥贡州阿贝奥库塔南地方政府区选定公立中学少女中 HPV 疫苗 接受度的相关因素,解决了低收入和中等收入国家 疫苗 接受度欠佳的问题。
设计与对象
对奥贡州阿贝奥库塔南地方政府区两所公立中学通过多阶段抽样技术选出的 380 名少女进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
使用结构化自填问卷收集数据,并使用描述性统计、卡方检验、Fisher精确检验和二元逻辑回归进行数据分析,显着性水平为p < 0.05。
主要结果
总体而言,285 人 (75.0%) 对 HPV 疫苗接种 表现出较高的接受度,双变量分析显示 HPV 疫苗 接受度与年龄、父母教育和职业、生活安排、知识水平和认知水平之间存在显着相关性。
解释与创新
尽管青春期女孩对 HPV 疫苗接种 的接受度相对较高,但对 HPV 及其 疫苗 的了解仍然不够理想,知识、认知、父母特征和生活安排与报告的接受度显着相关。
科研与公卫意义
加强以学校为基础的健康教育并让家长参与 HPV 疫苗 宣传计划可能会提高知情接受度,并有可能支持未来青少年对 疫苗接种 的吸收。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了因果推断,但大样本量和严格的统计分析提供了中等的证据强度。

Factors Associated with Human Papillomavirus Vaccine Acceptance Among Adolescent Girls in Abeokuta South Local Government Area, Ogun State, Nigeria

Authors: Eunice Oluwakemi Ogunmodede, Dolapo Emmanuel Ajala, Sunmibola Esther Oluseye, Rafiat Omotayo Ishola, Ifeoluwa Elizabeth Alao, Taiwo Omotayo Dosumu, Ronke Gbonjubola Ajala, Grace Folasade Olayanju, Adetunmise Oluseyi Olajide

Question and background
This study assessed factors associated with HPV vaccine acceptance among adolescent girls in selected public secondary schools in Abeokuta South Local Government Area, Ogun State, Nigeria, addressing the suboptimal vaccine acceptance in low- and middle-income countries.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 380 adolescent girls selected through a multistage sampling technique from two public secondary schools in Abeokuta South Local Government Area, Ogun State.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured self-administered questionnaire, and descriptive statistics, chi-square test, Fisher's exact test, and binary logistic regression were used for data analysis at a significance level of p < 0.05.
Main results
Overall, 285 (75.0%) demonstrated high acceptance of HPV vaccination, and bivariate analysis showed significant associations between HPV vaccine acceptance and age, parental education and occupation, living arrangement, knowledge level, and perception level.
Interpretation and novelty
Although acceptance of HPV vaccination among adolescent girls was relatively high, knowledge regarding HPV and its vaccine remained suboptimal, with knowledge, perception, parental characteristics, and living arrangement significantly associated with reported acceptance.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening school-based health education and involving parents in HPV vaccine advocacy programs may improve informed acceptance and potentially support future vaccination uptake among adolescents.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was cross-sectional, limiting causal inference, but the large sample size and rigorous statistical analyses provide moderate evidence strength.

高危型HPV 阳性女性中 HPV 16/18、HPV 整合状态和 CIN3 + 之间的关联:一项多中心横断面研究

Association between HPV 16/18, HPV integration status, and CIN3 + in high-risk HPV-positive women: a multicenter cross-sectional study
作者： Yong Wu, Xin Liu, Xuxu Zhao, Junlong Liu, Hu Zhou, Nan Yu, Ding Ma, Wei Zhang, Xiaowen Pu, Liang Zhuang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危型 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 持续感染,尤其是 16 型和 18 型,是宫颈上皮内瘤变 3 级或更严重 (CIN3+) 的必要原因。该研究解决了有关 HPV 整合状态是否介导 HPV 16/18 和 CIN3+ 之间关联的知识差距,为 HPV 整合检测的临床转化提供信息。
设计与对象
这是在中国的 宫颈癌 筛查项目中进行的一项多中心横断面研究,在研究日期之间招募了 3,077 名 高危型HPV 阳性女性。
核心方法
使用高通量病毒整合检测 (HIVID) 检测 HPV 整合,并使用多变量逻辑回归和结构方程模型 (SEM) 评估 HPV 16/18、HPV 整合和 CIN3+ 之间的关联和中介效应。
主要结果
HPV 16/18 患病率 为 29.5%,HPV 整合 患病率 为 12.9%,CIN3+ 患病率 为 5.9%。在多变量分析中,HPV 整合和 HPV 16/18 均与 CIN3+ 独立相关。
解释与创新
该研究表明,HPV 16/18 对 CIN3+ 的总影响中,HPV 整合介导了 78.1%,这提供了新的证据,表明 HPV 整合状态可能会细化 HPV 阳性女性中 CIN3+ 的风险分层。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 HPV 整合检测纳入 宫颈癌 筛查可以提高对 CIN3+ 风险最高女性的识别,从而有可能指导更有针对性的临床管理和随访。
局限与证据强度
作为一项横断面研究,无法建立时间性,限制了因果推断;鉴于单一国家的背景和对筛查计划数据的依赖,证据强度中等。

Association between HPV 16/18, HPV integration status, and CIN3 + in high-risk HPV-positive women: a multicenter cross-sectional study

Authors: Yong Wu, Xin Liu, Xuxu Zhao, Junlong Liu, Hu Zhou, Nan Yu, Ding Ma, Wei Zhang, Xiaowen Pu, Liang Zhuang
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to investigate the association between human papillomavirus (HPV) 16/18, HPV integration status, and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 or worse (CIN3 +), and to provide evidence and theoretical support for the clinical translation of HPV integration testing. This was a multicenter cross-sectional study conducted within a cervical cancer screening program in China. HPV integration was detected using high-throughput viral integration detection (HIVID). Structural equation modeling (SEM) was employed to examine the mediating effect of HPV integration. A total of 3,077 high-risk HPV-positive women were included in the final analysis. Among these participants, 29.5% were infected with HPV 16/18, 12.9% tested positive for HPV integration, and 5.9% had biopsy-confirmed CIN3 + . Multivariable logistic regression analysis demonstrated that both HPV integration and HPV 16/18 were independently associated with CIN3 + . In mediation analysis, the standardized path coefficient for the direct effect of HPV 16/18 on CIN3 + was 0.087. For the indirect effect, the standardized path coefficient from HPV 16/18 to HPV integration was 0.404, and from HPV integration to CIN3 + was 0.771. The mediating effect of HPV integration accounted for 78.1% of the total effect. This study elucidates the mediating role of HPV integration in the progression from HPV 16/18 infection to CIN3 + , offering new insights into HPV risk stratification and clinical management.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomaviruses (HPV), particularly types 16 and 18, is a necessary cause of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 or worse (CIN3+). The study addressed a knowledge gap regarding whether HPV integration status mediates the association between HPV 16/18 and CIN3+ to inform clinical translation of HPV integration testing.
Design and population
This was a multicenter cross-sectional study conducted within a cervical cancer screening program in China, enrolling 3,077 high-risk HPV-positive women between the study dates.
Core methods
HPV integration was detected using high-throughput viral integration detection (HIVID), and multivariable logistic regression and structural equation modeling (SEM) were used to assess associations and mediating effects between HPV 16/18, HPV integration, and CIN3+.
Main results
HPV 16/18 prevalence was 29.5%, HPV integration prevalence was 12.9%, and CIN3+ prevalence was 5.9%. Both HPV integration and HPV 16/18 were independently associated with CIN3+ in multivariable analysis.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that HPV integration mediates 78.1% of the total effect of HPV 16/18 on CIN3+, providing new evidence that HPV integration status may refine risk stratification for CIN3+ in HPV-positive women.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that incorporating HPV integration testing into cervical cancer screening could improve identification of women at highest risk for CIN3+, potentially guiding more targeted clinical management and follow-up.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a cross-sectional study, temporality cannot be established, limiting causal inference; the evidence strength is moderate given the single-country setting and reliance on screening-program data.

多表位 HPV16 E7 疫苗 构建体中 Gp96 和钙网蛋白 T 细胞表位的方向影响预测的免疫刺激特性:一项计算机模拟和表达验证研究

Orientation of Gp96 and Calreticulin T-cell epitopes in a multiepitope HPV16 E7 vaccine construct affects predicted immunostimulatory properties: An in silico and expression validation study
作者： Giti Esmail Nia, Zahra Shahosseini, Elahe Akbari, Hadi Bamehr, Fatemeh Heidarnejad, Monireh Mohsenzadegan, Azam Bolhassani, Mohammadmorad Farajollahi
摘要中文翻译热休克蛋白 (HSP) 可用作佐剂来开发治疗性 疫苗,因为它们增强相关肿瘤抗原的交叉呈递并刺激 T 细胞。在这项研究中,评估了 gp96 和钙网蛋白的免疫刺激特性,以增强基于 HPV16 E7 的 疫苗 的有效性。基于对 MHC-I/II 的高结合亲和力、强免疫原性和群体覆盖度,进行计算机评估以从 HSP 中选择表位。以不同方向设计了六种新型多表位构建体,其中包含 E7、gp96 和钙网蛋白保守表位。在这些构建体与信号传导 (TLR) 和内吞(CD14、CD91、LOX-1 和 SREC-1)受体之间进行分子对接。经过分子对接和MD模拟确定了有效的构建体后,制备了含有HPV16 E7、gp96和钙网蛋白8个(CTL/HTL)表位的原核表达质粒,并在E中生成了重组多表位肽。我们的数据表明,gp96-CRT-E7构建体与受体(特别是TLR2和LOX-1)具有优异的对接分数,这表明比其他构建体具有更强的先天和适应性免疫刺激作用构造。预计它是无毒、非过敏性、抗原性、免疫原性和结构稳定的。此外,多表位gp96-CRT-E7融合肽在OD 600:0.6-0.7、0.5 mM IPTG、温度18°C、IPTG诱导24小时的条件下在Rosetta菌株中表达,并在天然条件下通过亲和层析纯化。一般来说,多表位 gp96-CRT-E7 融合肽的计算机模拟和表达验证的成功结果表明其作为 HPV16 疫苗 开发的新候选者的强大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
热休克蛋白 (HSP) 可用作佐剂来开发治疗性 疫苗,因为它们增强相关肿瘤抗原的交叉呈递并刺激 T 细胞。在这项研究中,评估了 gp96 和钙网蛋白的免疫刺激特性,以增强基于 HPV16 E7 的 疫苗 的有效性。
设计与对象
以不同方向设计了六种新型多表位构建体,其中包含 E7、gp96 和钙网蛋白保守表位。在这些构建体与信号传导 (TLR) 和内吞(CD14、CD91、LOX-1 和 SREC-1)受体之间进行分子对接。通过分子对接和MD模拟确定有效构建体后,制备了含有HPV16 E7、gp96和钙网蛋白8个合适方向表位的原核表达质粒,并在大肠杆菌中产生了重组多表位肽。
核心方法
基于对 MHC-I/II 的高结合亲和力、强免疫原性和群体覆盖度,进行计算机评估以从 HSP 中选择表位。多表位gp96-CRT-E7融合肽在特定条件下在Rosetta菌株中表达,并通过亲和层析纯化。
主要结果
我们的数据表明,gp96-CRT-E7 构建体与受体(特别是 TLR2 和 LOX-1)具有优异的对接分数,表明比其他构建体对先天免疫和适应性免疫具有更强的刺激作用。预计它是无毒、非过敏性、抗原性、免疫原性和结构稳定的。一般来说,多表位 gp96-CRT-E7 融合肽的计算机模拟和表达验证的成功结果表明其作为 HPV16 疫苗 开发的新候选者的强大潜力。
解释与创新
多表位 HPV16 E7 疫苗 构建体中 Gp96 和钙网蛋白 T 细胞表位的方向影响预测的免疫刺激特性,其中 gp96-CRT-E7 构建体表现出卓越的免疫刺激潜力。这项研究通过计算机模拟和表达验证为 HPV16 疫苗 开发提供了一种新的候选药物。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,gp96-CRT-E7 构建体具有作为 HPV16 疫苗 开发的新候选者的强大潜力,这可以增强 疫苗 治疗 HPV 相关癌症的有效性。这种方法可能有助于改善 HPV 相关疾病的免疫治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于原核系统中的计算机预测和表达验证,没有体内或临床评估。证据强度中等,因为该研究证明了成功的表达和预测的免疫原性,但缺乏生物系统或人体试验的验证。

Orientation of Gp96 and Calreticulin T-cell epitopes in a multiepitope HPV16 E7 vaccine construct affects predicted immunostimulatory properties: An in silico and expression validation study

Authors: Giti Esmail Nia, Zahra Shahosseini, Elahe Akbari, Hadi Bamehr, Fatemeh Heidarnejad, Monireh Mohsenzadegan, Azam Bolhassani, Mohammadmorad Farajollahi
Original abstract (English)
Heat shock proteins (HSPs) can be used as adjuvants to develop therapeutic vaccines, as they enhance the cross-presentation of related tumor antigens and stimulate T cells. In this study, the immunostimulatory properties of gp96 and calreticulin was evaluated to enhance HPV16 E7-based vaccines effectiveness. In silico evaluation was performed to select epitopes from HSPs based on high binding affinity to MHC-I/II, strong immunogenicity, and population coverage. Six novel multiepitope constructs harboring conserved epitopes of E7, gp96, and calreticulin were designed in different orientations. Molecular docking was performed between these constructs and signaling (TLRs) and endocytic (CD14, CD91, LOX-1, and SREC-1) receptors. After determination of an effective construct in molecular docking and MD simulation, prokaryotic expression plasmid containing eight (CTL/HTL) epitopes from HPV16 E7, gp96, and calreticulin in suitable orientation was prepared, and the recombinant multiepitope peptide was generated in E. Our data showed that the gp96-CRT-E7 construct had superior docking scores with receptors (especially TLR2 and LOX-1), suggesting stronger stimulation of both innate and adaptive immunity than other constructs. It was predicted to be non-toxic, non-allergenic, antigenic, immunogenic, and structurally stable. Moreover, the multiepitope gp96-CRT-E7 fusion peptide was expressed in the Rosetta strain under conditions of OD 600 : 0.6–0.7, 0.5 mM IPTG, temperature of 18oC, and 24 hours after IPTG induction, and purified through affinity chromatography under native conditions. Generally, successful results of in silico and expression validation of the multiepitope gp96-CRT-E7 fusion peptide showed its strong potential as a novel candidate for HPV16 vaccine development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Heat shock proteins (HSPs) can be used as adjuvants to develop therapeutic vaccines, as they enhance the cross-presentation of related tumor antigens and stimulate T cells. In this study, the immunostimulatory properties of gp96 and calreticulin were evaluated to enhance HPV16 E7-based vaccine effectiveness.
Design and population
Six novel multiepitope constructs harboring conserved epitopes of E7, gp96, and calreticulin were designed in different orientations. Molecular docking was performed between these constructs and signaling (TLRs) and endocytic (CD14, CD91, LOX-1, and SREC-1) receptors. After determination of an effective construct in molecular docking and MD simulation, a prokaryotic expression plasmid containing eight epitopes from HPV16 E7, gp96, and calreticulin in suitable orientation was prepared, and the recombinant multiepitope peptide was generated in E.
Core methods
In silico evaluation was performed to select epitopes from HSPs based on high binding affinity to MHC-I/II, strong immunogenicity, and population coverage. The multiepitope gp96-CRT-E7 fusion peptide was expressed in the Rosetta strain under specific conditions and purified through affinity chromatography.
Main results
Our data showed that the gp96-CRT-E7 construct had superior docking scores with receptors (especially TLR2 and LOX-1), suggesting stronger stimulation of both innate and adaptive immunity than other constructs. It was predicted to be non-toxic, non-allergenic, antigenic, immunogenic, and structurally stable. Generally, successful results of in silico and expression validation of the multiepitope gp96-CRT-E7 fusion peptide showed its strong potential as a novel candidate for HPV16 vaccine development.
Interpretation and novelty
The orientation of Gp96 and Calreticulin T-cell epitopes in a multiepitope HPV16 E7 vaccine construct affects predicted immunostimulatory properties, with the gp96-CRT-E7 construct demonstrating superior immunostimulatory potential. This study provides a novel candidate for HPV16 vaccine development through in silico and expression validation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the gp96-CRT-E7 construct has strong potential as a novel candidate for HPV16 vaccine development, which could enhance the effectiveness of therapeutic vaccines against HPV-associated cancers. This approach may contribute to improved immunotherapy strategies for HPV-related diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in silico predictions and expression validation in a prokaryotic system, without in vivo or clinical evaluation. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study demonstrates successful expression and predicted immunogenicity but lacks validation in biological systems or human trials.

来自组织环境的数据塑造了 HPV16 E6/E7 突变体 Pik3ca 转基因小鼠模型中不同的癌前结果

Data from Tissue Context Shapes Distinct Premalignant Outcomes in an HPV16 E6/E7-Mutant Pik3ca Transgenic Mouse Model
作者： Marina Ayre, Gonzalo Fernandez-Ugazio, Anabel V. DiGaudio, Omar Adrián Coso, J. Silvio Gutkind, Ana Rosa Raimondi
摘要中文翻译E6/E7 表达足以诱导 TG-E6/E7 小鼠肛管鳞状上皮内病变的发生。A,雌性小鼠生殖器区域低倍放大全景图,显示小鼠肛管(黑色虚线矩形)相对于阴道(左下)的位置。B,A 中矩形轮廓区域的更高放大倍数,显示对照动物肛管的正常组织学。C-F,TG-E6/E7 肛门粘膜发生形态变化。C,对照小鼠肛门粘膜上皮的代表性组织学图像。D–F,TG-E6/E7 肛门粘膜 (AM) 中 LSIL 的代表性实例。D,DOX 治疗 后 2 个月,肛门粘膜内衬过度正角化鳞状上皮,显示轻度至中度发育不良变化。进化 2 个月后 E、AM 出现中度发育不良变化。注意涉及下上皮层和中上皮层的颗粒过多、基底分层以及细胞和核不规则性。F、AM 在 进化 治疗 4 个月后出现严重发育不良性改变 (HSIL)。角化过度以及涉及中上皮层的细胞和核不规则。G,TG-E6/E7小鼠肛门粘膜中E6的IHC表达。在 G 中,低倍图像显示 E6 表达沿肛管的斑片状图案。黑色矩形表示具有阳性染色的区域,以较高的放大倍数显示在右侧。注意 E6 在基底上皮层中的表达(顶部)以及 E6 在单个基底上细胞中的细胞质定位(底部)。H,G 所示的 E6/E7 转基因小鼠的肛周皮肤,缺乏 E6 阳性细胞。I,TG-E6/E7 肛门粘膜中不含一抗的对照。J,发病率 对照小鼠(N=10)和TG-E6/E7动物(N=17)肛门粘膜中LSIL和HSIL占每组动物总数的百分比。LSIL,诊断为轻度不典型增生、增生以及涉及上皮下三分之一的细胞和组织改变的病例;HSIL,诊断为中度或重度发育不良。TG-E6/E7:2 个月(N = 7)、4 个月(N = 5)和 5-6 个月(N = 5)。*,P K,K14 的 IHC 表达。显示 K14 表达的 TG-E6/E7 肛门粘膜的低放大倍率概览。右图是增生和角化过度区域的高倍放大图像,显示整个上皮厚度中都有 K14 表达。L,K7 的免疫染色。TG-E6/E7 肛门粘膜的低放大倍数概览显示无 K7 表达。M,K7 阴性的 TG-E6/E7 肛门粘膜区域的详细视图。插图,K7 的阳性对照;腺上皮(肠)。N,TG-E6/E7 动物肛门粘膜中 pS6 的免疫染色。对照动物的肛门粘膜显示出最小的 pS6 表达。E6/E7 诱导后 2 个月,在基底上层观察到 pS6 在肛门粘膜中的表达;E6/E7 诱导后 4 个月,动物肛门粘膜中 pS6 的表达。比例尺,1 毫米 (A);250微米(B);200微米(C);100μm(K和L);50 μm(F-I 和 N);25 μm(D、E、M 以及 C 和 K 中的插图)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨组织背景如何在 HPV16 E6/E7 突变型 Pik3ca 转基因小鼠模型中塑造不同的癌前结局,重点关注肛管鳞状上皮内病变的发展。
设计与对象
该研究使用具有HPV16 E6/E7表达和Pik3ca 突变的转基因小鼠模型(TG-E6/E7),比较转基因动物(N=17)与对照(N=10)在不同时间点(2、4和5-6个月)的肛门粘膜病变。
核心方法
进行组织病理学分析和免疫组织化学 (IHC) 以评估转基因小鼠和对照小鼠肛门粘膜中的发育异常变化、E6 表达以及 K14、K7 和 pS6 等标记物。
主要结果
TG-E6/E7小鼠在肛门粘膜中出现低度(LSIL)和高度(HSIL)鳞状上皮内病变,E6表达局限于基底层和基底上层,而对照组则没有病变或E6表达。
解释与创新
作者解释说,肛管中的组织特异性因素会导致不同的癌前结果,其中 E6/E7 表达足以诱发病变,并且该研究证明了该模型在研究 HPV 相关癌发生方面的实用性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 HPV 驱动的致癌机制的见解,并可能为 监测 和人类 HPV 相关肛门病变的预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
尽管该研究提供了详细的组织病理学和分子数据,但由于样本量有限且依赖单一动物模型,证据强度中等。

Data from Tissue Context Shapes Distinct Premalignant Outcomes in an HPV16 E6/E7-Mutant Pik3ca Transgenic Mouse Model

Authors: Marina Ayre, Gonzalo Fernandez-Ugazio, Anabel V. DiGaudio, Omar Adrián Coso, J. Silvio Gutkind, Ana Rosa Raimondi
Original abstract (English)
E6/E7 expression is sufficient to induce the development of squamous intraepithelial lesions in the anal canal of TG-E6/E7 mice. A, Panoramic view at low magnification of the genital area in a female mouse, showing the location of the murine anal canal (black dotted rectangle) in relation to the vagina (bottom left). B, Higher magnification of the area outlined in the rectangle in A , showing the normal histology of the anal canal in a control animal. C–F, Morphologic changes developed in the TG-E6/E7 anal mucosa. C, Representative histologic image of the anal mucosal epithelium from a control mouse. D–F, Representative examples of LSIL in TG-E6/E7 anal mucosa (AM). D, Anal mucosa lined by the hyperorthokeratotic squamous epithelium showing mild to moderate dysplastic changes 2 months after DOX treatment. E, AM with moderate dysplastic changes after 2 months of evolution. Note the hypergranulosis, basal stratification, and cellular and nuclear irregularities involving the lower and middle epithelial layers. F, AM with severe dysplastic changes (HSIL) after 4 months of evolution. Hyperkeratosis and cellular and nuclear irregularities involving the middle and upper epithelial layers. G, IHC expression of E6 in the anal mucosa of TG-E6/E7 mice. In G , a low-magnification image shows the patchy pattern of E6 expression along the anal canal. Black rectangles indicate areas with positive staining, shown at higher magnification to the right. Note the expression of E6 in the basal epithelial layer (top) as well as the cytoplasmic localization of E6 in individual suprabasal cells (bottom). H, Perianal skin from the E6/E7 transgenic mouse shown in G , lacking E6-positive cells. I, Control without primary antibody in TG-E6/E7 anal mucosa. J, Incidence of LSIL and HSIL in the anal mucosa of control mice ( N = 10) and TG-E6/E7 animals ( N = 17) as the percentage of the total number of animals per group. LSIL, cases diagnosed with mild dysplasia, hyperplasia, and cellular and tissue alterations involving the lower third of the epithelium; HSIL, diagnosed with moderate or severe dysplasia. TG-E6/E7: 2 months ( N = 7), 4 months ( N = 5), and 5–6 months ( N = 5). *, P K, IHC expression of K14. Low-magnification overview of TG-E6/E7 anal mucosa showing K14 expression. Right, a higher-magnification image of a hyperplastic and hyperkeratotic area shows K14 expression throughout the entire epithelial thickness. L, Immunostaining for K7. Low-magnification overview of TG-E6/E7 anal mucosa showing no K7 expression. M, Detailed view of an TG-E6/E7 anal mucosal area negative for K7. Inset, positive control for K7; glandular epithelium (intestine). N, Immunostaining for pS6 in the anal mucosa of TG-E6/E7 animals. Anal mucosa of a control animal showing minimal pS6 expression. pS6 expression in anal mucosa 2 months after E6/E7 induction, observed in the suprabasal layers; pS6 expression in anal mucosa of an animal 4 months after E6/E7 induction. Scale bars, 1 mm ( A ); 250 μm ( B ); 200 μm ( C ); 100 μm ( K and L ); 50 μm ( F–I and N ); 25 μm ( D , E , M , and insets in C and K ).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how tissue context shapes distinct premalignant outcomes in an HPV16 E6/E7-mutant Pik3ca transgenic mouse model, focusing on the development of squamous intraepithelial lesions in the anal canal.
Design and population
The study used a transgenic mouse model (TG-E6/E7) with HPV16 E6/E7 expression and Pik3ca mutation, comparing anal mucosa lesions in transgenic animals (N=17) to controls (N=10) at different time points (2, 4, and 5-6 months).
Core methods
Histopathological analysis and immunohistochemistry (IHC) were performed to assess dysplastic changes, E6 expression, and markers like K14, K7, and pS6 in the anal mucosa of transgenic and control mice.
Main results
TG-E6/E7 mice developed low-grade (LSIL) and high-grade (HSIL) squamous intraepithelial lesions in the anal mucosa, with E6 expression localized to basal and suprabasal layers, while controls showed no lesions or E6 expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that tissue-specific factors in the anal canal drive distinct premalignant outcomes, with E6/E7 expression sufficient to induce lesions, and the study demonstrated the model's utility for studying HPV-associated carcinogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into HPV-driven carcinogenesis mechanisms and may inform surveillance and prevention strategies for HPV-associated anal lesions in humans.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited sample size and reliance on a single animal model, though the study provides detailed histopathological and molecular data.

在早期和持续感染模型中,NFX1-123 是角质形成细胞分化和 HPV 16 DNA 维持所必需的

NFX1-123 is required for keratinocyte differentiation and HPV 16 DNA maintenance in early and persistent infection models
作者： Maura A. Dankoski, DeShawn Thompson, Kevin M. Quist, Rachel A. Katzenellenbogen
摘要中文翻译摘要 持续高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR HPV) 感染导致全球近 5% 的癌症,包括宫颈癌和口咽癌。HR HPV 通过在基底角质形成细胞中维持病毒 基因组 的拷贝并利用上层的细胞分化过程来协调病毒生命周期中的关键步骤,从而维持分层上皮组织中的长期感染。我们之前已经证明,内源性 宿主 蛋白 NFX1-123 直接与 HR HPV 16 型癌蛋白 E6 (16E6) 结合,并且这种伙伴关系增加了角质形成细胞增殖和分化,这两个过程都是持续 HPV 16 感染所必需的。由于正常基底角质形成细胞中存在一系列 NFX1-123 内源性表达,我们旨在研究 NFX1-123 丰度对 HPV 16 感染的起始和持续性的作用。在这项研究中,我们利用 NFX1-123 过度表达、内源性或降低水平的角质形成细胞,并用 HPV 16 准病毒 (Qsv) 感染这些细胞,以探究 NFX1-123 调制对角质形成细胞生长和病毒 DNA 拷贝数的变化。NFX1-123 减少的角质形成细胞在筏培养物中的分化和分层减弱,这与 HPV 16 Qsv DNA 拷贝数减少相关。同样,当我们减少 W12E 细胞(一种含有附加型 HPV 16 基因组 的宫颈细胞系)中的 NFX1-123 时,我们发现与含有内源性 NFX1-123 的 W12E 细胞相比,筏培养物分化和厚度减少,病毒 基因组 拷贝数减少。这些发现表明 NFX1-123 在 HPV 16 感染的启动和维持中的重要性,这种感染可能持续并进展为癌症。重要性 持续数十年的高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是患宫颈癌和其他 HPV 相关癌症的最大危险因素。HPV 维持其 基因组 并在复层上皮中持续存在的能力取决于其与 宿主 蛋白的伙伴关系及其对细胞途径的选择。在这里,我们研究了 HPV 16 的 宿主 蛋白伴侣 NFX1-123 的丰度如何影响单层和三维培养物中病毒 基因组 的维持和复制,模拟早期和持续感染。这项研究表明,NFX1-123 是筏培养物中角质形成细胞分化和分层所必需的,并且这些过程的破坏显着阻碍了 HPV 16 将其附加型 基因组 维持在一致拷贝数的能力。这些发现表明 NFX1-123 对于 HPV 16 感染持续存在的重要性,并鼓励进一步研究 NFX1-123 作为 HPV 16 阳性癌症和癌前病变的潜在治疗靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
持续的高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR HPV) 感染导致全球近 5% 的癌症,包括宫颈癌和口咽癌。该研究旨在调查 NFX1-123 丰度对 HPV 16 感染的起始和持续性的作用。
设计与对象
该研究利用 NFX1-123 过度表达、内源性或降低水平的角质形成细胞,并用 HPV 16 准病毒 (Qsv) 感染这些细胞来模拟早期和持续感染。
核心方法
具有调节的 NFX1-123 水平的角质形成细胞被 HPV 16 Qsv 感染,并在单层和三维筏培养物中分析角质形成细胞生长和病毒 DNA 拷贝数的变化。
主要结果
NFX1-123 减少的角质形成细胞在筏培养物中的分化和分层减弱,这与 HPV 16 Qsv DNA 拷贝数减少相关。同样,减少 W12E 细胞中的 NFX1-123 会降低筏培养物的分化、厚度和病毒 基因组 拷贝。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,NFX1-123 是角质形成细胞分化和分层所必需的,其破坏会显着损害 HPV 16 将其附加型 基因组 维持在一致拷贝数的能力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了 NFX1-123 对于 HPV 16 感染持续存在的重要性,并表明它是 HPV 16 阳性癌症和癌前病变的潜在治疗靶点。
局限与证据强度
该研究是使用角质形成细胞模型在体外进行的,该模型可能无法完全复制体内条件。由于使用细胞培养模型并且需要进一步体内验证,证据强度中等。

NFX1-123 is required for keratinocyte differentiation and HPV 16 DNA maintenance in early and persistent infection models

Authors: Maura A. Dankoski, DeShawn Thompson, Kevin M. Quist, Rachel A. Katzenellenbogen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR HPV) infections cause nearly 5% of cancers worldwide, including cervical and oropharyngeal cancers. HR HPVs sustain long-term infections in stratified epithelial tissues by maintaining copies of viral genomes in basal keratinocytes and utilizing the cellular differentiation processes in the upper layers to coordinate key steps in the viral life cycle. We have demonstrated previously that the endogenous host protein NFX1-123 bound directly to the HR HPV type 16 oncoprotein E6 (16E6), and that this partnership increased keratinocyte proliferation and differentiation, two processes that are both required for persistent HPV 16 infections. As there is a range of endogenous expression of NFX1-123 in normal basal keratinocytes, we aimed to investigate the role of NFX1-123 abundance on the initiation and persistence of HPV 16 infections. In this study, we utilized keratinocytes with overexpressed, endogenous, or reduced levels of NFX1-123 and infected these cells with an HPV 16 quasivirus (Qsv) to interrogate changes to keratinocyte growth and viral DNA copy numbers with NFX1-123 modulation. Keratinocytes with reduced NFX1-123 had blunted differentiation and stratification in raft cultures, and this correlated with decreased HPV 16 Qsv DNA copies. Similarly, when we reduced NFX1-123 in W12E cells, a cervical cell line harboring episomal HPV 16 genomes, we saw a decrease in raft culture differentiation and thickness, and fewer viral genome copies compared to W12E cells with endogenous NFX1-123. These findings indicate the importance of NFX1-123 on the initiation and maintenance of HPV 16 infections that may persist and progress to cancer. IMPORTANCE A high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection that persists for decades is the biggest risk factor for the development of cervical and other HPV-associated cancers. The ability of HPV to maintain its genome and persist in stratified epithelium is dependent on its partnership with host proteins and its co-opting of cellular pathways. Here, we investigate how the abundance of HPV 16’s host protein partner NFX1-123 affects viral genome maintenance and replication in monolayer and three-dimensional cultures, modeling both early and persistent infection. This study reveals that NFX1-123 is required for keratinocyte differentiation and stratification in raft cultures, and the disruption of these processes significantly impedes the ability of HPV 16 to maintain its episomal genome at consistent copy numbers. These findings indicate the importance of NFX1-123 for the persistence of HPV 16 infections and encourage further investigation of NFX1-123 as a potential therapeutic target in HPV 16-positive cancers and precancers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR HPV) infections cause nearly 5% of cancers worldwide, including cervical and oropharyngeal cancers. The study aimed to investigate the role of NFX1-123 abundance on the initiation and persistence of HPV 16 infections.
Design and population
The study utilized keratinocytes with overexpressed, endogenous, or reduced levels of NFX1-123 and infected these cells with an HPV 16 quasivirus (Qsv) to model both early and persistent infection.
Core methods
Keratinocytes with modulated NFX1-123 levels were infected with HPV 16 Qsv, and changes to keratinocyte growth and viral DNA copy numbers were analyzed in monolayer and three-dimensional raft cultures.
Main results
Keratinocytes with reduced NFX1-123 had blunted differentiation and stratification in raft cultures, correlating with decreased HPV 16 Qsv DNA copies. Similarly, reducing NFX1-123 in W12E cells decreased raft culture differentiation, thickness, and viral genome copies.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that NFX1-123 is required for keratinocyte differentiation and stratification, and its disruption significantly impairs HPV 16's ability to maintain its episomal genome at consistent copy numbers.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the importance of NFX1-123 for the persistence of HPV 16 infections and suggest it as a potential therapeutic target in HPV 16-positive cancers and precancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in vitro using keratinocyte models, which may not fully replicate in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the use of cell culture models and the need for further validation in vivo.

活乳酸菌制剂治疗HPV感染并发细菌性阴道病患者宫颈炎症及清除病毒感染的疗效

Efficacy of live lactobacillus preparations in treating cervical inflammation and clearing viral infections in patients with HPV infection complicated by bacterial vaginosis
作者： Yan Cui, Xiaoping Cheng
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评价定君生(一种活乳杆菌制剂)辅助治疗人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染并发细菌性阴道病(BV)患者的有效性和安全性。
设计与对象
对152例符合条件的患者进行回顾性分析,随机分为两组:观察组(n=87)接受标准BV抗菌治疗加定君生治疗,对照组(n=65)单独接受标准BV抗菌治疗。对患者进行了六个月的随访。
核心方法
主要结局包括 HPV 清除率和宫颈细胞学炎症改善,次要结局包括 BV 复发率和不良反应 发病率。
主要结果
The results showed that the observation group had a significantly higher HPV clearance rate (73.6% vs 53.9%) and inflammation improvement rate (90.8% vs 76.9%), as well as a lower BV recurrence rate (3.1% vs 13.9%) compared to the control group. Dingjunsheng was identified as an independent protective factor for HPV clearance (OR=2.5, 95%CI:1.3–5.1).
解释与创新
作者得出结论,定君生辅助治疗在改善 HPV 清除、减轻炎症和减少 BV 复发方面显示出有效性,且安全性良好,使其成为这些患者的有价值的 治疗 策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,定君生作为辅助治疗可以改善 HPV 感染并发 BV 患者的预后,有可能降低复发率并提高病毒清除率。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性分析,可能会引入选择偏差。由于研究设计和样本量,证据强度中等。

Efficacy of live lactobacillus preparations in treating cervical inflammation and clearing viral infections in patients with HPV infection complicated by bacterial vaginosis

Authors: Yan Cui, Xiaoping Cheng
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to evaluate the efficacy and safety of Dingjunsheng, a live Lactobacillus preparation, as adjuvant therapy in patients with human papillomavirus (HPV) infection complicated by bacterial vaginosis (BV). A retrospective analysis was conducted on 152 eligible patients, randomly divided into two groups: the observation group (n=87) receiving standard BV antibacterial therapy plus Dingjunsheng, and the control group (n=65) who received standard BV antibacterial therapy alone. The patients were followed up for six months. Primary outcomes included HPV clearance rate and cervical cytological inflammation improvement, while secondary outcomes involved BV recurrence rate and adverse reaction incidence. The results showed that the observation group had a significantly higher HPV clearance rate (73.6% vs 53.9%) and inflammation improvement rate (90.8% vs 76.9%), as well as a lower BV recurrence rate (3.1% vs 13.9%) compared to the control group. Dingjunsheng was identified as an independent protective factor for HPV clearance (OR=2.5, 95%CI:1.3–5.1). The incidence of adverse reactions was similar between both groups. We conclude that Dingjunsheng adjuvant therapy demonstrated effectiveness in improving HPV clearance, alleviating inflammation, and reducing BV recurrence, with good safety, making it a valuable treatment strategy for these patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the efficacy and safety of Dingjunsheng, a live Lactobacillus preparation, as adjuvant therapy in patients with human papillomavirus (HPV) infection complicated by bacterial vaginosis (BV).
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on 152 eligible patients, randomly divided into two groups: the observation group (n=87) receiving standard BV antibacterial therapy plus Dingjunsheng, and the control group (n=65) who received standard BV antibacterial therapy alone. The patients were followed up for six months.
Core methods
Primary outcomes included HPV clearance rate and cervical cytological inflammation improvement, while secondary outcomes involved BV recurrence rate and adverse reaction incidence.
Main results
The results showed that the observation group had a significantly higher HPV clearance rate (73.6% vs 53.9%) and inflammation improvement rate (90.8% vs 76.9%), as well as a lower BV recurrence rate (3.1% vs 13.9%) compared to the control group. Dingjunsheng was identified as an independent protective factor for HPV clearance (OR=2.5, 95%CI:1.3–5.1).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that Dingjunsheng adjuvant therapy demonstrated effectiveness in improving HPV clearance, alleviating inflammation, and reducing BV recurrence, with good safety, making it a valuable treatment strategy for these patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that Dingjunsheng as an adjuvant therapy could improve outcomes in patients with HPV infection complicated by BV, potentially reducing recurrence rates and enhancing viral clearance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a retrospective analysis, which may introduce selection bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the study design and sample size.

患病率 和肯尼亚男男性行为者阴茎和直肠 HPV 感染相关的行为因素

Prevalence and behavioral factors associated with penile and rectal HPV infection among men who have sex with men in Kenya
作者： Kate Klein, Fredrick O. Otieno, Walter Agingu, Patriciah Wambua, Felix Ochieng, Minjee Lee, Lyle R. McKinnon, Lifang Hou, Supriya D. Mehta
摘要中文翻译方法 我们对 18-35 岁 MSM 的基线数据进行了横断面分析。结果 阴茎 hrHPV 患病率 为 24.7%(39/158),直肠 hrHPV 患病率 为 12.5%(22/176);在两个站点进行测试的参与者中有 6.7% 患有双站点感染。这些发现支持扩大性别中性 HPV 疫苗接种 和病因性 STI 筛查,以降低撒哈拉以南非洲 MSM 中 HPV 相关癌症风险。资助 这项研究得到了美国国立卫生研究院、国立过敏和传染病研究所的 R01-AI177005 号拨款(联系 MPI:Mehta)以及博士的催化剂资助。这项研究得到了拉什大学医学中心 Jaramogi Odinga Oginga 教学和转诊医院 (JOOTRH、ISERC/JOOTRH/754/23) 机构审查委员会的批准(RUMC,#23060502-IRB1)、马尼托巴大学(UM,HS26665)以及南伊利诺伊大学(041540)和西北大学(STU00224946)的非人类受试者确定没有人类受试者接触并且仅接收去识别化数据。摘要 背景 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 是肛门生殖器癌症的主要原因,对男男性行为者 (MSM) 的影响尤为严重,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。撒哈拉以南非洲地区很少有研究同时检查阴茎和直肠 hrHPV 感染及其特定部位的行为和生物学相关性。我们评估了肯尼亚基苏木 MSM 中与阴茎和直肠 hrHPV 相关的 患病率、基因型分布和行为因素。使用 ScreenFire HPV 检测对临床医生收集的阴茎和直肠拭子进行 hrHPV 检测。使用 GeneXpert 对尿液和直肠标本进行了沙眼衣原体和淋病奈瑟菌检测,并且...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 是肛门生殖器癌症的主要原因,对男男性行为者 (MSM) 的影响尤为严重,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。撒哈拉以南非洲地区很少有研究同时检查阴茎和直肠 hrHPV 感染及其特定部位的行为和生物学相关性。
设计与对象
我们对肯尼亚基苏木 18-35 岁 MSM 的基线数据进行了横断面分析。使用 ScreenFire HPV 检测对临床医生收集的阴茎和直肠拭子进行 hrHPV 检测。
核心方法
使用 GeneXpert 对尿液和直肠标本进行沙眼衣原体和淋病奈瑟菌检测,并根据肯尼亚国家指南确认艾滋病毒状况。使用对数二项式回归来估计调整后的 患病率 比率 (aPR),以了解与社会人口统计学、行为和生物因素的关联。
主要结果
阴茎 hrHPV 患病率 为 24.7% (39/158),直肠 hrHPV 患病率 为 12.5% (22/176);在两个站点进行测试的参与者中有 6.7% 患有双站点感染。HPV-18/45 是两个地点最常见的基因型组。阴茎 hrHPV 在 HIV 感染者中更常见(aPR = 2.27,p = 0.050),而在就业参与者中则不太常见(aPR = 0.25,p = 0.048)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,hrHPV 感染在肯尼亚基苏木的 MSM 中很常见,并且具有明显的位点特异性相关性。直肠细菌性 STI 与直肠 hrHPV 之间的密切关联凸显了 HPV 和 STI 综合筛查的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持扩大性别中性 HPV 疫苗接种 和病因性 STI 筛查,以降低撒哈拉以南非洲 MSM 中 HPV 相关癌症风险。综合筛查可以改善这一高危人群中 HPV 相关疾病的早期发现和预防。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了因果推断。由于样本量和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等,但研究结果与生物学合理性和先前的研究一致。

Prevalence and behavioral factors associated with penile and rectal HPV infection among men who have sex with men in Kenya

Authors: Kate Klein, Fredrick O. Otieno, Walter Agingu, Patriciah Wambua, Felix Ochieng, Minjee Lee, Lyle R. McKinnon, Lifang Hou, Supriya D. Mehta
Original abstract (English)
Methods We conducted a cross-sectional analysis of baseline data from MSM aged 18–35 years. Results Penile hrHPV prevalence was 24.7% (39/158) and rectal hrHPV prevalence was 12.5% (22/176); 6.7% of participants tested at both sites had dual-site infection. These findings support expansion of gender-neutral HPV vaccination and etiologic STI screening to reduce HPV-associated cancer risk among MSM in sub-Saharan Africa. Funding This study was supported by grant number R01-AI177005 (Contact MPI: Mehta) from the National Institutes of Health, National Institutes of Allergy and Infectious Diseases, and by the catalyzer funding to Dr. This study was approved by the institutional review boards of Jaramogi Odinga Oginga Teaching and Referral Hospital (JOOTRH, ISERC/JOOTRH/754/23), Rush University Medical Center (RUMC, #23060502-IRB1), University of Manitoba (UM, HS26665), and non-human subjects determination from Southern Illinois University (041540) and Northwestern University (STU00224946) for having no human subjects contact and only receiving de-identified data. Abstract Background High-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) is a leading cause of anogenital cancers and disproportionately affects men who have sex with men (MSM), particularly in low- and middle-income countries. Few studies in sub-Saharan Africa have concurrently examined penile and rectal hrHPV infection and their site-specific behavioral and biological correlates. We assessed the prevalence, genotype distribution, and behavioral factors associated with penile and rectal hrHPV among MSM in Kisumu, Kenya. Clinician-collected penile and rectal swabs were tested for hrHPV using the ScreenFire HPV assay. Urine and rectal specimens were tested for Chlamydia trachomatis and Neisseria gonorrhoeae using GeneXpert, and...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) is a leading cause of anogenital cancers and disproportionately affects men who have sex with men (MSM), particularly in low- and middle-income countries. Few studies in sub-Saharan Africa have concurrently examined penile and rectal hrHPV infection and their site-specific behavioral and biological correlates.
Design and population
We conducted a cross-sectional analysis of baseline data from MSM aged 18–35 years in Kisumu, Kenya. Clinician-collected penile and rectal swabs were tested for hrHPV using the ScreenFire HPV assay.
Core methods
Urine and rectal specimens were tested for Chlamydia trachomatis and Neisseria gonorrhoeae using GeneXpert, and HIV status was confirmed as per Kenyan national guidelines. Log-binomial regression was used to estimate adjusted prevalence ratios (aPR) for associations with sociodemographic, behavioral, and biological factors.
Main results
Penile hrHPV prevalence was 24.7% (39/158) and rectal hrHPV prevalence was 12.5% (22/176); 6.7% of participants tested at both sites had dual-site infection. HPV-18/45 was the most common genotype group at both sites. Penile hrHPV was more likely among participants with HIV (aPR = 2.27, p = 0.050) and less common among employed participants (aPR = 0.25, p = 0.048).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that hrHPV infection was common among MSM in Kisumu, Kenya, with distinct site-specific correlates. The strong association between rectal bacterial STIs and rectal hrHPV highlights the need for integrated HPV and STI screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support expansion of gender-neutral HPV vaccination and etiologic STI screening to reduce HPV-associated cancer risk among MSM in sub-Saharan Africa. Integrated screening could improve early detection and prevention of HPV-related diseases in this high-risk population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was cross-sectional, limiting causal inference. The evidence strength is moderate due to the sample size and potential confounding factors, but the findings are consistent with biological plausibility and prior research.

B 常规重要学术文献 · 研究B routine priorityAcademic literature · Research

尼日利亚东北部社区的高风险宫颈 人乳头瘤病毒 患病率:描述性研究。

High-Risk Cervical Human Papillomavirus Prevalence in a Northeast Nigeria Community: A Descriptive Study.
作者： Uchenna Simon Ezenkwa, Dauda A. Katagum, Sunday Enemona Achanya, Mairo Usman Kadaura, Babagana Bako, Musa A. Garbati, B M Audu
摘要中文翻译背景:宫颈癌筛查的不平等导致人群中的宫颈癌负担不均衡。本研究评估了尼日利亚东北部一个农村社区(该地区宫颈癌筛查服务可及性低)的人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染患病率。方法:在尼日利亚包奇州Azare的一家基层医疗设施开展社区健康外展活动。愿意并同意接受妇科咨询的女性被非随机纳入研究,并提供其社会人口学数据和宫颈标本(巴氏涂片和细胞刷脱落物置于病毒运输培养基中)用于研究。这些标本通过细胞学分析检测HPV脱氧核糖核酸(DNA)的存在情况。结果:在62名接受筛查的女性中(平均年龄38.5±9.8岁),34人(54.8%)HPV DNA检测呈阳性(95%置信区间=43-67%)。在这些阳性病例中,19人无上皮内病变或恶性病变(NILM),8人被诊断为意义不明的非典型鳞状细胞(ASCUS),6人显示低级别鳞状上皮内病变(LSIL)。相比之下,1例为高级别SIL(HSIL)。观察到30例单一感染,其中HPV 16最常见(18例),其次是HPV 18(9例),而非16、非18型别(型别31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66、67、68)统称为"其他"型别,占3例。不同型别类别(16、18和其他型别)之间发生共感染的有4例。大多数女性具有小学或更高学历(43人;69.4%),已婚(53人;85.5%),且为经产妇(52人;83.9%)。然而,大多数女性从未听说过宫颈癌(35人;56.5%),而60人(96.8%)从未接受过筛查。结论:结果表明,Azare地区宫颈高危型HPV和鳞状上皮内病变的患病率较高。加强公众健康教育并提高宫颈癌筛查服务的可及性,可能有助于降低人群的HPV感染率和随后的宫颈癌发病率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈筛查的差异导致人群的 宫颈癌 负担不成比例。本研究评估了尼日利亚东北部农村社区宫颈 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染 患病率 的情况,宫颈筛查的普及率较低。
设计与对象
在尼日利亚包奇州阿扎雷的一家初级医疗机构开展了社区健康外展活动。希望并同意接受妇科咨询的女性是非随机登记的,并提供了她们的社会人口统计数据和宫颈标本。
核心方法
对这些样本进行细胞学分析并分析是否存在 HPV 脱氧核糖核酸 (DNA)。
主要结果
在 62 名接受筛查的女性中(平均年龄 38.5 ± 9.8 岁),34 名 (54.8%) HPV DNA 检测呈阳性(95% 置信区间 = 43-67%)。在这些阳性病例中,19例没有上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤(NILM),8例被指定为意义未明的非典型鳞状细胞(ASCUS),6例显示低度鳞状上皮内病变(LSIL)。相比之下,1 例为高级别 SIL (HSIL)。
解释与创新
结果表明,Azare 中宫颈 HR-HPV 和 SIL 的 患病率 较高。提高公众意识和获得 宫颈癌 筛查服务可能有助于减少人群中 HPV 的 传播 和随后的 宫颈癌。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,迫切需要在尼日利亚阿扎雷等服务欠缺的社区加强 传播 筛查和公共卫生教育,以减少 HPV 传播 和相关的 宫颈癌 风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到非随机入组和小样本的限制,这可能会影响普遍性。由于描述性且缺乏对照组,证据强度中等。

High-Risk Cervical Human Papillomavirus Prevalence in a Northeast Nigeria Community: A Descriptive Study.

Authors: Uchenna Simon Ezenkwa, Dauda A. Katagum, Sunday Enemona Achanya, Mairo Usman Kadaura, Babagana Bako, Musa A. Garbati, B M Audu
Original abstract (English)
Background: Disparities in cervical screening result in disproportionate cervical cancer burdens in populations. This study assessed cervical human papillomavirus (HPV) infection prevalence in a rural northeast Nigeria community with low access to cervical screening. Methodology: A community health outreach was conducted at a primary healthcare facility in Azare, Bauchi State, Nigeria. The women who wished to and consented to undergo gynaecological consultations were non-randomly enrolled on the study and provided their sociodemographic data and cervical specimens (Pap smear and cytobrush exfoliates in viral transport media) for the study. These were analysed cytologically and for the presence of HPV deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA). Results: Of 62 women screened (mean age 38.5 ± 9.8 years), 34 (54.8%) tested positive for HPV DNA (95% confidence interval = 43-67%). Among these positive cases, 19 had no intraepithelial lesion or malignancy (NILM), 8 were designated atypical squamous cell of undetermined significance (ASCUS), and 6 showed low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL). In contrast, one case was high-grade SIL (HSIL). Thirty single infections were seen, HPV 16 being the commonest (18 cases), followed by HPV 18 (9 cases), while non-16, non-18 genotypes (genotypes 31, 33, 35, 39, 45, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, 67, 68), collectively termed "Others", accounted for 3 cases. Coinfections among different genotype classes (genotypes 16, 18 and Others) occurred in 4 additional cases. Most of the women had a primary education or higher (43; 69.4%), were married (53; 85.5%), and were multiparous (52; 83.9%). However, the majority had not heard about cervical cancer (35; 56.5%), while 60 (96.8%) women had never been screened previously. Conclusions: The results suggest a high prevalence of cervical HR-HPV and SIL in Azare. Improved public enlightenment and access to cervical cancer screening services may help to decrease transmission of HPV and subsequent cervical cancer in the population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Disparities in cervical screening result in disproportionate cervical cancer burdens in populations. This study assessed cervical human papillomavirus (HPV) infection prevalence in a rural northeast Nigeria community with low access to cervical screening.
Design and population
A community health outreach was conducted at a primary healthcare facility in Azare, Bauchi State, Nigeria. The women who wished to and consented to undergo gynaecological consultations were non-randomly enrolled on the study and provided their sociodemographic data and cervical specimens.
Core methods
These specimens were analysed cytologically and for the presence of HPV deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA).
Main results
Of 62 women screened (mean age 38.5 ± 9.8 years), 34 (54.8%) tested positive for HPV DNA (95% confidence interval = 43-67%). Among these positive cases, 19 had no intraepithelial lesion or malignancy (NILM), 8 were designated atypical squamous cell of undetermined significance (ASCUS), and 6 showed low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL). In contrast, one case was high-grade SIL (HSIL).
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest a high prevalence of cervical HR-HPV and SIL in Azare. Improved public enlightenment and access to cervical cancer screening services may help to decrease transmission of HPV and subsequent cervical cancer in the population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the urgent need for increased cervical cancer screening and public health education in underserved communities like Azare, Nigeria, to reduce HPV transmission and associated cervical cancer risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its non-random enrollment and small sample size, which may affect generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the descriptive nature and lack of a control group.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

未接种中国医学院校学生 HPV 疫苗接种 意向及其与 疫苗 犹豫和健康信念的关联:一项横断面研究

HPV vaccination intention and its association with vaccine hesitancy and health beliefs among unvaccinated Chinese medical college students: A cross-sectional study
作者： Ying-Ai Cui, Yuan Li, Liping Yang, Zheng-hui Cui
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染构成了重大的公共卫生挑战,而中国大学生中 HPV 疫苗接种 的吸收率仍然不理想。因此,有必要对HPV 疫苗接种意向的心理和认知机制进行持续、深入的研究。本研究旨在探讨中国医学院校学生HPV 疫苗接种意向、疫苗犹豫与健康信念之间的关联。方法:于2024年6月16日至7月16日进行横断面调查,评估广东医科大学大学生的社会人口学特征、疫苗犹豫度和健康信念模型(HBM)。通过描述性统计、单变量分析和二元逻辑回归来确定与未接种疫苗的学生中 HPV 疫苗接种 意图相关的因素。结果:在 3,584 名未接种疫苗的参与者中,73.16% 表示愿意接受 HPV 疫苗。确定的关键显着关联包括性别(男性)(OR:0.26;95% CI:0.22 ~ 0.31;p < 0.001)、父母职业(企业、商业和服务业员工(包括个体经营者))(OR:1.43;95% CI:1.18 ~ 1.75;p < 0.001)和信息来源(医院或医院)。医生)(OR:1.37;95% CI:1.15 ~ 1.64;p = 0.001)。疫苗接种 意图还与 疫苗 犹豫的感知必要性(OR:0.91;95% CI:0.88 ~ 0.94;p < 0.001)和重要性(OR:1.07;95% CI:1.03 ~ 1.10;p < 0.001)以及行为对 疫苗 的感知益处直接相关。HBM(OR:1.21;95% CI:1.12 ~ 1.32;p < 0.001)。结论:在本样本中,中国医学院校学生表现出较高的接受 HPV 疫苗接种 的意愿。意愿与 疫苗接种 的必要性、重要性和好处呈正相关。将临床医生提供的结构化咨询纳入课程,使用父母职业数据进行有针对性的补贴,以及嵌入课堂课程以比较个人 HPV 风险与 疫苗 安全数据,可能会提高采用率。还建议政府对性别中立的 疫苗接种 政策进行评估并提供包容男性的校园服务。
