Implementation and impact of primary HPV testing in cervical cancer screening: Historical perspectives and institutional experience at a large academic medical center

Authors: Rebecca A. Yoda, Eric C. Huang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Recognition of high‐risk human papillomavirus (HPV) as the etiologic agent in nearly all cervical cancers has fundamentally reshaped screening strategies, driving the development and adoption of highly sensitive molecular HPV assays. Over the past several decades, cervical cancer screening guidelines have shifted from primarily cytology‐based approaches toward HPV‐based modalities, with primary HPV testing now recommended as the preferred method by the American Cancer Society and anticipated to be similarly endorsed by the United States Preventive Services Task Force. This article traces the evolution of cervical cancer screening, from the original Papanicolaou test to current guideline recommendations, and discusses the logistical requirements for transitioning to a primary HPV screening system, including triage strategies for HPV‐positive cases. The authors additionally present an institutional experience of initiating primary HPV testing, highlighting its impact on cytopathology laboratory workflow and specimen volumes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on human papillomavirus (HPV) as the etiologic agent in cervical cancer, examining the evolution of screening strategies from cytology-based approaches to primary HPV testing, with emphasis on logistical requirements and triage strategies for HPV-positive cases.
Evidence base and method
The review is a historical and institutional experience article, not a systematic review or meta-analysis. It traces the evolution of cervical cancer screening guidelines and presents an institutional case study of implementing primary HPV testing.
Consensus and conclusions
Primary HPV testing is now the preferred screening method recommended by the American Cancer Society and anticipated to be endorsed by the United States Preventive Services Task Force, due to its high sensitivity in detecting high-risk HPV.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not explicitly discuss controversies or evidence gaps, focusing instead on the transition to primary HPV testing and its institutional impact.
Research and practice implications
The implications include the need for logistical adjustments in cytopathology laboratories to accommodate increased specimen volumes and the development of triage strategies for HPV-positive cases.

日本女性中与 HPV 疫苗接种 相关的因素:使用 7 C 疫苗接种 准备模型的横断面研究

Factors Associated with HPV Vaccination Among Japanese Women: A Cross-Sectional Study Using the 7 C Vaccination Readiness Model
作者： Nobuyuki Wakui, Miyu Hirata, Yuna Hirota, Hazuki Ogami, Karen Iwasaki, Saaya Tsuruoka, Asuka Hayashi, Manaka Kawakami, Asuka Ozaki, Mayumi Kikuchi
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究旨在使用 7 C 疫苗接种 准备模型检查日本女性中与 HPV 疫苗接种 相关的因素。重点是了解知识、心理和社会因素对 疫苗接种 行为的影响。
证据基础与方法
2025 年 2 月,对 15-49 岁的日本女性进行了一项横断面在线调查。收集了有关人口特征、HPV 疫苗接种 状态、自我报告的 HPV 疫苗 知识以及 7 C 疫苗接种 准备情况的数据。使用逻辑回归分析与 HPV 疫苗接种 相关的因素,并按学生身份(学生与学生)进行单独分析。
共识与结论
Multivariable analysis showed that younger age, non-student status, greater self-reported knowledge, and higher Constraints and Compliance scores were independently associated with HPV vaccination. Associations were observed for self-reported knowledge (OR 2.12, 95% CI 1.88-2.38, p < 0.001), Constraints (OR 1.49, 95% CI 1.27-1.76, p < 0.001), and Compliance (OR 1.32, 95% CI 1.15-1.51, p < 0.001). In analyses stratified by student status, greater self-reported knowledge and higher Constraints and Compliance scores were significantly associated with vaccination in both groups.
争议与缺口
该研究没有报告7C模型在预测疫苗接种行为方面的具体机制或详细局限性。它还没有解决潜在的混杂因素,也没有提供公共卫生政策对 疫苗接种 率影响的全面分析。
科研与实践启示
这些发现表明 HPV 疫苗 教育可能包括 疫苗 相关信息以及有关 疫苗接种 网站、费用、资格和预约程序的实用指南。它还可能包括讨论 疫苗接种 相关问题的机会,以解决约束中反映的障碍,以及对旨在促进 疫苗接种 高覆盖率的公共卫生措施的平衡解释,以解决合规性所捕获的态度。

Factors Associated with HPV Vaccination Among Japanese Women: A Cross-Sectional Study Using the 7 C Vaccination Readiness Model

Authors: Nobuyuki Wakui, Miyu Hirata, Yuna Hirota, Hazuki Ogami, Karen Iwasaki, Saaya Tsuruoka, Asuka Hayashi, Manaka Kawakami, Asuka Ozaki, Mayumi Kikuchi
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination rates in Japan declined after the suspension of the government's proactive recommendation in 2013. Vaccination behavior is influenced by knowledge as well as psychological and social factors. This study examined factors associated with HPV vaccination among Japanese women using the 7 C vaccination readiness model. A cross-sectional online survey was conducted among Japanese women aged 15-49 years in February 2025. Data on demographic characteristics, HPV vaccination status, self-reported knowledge of the HPV vaccine, and 7 C vaccination readiness were collected. Factors associated with HPV vaccination were analyzed using logistic regression, with separate analyses by student status (students vs. Among 1,200 participants, 339 (28.3%) had received the HPV vaccine. Multivariable analysis showed that younger age, non-student status, greater self-reported knowledge, and higher Constraints and Compliance scores were independently associated with vaccination. Associations were observed for self-reported knowledge (OR 2.12, 95% CI 1.88-2.38, p < 0.001), Constraints (OR 1.49, 95% CI 1.27-1.76, p < 0.001), and Compliance (OR 1.32, 95% CI 1.15-1.51, p < 0.001). In analyses stratified by student status, greater self-reported knowledge and higher Constraints and Compliance scores were significantly associated with vaccination in both groups. These findings suggest that HPV vaccine education may include vaccine-related information and practical guidance on vaccination sites, costs, eligibility, and appointment procedures. It may also include opportunities to discuss vaccination-related concerns to address barriers reflected in Constraints, as well as balanced explanations of public-health measures intended to promote high vaccination coverage to address attitudes captured by Compliance.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study aimed to examine factors associated with HPV vaccination among Japanese women using the 7 C vaccination readiness model. The focus was on understanding the influence of knowledge, psychological, and social factors on vaccination behavior.
Evidence base and method
A cross-sectional online survey was conducted among Japanese women aged 15-49 years in February 2025. Data on demographic characteristics, HPV vaccination status, self-reported knowledge of the HPV vaccine, and 7 C vaccination readiness were collected. Factors associated with HPV vaccination were analyzed using logistic regression, with separate analyses by student status (students vs.
Consensus and conclusions
Multivariable analysis showed that younger age, non-student status, greater self-reported knowledge, and higher Constraints and Compliance scores were independently associated with HPV vaccination. Associations were observed for self-reported knowledge (OR 2.12, 95% CI 1.88-2.38, p < 0.001), Constraints (OR 1.49, 95% CI 1.27-1.76, p < 0.001), and Compliance (OR 1.32, 95% CI 1.15-1.51, p < 0.001). In analyses stratified by student status, greater self-reported knowledge and higher Constraints and Compliance scores were significantly associated with vaccination in both groups.
Controversies and gaps
The study did not report on the specific mechanisms or detailed limitations of the 7 C model in predicting vaccination behavior. It also did not address potential confounding factors or provide a comprehensive analysis of the impact of public health policies on vaccination rates.
Research and practice implications
These findings suggest that HPV vaccine education may include vaccine-related information and practical guidance on vaccination sites, costs, eligibility, and appointment procedures. It may also include opportunities to discuss vaccination-related concerns to address barriers reflected in Constraints, as well as balanced explanations of public-health measures intended to promote high vaccination coverage to address attitudes captured by Compliance.

口腔鳞状细胞癌中的口腔微生物群:揭示机制和临床潜力

The oral microbiota in oral squamous cell carcinoma: unravelling mechanisms and clinical potential
作者： Amany Hany Mohamed Kamel, Nourhan El-Tantawy, Sarah Ahmed, Al-Hassan Soliman Wadan, Nermeen AbuBakr
摘要中文翻译口腔鳞状细胞癌起源于口腔粘膜,是头颈部最常见的恶性肿瘤。其发病机制是多因素的,涉及环境暴露、遗传易感性和生活方式相关的危险因素。越来越多的证据表明口腔微生物失调有助于口腔鳞癌的发生和进展。在健康条件下,口腔拥有多样化且功能平衡的微生物生态系统,可维持粘膜完整性,支持免疫稳态,并防止病原菌定植。这种平衡的破坏被称为口腔生态失调,越来越多地被认为是口腔癌发生的关键事件。在口腔鳞癌中,一直观察到向致病性和促炎性微生物群落的转变,特别是涉及牙周细菌,如牙龈卟啉单胞菌、齿垢密螺旋体和具核梭杆菌。这些生物体通过激活炎症和致癌信号通路(包括 NF-κB、STAT3 和 PI3K/Akt)来促进肿瘤进展;抑制细胞凋亡;诱导上皮-间质转化;和免疫逃避,从而创造一个促进肿瘤的微环境。除了细菌失调外,口腔微生物组的病毒和真菌成分可能是口腔鳞癌的重要辅助因子。高风险 Epstein-Barr 病毒 (EBV) 和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与破坏肿瘤抑制途径、导致基因组不稳定和调节 免疫应答 有关。真菌物种,特别是白色念珠菌,可能进一步产生致癌代谢物并诱发慢性炎症。本综述对口腔鳞癌的口腔微生物群进行了综合概述,重点关注核心微生物群的组成和保护作用、影响微生物稳定性的因素以及生态失调导致癌变的机制。它还强调口腔微生物组作为非侵入性生物标志物的潜在来源,并讨论了针对微生物组的策略,包括益生元、益生菌和后生元,作为恢复微生物平衡和减少促肿瘤炎症的有前途的辅助方法。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注口腔鳞状细胞癌 (OSCC) 中的口腔微生物群,探讨其在致癌机制和临床潜力中的作用。核心问题涉及了解微生物失调如何导致口腔鳞癌的发生和进展。
证据基础与方法
该综述对 OSCC 中的口腔微生物组进行了综合概述,重点关注其组成、保护作用、稳定性因素和致癌机制。它还讨论了作为潜在辅助方法的微生物组靶向策略。
共识与结论
人们一致认为口腔微生物失调会导致口腔鳞癌的发生和进展。牙龈卟啉单胞菌和齿垢密螺旋体等致病菌可激活致癌途径,而 高危型HPV 和白色念珠菌则充当辅助因子。口腔微生物组被认为是非侵入性生物标志物的来源。
争议与缺口
该综述没有明确提及争议或证据差距,但强调需要进一步研究微生物组靶向策略以及不同微生物成分促进致癌的具体机制。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议以微生物组为目标的策略,包括益生元、益生菌和后生元,作为恢复微生物平衡和减少促肿瘤炎症的有前途的辅助方法。它还强调了口腔微生物组作为非侵入性生物标志物来源的潜力。

The oral microbiota in oral squamous cell carcinoma: unravelling mechanisms and clinical potential

Authors: Amany Hany Mohamed Kamel, Nourhan El-Tantawy, Sarah Ahmed, Al-Hassan Soliman Wadan, Nermeen AbuBakr
Original abstract (English)
Originating in the mucosal lining of the mouth, oral squamous cell carcinoma is the most common malignancy of the head and neck regions. Its pathogenesis is multifactorial, involving environmental exposures, genetic susceptibility, and lifestyle-related risk factors. Increasing evidence indicates that oral microbial dysbiosis contributes to the initiation and progression of OSCC. Under healthy conditions, the oral cavity harbors a diverse and functionally balanced microbial ecosystem that maintains mucosal integrity, supports immune homeostasis, and prevents colonization by pathogenic species. Disruption of this equilibrium, known as oral dysbiosis, is increasingly recognized as a key event in oral carcinogenesis. In OSCC, a shift toward pathogenic and pro-inflammatory microbial communities has been consistently observed, particularly involving periodontal bacteria such as Porphyromonas gingivalis, Treponema denticola, and Fusobacterium nucleatum. These organisms contribute to tumor progression by activating inflammatory and oncogenic signaling pathways, including NF-κB, STAT3, and PI3K/Akt; suppressing apoptosis; inducing epithelial–mesenchymal transition; and immune evasion, thereby creating a tumor-promoting microenvironment. In addition to bacterial dysbiosis, viral and fungal components of the oral microbiome may act as important cofactors in OSCC. High-risk Epstein–Barr virus (EBV) and human papillomavirus (HPV) have been implicated in disrupting tumor suppressor pathways, causing genomic instability, and modulating the immune response. Fungal species, particularly Candida albicans, may further contribute by producing carcinogenic metabolites and inducing chronic inflammation. This review provides an integrated overview of the oral microbiome in OSCC, focusing on the composition and protective roles of the core microbiota, factors influencing microbial stability, and mechanisms by which dysbiosis contributes to carcinogenesis. It also highlights the oral microbiome as a potential source of non-invasive biomarkers and discusses microbiome-targeted strategies, including prebiotics, probiotics, and postbiotics, as promising adjunctive approaches to restore microbial balance and reduce tumor-promoting inflammation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the oral microbiota in oral squamous cell carcinoma (OSCC), examining its role in carcinogenesis mechanisms and clinical potential. The central question involves understanding how microbial dysbiosis contributes to OSCC initiation and progression.
Evidence base and method
The review provides an integrated overview of the oral microbiome in OSCC, focusing on composition, protective roles, stability factors, and carcinogenesis mechanisms. It also discusses microbiome-targeted strategies as potential adjunctive approaches.
Consensus and conclusions
There is consensus that oral microbial dysbiosis contributes to OSCC initiation and progression. Pathogenic bacteria like Porphyromonas gingivalis and Treponema denticola activate oncogenic pathways, while high-risk HPV and Candida albicans act as cofactors. The oral microbiome is recognized as a source of non-invasive biomarkers.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not explicitly mention controversies or evidence gaps, but it highlights the need for further research on microbiome-targeted strategies and the specific mechanisms by which different microbial components contribute to carcinogenesis.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests microbiome-targeted strategies, including prebiotics, probiotics, and postbiotics, as promising adjunctive approaches to restore microbial balance and reduce tumor-promoting inflammation. It also emphasizes the potential of the oral microbiome as a source of non-invasive biomarkers.

评估卢旺达使用 SMS 招募女性进行 宫颈癌 筛查的可行性:一项试点研究。

Evaluating the feasibility of using SMS to recruit women for cervical cancer screening in Rwanda: A pilot study.
作者： Grace McCloskey, Candice Ruck, Amy Booth, Barbra Mutamba, Marc Hagenimana, Alice Nyamwiza, Francois Uwinkindi, Laurie Smith, Stephen Rulisa, Gina S Ogilvie
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究评估了在卢旺达基加利市区使用短信服务 (SMS) 作为 宫颈癌 筛查招募策略的可行性和可接受性。方法:该试点项目邀请了 300 名在当地活动期间未参加子宫颈普查且能够使用手机的女性提供 宫颈癌 筛查邀请的手机号码。最多发出了三条短信,邀请女性前往当地卫生中心进行 宫颈癌 筛查。在那里,记录了出勤率,采集了自我采集的阴道样本用于 人乳头瘤病毒 测试,并进行了一项简短的调查。那些没有出席的人会接到电话并接受调查,了解缺席的原因。使用描述性分析来详细说明出勤率并评估有关干预措施的可行性和可接受性的调查问题。结果:共有 294 名女性符合纳入标准。在这些女性中,86.4% (254/294) 前往健康中心进行 宫颈癌 筛查,92.9% (236/254) 的参与者表示由于短信提醒而参与了筛查。评估可接受性和可行性的调查项目表明,消息理解程度高,单个短信提示后的出席率很高,并且高度同意短信是参加筛选的有用提醒。大多数未出席者表示已收到短信,但由于环境因素而没有出席。结论:在卢旺达基加利城市地区的这项试点研究中,短信招聘是高度可行的,并且得到了广泛接受。未来的研究应旨在了解该策略与卢旺达不同环境中通常的招募方法相比的有效性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究评估了在卢旺达基加利市区使用短信服务 (SMS) 作为 宫颈癌 筛查招募策略的可行性和可接受性。
证据基础与方法
该试点项目邀请了 300 名在当地活动期间未参加宫颈筛查且能够使用手机的女性提供手机号码以进行 宫颈癌 筛查邀请。最多发出了三条短信,邀请女性前往当地卫生中心进行 宫颈癌 筛查。在那里,记录了出勤率,采集了自我采集的阴道样本用于 人乳头瘤病毒 测试,并进行了一项简短的调查。那些没有出席的人会接到电话并接受调查,了解缺席的原因。使用描述性分析来详细说明出勤率并评估有关干预措施的可行性和可接受性的调查问题。
共识与结论
共有 294 名女性符合纳入标准。在这些女性中,86.4% (254/294) 前往健康中心进行 宫颈癌 筛查,92.9% (236/254) 的参与者表示由于短信提醒而参与了筛查。评估可接受性和可行性的调查项目表明,消息理解程度高,单个短信提示后的出席率很高,并且高度同意短信是参加筛选的有用提醒。
争议与缺口
大多数未出席者表示已收到短信,但由于环境因素而没有出席。未来的研究应旨在了解该策略与卢旺达不同环境中通常的招募方法相比的有效性。
科研与实践启示
在卢旺达基加利城市地区的这项试点研究中,短信招聘非常可行并得到广泛接受。

Evaluating the feasibility of using SMS to recruit women for cervical cancer screening in Rwanda: A pilot study.

Authors: Grace McCloskey, Candice Ruck, Amy Booth, Barbra Mutamba, Marc Hagenimana, Alice Nyamwiza, Francois Uwinkindi, Laurie Smith, Stephen Rulisa, Gina S Ogilvie
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: This study evaluates the feasibility and acceptability of using short message service (SMS) as a recruitment strategy for cervical cancer screening in an urban sector of Kigali, Rwanda. METHODS: This pilot invited 300 women who had not attended cervical screening during the local campaign and had access to a mobile phone to provide a mobile number for cervical cancer screening invitation. Up to three SMSs were sent out, inviting women to visit a local health center for cervical cancer screening. There, attendance rates were recorded, self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus testing were taken, and a short survey was administered. Those who did not attend received a phone call and were surveyed on reasons for non-attendance. A descriptive analysis was used to detail attendance rates and evaluate survey questions on the perceived feasibility and acceptability of the intervention. RESULTS: A total of 294 women met the inclusion criteria. Of these women, 86.4% (254/294) attended the health center for cervical cancer screening and 92.9% (236/254) of attendees reported they participated due to the SMS reminder. Survey items evaluating acceptability and feasibility indicated high message comprehension, strong attendance following a single SMS prompt, and high agreement that SMS was a helpful reminder to attend screening. Most non-attendees reported having received the SMSs but not attending due to circumstantial factors. CONCLUSION: SMS recruitment was highly feasible and well accepted in this pilot study in an urban sector of Kigali, Rwanda. Future research should aim to understand the efficacy of this strategy compared to usual recruitment methods across diverse settings in Rwanda.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study evaluates the feasibility and acceptability of using short message service (SMS) as a recruitment strategy for cervical cancer screening in an urban sector of Kigali, Rwanda.
Evidence base and method
This pilot invited 300 women who had not attended cervical screening during the local campaign and had access to a mobile phone to provide a mobile number for cervical cancer screening invitation. Up to three SMSs were sent out, inviting women to visit a local health center for cervical cancer screening. There, attendance rates were recorded, self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus testing were taken, and a short survey was administered. Those who did not attend received a phone call and were surveyed on reasons for non-attendance. A descriptive analysis was used to detail attendance rates and evaluate survey questions on the perceived feasibility and acceptability of the intervention.
Consensus and conclusions
A total of 294 women met the inclusion criteria. Of these women, 86.4% (254/294) attended the health center for cervical cancer screening and 92.9% (236/254) of attendees reported they participated due to the SMS reminder. Survey items evaluating acceptability and feasibility indicated high message comprehension, strong attendance following a single SMS prompt, and high agreement that SMS was a helpful reminder to attend screening.
Controversies and gaps
Most non-attendees reported having received the SMSs but not attending due to circumstantial factors. Future research should aim to understand the efficacy of this strategy compared to usual recruitment methods across diverse settings in Rwanda.
Research and practice implications
SMS recruitment was highly feasible and well accepted in this pilot study in an urban sector of Kigali, Rwanda.

致癌病毒及其在口腔和口咽鳞状细胞癌中的作用:系统评价

Oncogenic viruses and their role in oral cavity and oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma: a systematic review
作者： Juan Manuel Quiroga-Garza, Maria Fernanda Herrera-Saldivar, Norma Carolina Hernandez-Bautista, Alondra Yamileth Alanis-Valdez, Aldo Sebastián Flores-Flores, José Manuel Vázquez-Guillén, Diana Reséndez-Pérez, Reyes S. Tamez-Guerra, Cristina Rodrı́guez-Padilla
摘要中文翻译口腔 (OC) 和口咽 (OP) 鳞状细胞癌 (SCC) 是全球主要的健康问题,占头颈部恶性肿瘤的大部分。这些肿瘤起源于 OC 和口咽部的上皮层,其特点是侵袭性局部侵袭、淋巴结转移和高发病率和 死亡率。除了烟草和饮酒等已知危险因素外,多种致癌病毒,包括 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)、Epstein-Barr 病毒 (EBV)、人类疱疹病毒 (HHV) 1 和 2、巨细胞病毒 (CMV) 和默克尔细胞多瘤病毒 (MCPyV),都与发生、进展或预后有关。肿瘤组织内病毒 基因组 的检测表明,病毒致癌基因 宿主 免疫应答 与致癌过程中的环境因素之间存在复杂的相互作用。本系统综述综合了有关致癌病毒对 OC 和 OP SCC 病因学贡献的最新证据。根据 PRISMA 2020 指南,通过 PubMed、Scopus 和 Web of Science 的搜索确定了 2015 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间的观察性研究。符合条件的研究仅限于人类研究和报告病毒检测。结果表明,HPV 感染与 OP SCC 之间存在强烈、一致且临床相关的关联,而支持非 HPV 致癌病毒在 OC 和 OP SCC 中的因果或预后作用的证据仍然有限且异质。观察到病毒检测方法和流行病学报告存在显着的方法学差异,这强调了标准化评估框架的必要性。总体而言,这些结果支持对病毒肿瘤发生的位点特异性解释,并为有针对性的预防、诊断分层和治疗策略的制定提供信息。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本系统综述重点关注致癌病毒,特别是 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 在口腔 (OC) 和口咽 (OP) 鳞状细胞癌 (SCC) 病因学中的作用。核心问题是综合关于这些病毒对 OC 和 OP SCC 的发生、进展或预后的贡献的当前证据。
证据基础与方法
遵循 PRISMA 2020 指南,本次系统综述包括 2015 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月的观察性研究,这些研究是通过 PubMed、Scopus 和 Web of Science 的搜索确定的。符合条件的研究仅限于报告病毒检测的人类研究。
共识与结论
该综述表明 HPV 感染与 OP SCC 之间存在强烈、一致且具有临床相关性的关联。然而,支持非 HPV 致癌病毒在 OC 和 OP SCC 中的因果或预后作用的证据仍然有限且异质。
争议与缺口
观察到病毒检测方法和流行病学报告存在显着的方法学差异,凸显了标准化评估框架的必要性。由于证据有限且异质性,非 HPV 致癌病毒在 OC 和 OP SCC 中的作用仍未得到解决。
科研与实践启示
这些发现支持病毒肿瘤发生的位点特异性解释,并为 OC 和 OP SCC 的针对性预防、诊断分层和治疗策略的制定提供信息。

Oncogenic viruses and their role in oral cavity and oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma: a systematic review

Authors: Juan Manuel Quiroga-Garza, Maria Fernanda Herrera-Saldivar, Norma Carolina Hernandez-Bautista, Alondra Yamileth Alanis-Valdez, Aldo Sebastián Flores-Flores, José Manuel Vázquez-Guillén, Diana Reséndez-Pérez, Reyes S. Tamez-Guerra, Cristina Rodrı́guez-Padilla
Original abstract (English)
Oral cavity (OC) and oropharyngeal (OP) squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) represent a major global health concern, accounting for most of head and neck malignancies. These tumors arise from the epithelial lining of the OC and oropharynx and are characterized by aggressive local invasion, lymph node metastasis, and high morbidity and mortality. In addition to known risk factors such as tobacco and alcohol consumption, several oncogenic viruses, including human papillomavirus (HPV), Epstein-Barr virus (EBV), human herpesviruses (HHV) 1 and 2, cytomegalovirus (CMV) and Merkel cell polyomavirus (MCPyV), have been implicated in the development, progression, or prognosis. Detection of viral genomes within tumor tissues suggests a complex interplay between viral oncogenes, host immune responses, and environmental factors in carcinogenesis. This systematic review synthesizes current evidence on the contribution of oncogenic viruses to the etiology of OC and OP SCC. Following the PRISMA 2020 guidelines, observational studies between January 2015 and December 2025 were identified through searches of PubMed, Scopus and Web of Science. Eligible studies were limited to human research and reporting viral detections. The results demonstrate a strong, consistent, and clinically relevant association between HPV infection and OP SCC, while evidence supporting a causal or prognostic role for non-HPV oncogenic viruses in OC and OP SCC remains limited and heterogeneous. Substantial methodological variability in viral detection approaches and epidemiological reporting was observed, underscoring the need for standardized assessment frameworks. Overall, these results support site-specific interpretation of viral oncogenesis and inform the development of targeted prevention, diagnostic stratification, and therapeutic strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review focuses on the role of oncogenic viruses, particularly human papillomavirus (HPV), in the etiology of oral cavity (OC) and oropharyngeal (OP) squamous cell carcinoma (SCC). The central question is to synthesize current evidence on the contribution of these viruses to the development, progression, or prognosis of OC and OP SCC.
Evidence base and method
Following PRISMA 2020 guidelines, this systematic review included observational studies from January 2015 to December 2025, identified through searches of PubMed, Scopus, and Web of Science. Eligible studies were limited to human research reporting viral detections.
Consensus and conclusions
The review demonstrates a strong, consistent, and clinically relevant association between HPV infection and OP SCC. However, evidence supporting a causal or prognostic role for non-HPV oncogenic viruses in OC and OP SCC remains limited and heterogeneous.
Controversies and gaps
Substantial methodological variability in viral detection approaches and epidemiological reporting was observed, highlighting the need for standardized assessment frameworks. The role of non-HPV oncogenic viruses in OC and OP SCC remains unresolved due to limited and heterogeneous evidence.
Research and practice implications
The findings support site-specific interpretation of viral oncogenesis and inform the development of targeted prevention, diagnostic stratification, and therapeutic strategies for OC and OP SCC.

妇科肿瘤学中的免疫疗法和相关抗体药物偶联物:最新进展、持续挑战和未来方向

Immunotherapy and Relevant Antibody–Drug Conjugates in Gynecologic Oncology: Recent Advances, Ongoing Challenges, and Future Directions
作者： Ting‐Tai Yen, Tina Yi-Jin Hsieh, Eugene P. Toy
摘要中文翻译免疫检查点抑制剂和抗体药物偶联物迅速扩大了 治疗 治疗妇科恶性肿瘤的选择,尽管不同肿瘤类型和生物标志物定义的人群的获益程度差异很大。这篇叙述性综述总结了子宫内膜癌、宫颈癌和卵巢癌中免疫检查点阻断和抗体药物偶联物的生物学原理、预测性生物标志物、关键临床试验、监管批准、指南支持的策略以及新兴方向。在子宫内膜癌中,分子分类和错配修复状态已经改变了 治疗 选择,PD-1 或​​ PD-L1 阻断现已纳入一线化学免疫治疗和复发性疾病管理。HER2 导向和 TROP-2 导向的抗体药物偶联物也正在作为生物标志物导向的策略出现。在 宫颈癌 中,人乳头瘤病毒 驱动的肿瘤生物学、PD-L1 表达和组织因子表达支持在局部晚期和复发或转移环境中使用检查点抑制剂、抗体药物偶联物和治疗 疫苗 方法。在卵巢癌中,单药检查点阻断在未选择的人群中显示出有限的活性,但最近的进展包括铂类耐药疾病中生物标志物选择的化学免疫疗法,以及叶酸受体α导向和HER2导向的抗体药物偶联物的临床有意义的活性。在妇科癌症中,关键挑战包括完善预测生物标志物、优化先前免疫治疗暴露后的测序、管理重叠毒性以及设计丰富生物反应亚组的试验。未来的进展将取决于将分子分类、免疫背景、ADC 靶点表达和患者特异性临床因素整合到 治疗 选择中。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注妇科恶性肿瘤的免疫疗法和抗体药物偶联物,特别检查它们在子宫内膜癌、宫颈癌和卵巢癌中的应用。中心问题围绕着这些 治疗 在不同肿瘤类型和生物标志物定义人群中的生物学原理、预测生物标志物、临床试验结果和新兴策略。
证据基础与方法
这是一篇叙述性综述,总结了子宫内膜癌、宫颈癌和卵巢癌中免疫检查点阻断和抗体药物偶联物的生物学原理、预测性生物标志物、关键临床试验、监管批准、指南支持的策略以及新兴方向。
共识与结论
在子宫内膜癌中,分子分类和错配修复状态已经改变了 治疗 选择,PD-1 或​​ PD-L1 阻断现已纳入一线化学免疫治疗和复发性疾病管理。在 宫颈癌 中,人乳头瘤病毒 驱动的肿瘤生物学、PD-L1 表达和组织因子表达支持在局部晚期和复发或转移环境中使用检查点抑制剂、抗体药物偶联物和治疗 疫苗 方法。
争议与缺口
在妇科癌症中,关键挑战包括完善预测生物标志物、优化先前免疫治疗暴露后的测序、管理重叠毒性以及设计丰富生物反应亚组的试验。
科研与实践启示
未来的进展将取决于将分子分类、免疫背景、ADC 靶点表达和患者特异性临床因素整合到 治疗 选择中。

Immunotherapy and Relevant Antibody–Drug Conjugates in Gynecologic Oncology: Recent Advances, Ongoing Challenges, and Future Directions

Authors: Ting‐Tai Yen, Tina Yi-Jin Hsieh, Eugene P. Toy
Original abstract (English)
Immune checkpoint inhibitors and antibody–drug conjugates have rapidly expanded treatment options for gynecologic malignancies, although the magnitude of benefit varies substantially across tumor types and biomarker-defined populations. This narrative review summarizes the biologic rationale, predictive biomarkers, pivotal clinical trials, regulatory approvals, guideline-supported strategies, and emerging directions for immune checkpoint blockade and antibody–drug conjugates in endometrial, cervical, and ovarian cancers. In endometrial cancer, molecular classification and mismatch repair status have transformed treatment selection, with PD-1 or PD-L1 blockade now integrated into first-line chemoimmunotherapy and recurrent disease management. HER2-directed and TROP-2-directed antibody–drug conjugates are also emerging as biomarker-directed strategies. In cervical cancer, human papillomavirus-driven tumor biology, PD-L1 expression, and tissue factor expression support the use of checkpoint inhibitors, antibody–drug conjugates, and therapeutic vaccine approaches across locally advanced and recurrent or metastatic settings. In ovarian cancer, single-agent checkpoint blockade has shown limited activity in unselected populations, but recent advances include biomarker-selected chemoimmunotherapy in platinum-resistant disease and clinically meaningful activity of folate receptor alpha-directed and HER2-directed antibody–drug conjugates. Across gynecologic cancers, key challenges include refining predictive biomarkers, optimizing sequencing after prior immunotherapy exposure, managing overlapping toxicities, and designing trials that enrich for biologically responsive subgroups. Future progress will depend on integrating molecular classification, immune contexture, ADC target expression, and patient-specific clinical factors into treatment selection.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on immunotherapy and antibody–drug conjugates for gynecologic malignancies, specifically examining their application in endometrial, cervical, and ovarian cancers. The central question revolves around the biologic rationale, predictive biomarkers, clinical trial outcomes, and emerging strategies for these treatments across different tumor types and biomarker-defined populations.
Evidence base and method
This is a narrative review that summarizes the biologic rationale, predictive biomarkers, pivotal clinical trials, regulatory approvals, guideline-supported strategies, and emerging directions for immune checkpoint blockade and antibody–drug conjugates in endometrial, cervical, and ovarian cancers.
Consensus and conclusions
In endometrial cancer, molecular classification and mismatch repair status have transformed treatment selection, with PD-1 or PD-L1 blockade now integrated into first-line chemoimmunotherapy and recurrent disease management. In cervical cancer, human papillomavirus-driven tumor biology, PD-L1 expression, and tissue factor expression support the use of checkpoint inhibitors, antibody–drug conjugates, and therapeutic vaccine approaches across locally advanced and recurrent or metastatic settings.
Controversies and gaps
Across gynecologic cancers, key challenges include refining predictive biomarkers, optimizing sequencing after prior immunotherapy exposure, managing overlapping toxicities, and designing trials that enrich for biologically responsive subgroups.
Research and practice implications
Future progress will depend on integrating molecular classification, immune contexture, ADC target expression, and patient-specific clinical factors into treatment selection.

新闻简报该报告提供了对 HPV16 阳性癌症市场的全面见解,分析了 流行病学、当前疗法和未来发展。HPV16 仍然是最常见的致癌类型,导致全球很大一部分宫颈癌、肛门癌和头颈癌。市场正在转向免疫疗法和靶向 治疗,多个有前途的候选药物正在临床开发中。主要更新包括 VGX-3100 的积极 3 期试验结果、BNT113 的快速通道指定以及 PDS0101 预计于 2026 年获得的 II 期数据。预防性 疫苗 已经影响了癌症 发病率 的发病率,而 pembrolizumab 等已批准的疗法继续推动市场增长。该报告预测了到 2036 年的市场趋势,强调了不断变化的 治疗 格局以及 HPV16 阳性癌症管理方面未满足的需求。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-07-24。引用的事件涵盖从 2026 年 1 月到 2036 年的预测发展。
地点与对象
全球市场分析,特别关注美国、日本和其他主要市场。受影响的人群包括 HPV16 阳性癌症患者,特别是宫颈癌、头颈癌和口咽癌患者。
事件
HPV16阳性癌症的市场分析和预测报告,涵盖流行病学、当前疗法、管道开发和市场趋势。包括临床试验、监管指定和治疗方法的最新信息。
规模、影响与风险
HPV16 is the predominant oncogenic type, accounting for ≥60% of anogenital cancers and 44-91% of head and neck cancers. In the US, about 91% of anal cancer cases are attributable to HPV infection. An estimated 26 million Americans have oral HPV infection, with approximately 2,600 infected with HPV16. Japan reported nearly 3,900 incident cases of HPV16+ oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma in 2025.
应对、状态与不确定性
市场正在从传统的 治疗 转向免疫疗法和抗原靶向方法。预防性 疫苗 已减少 HPV16 相关癌症 发病率。管道包括针对 E6/E7 癌蛋白的 HPV16 特异性 治疗,有多个候选药物处于 II/III 期开发中。某些疗法已获得快速通道称号。该报告提供了 流行病学 细分和截至 2036 年的市场预测,部分数据预计在 2026 年下半年公布。

Human Papillomavirus 16-Positive Cancers Market Insights, Epidemiology, and Market Forecast 2036 | DelveInsight - openPR.com

News Brief
This report provides comprehensive insights into the HPV16-positive cancers market, analyzing epidemiology, current therapies, and future developments. HPV16 remains the most prevalent oncogenic type, causing a significant proportion of cervical, anal, and head and neck cancers globally. The market is shifting toward immunotherapies and targeted treatments, with several promising pipeline candidates in clinical development. Key updates include positive Phase 3 trial results for VGX-3100, Fast Track Designation for BNT113, and expected Phase II data for PDS0101 in 2026. Preventive vaccines have already impacted cancer incidence rates, while approved therapies like pembrolizumab continue to drive market growth. The report forecasts market trends through 2036, highlighting the evolving treatment landscape and unmet needs in HPV16-positive cancer management.

the trial met its primary efficacy endpoint with histopathologic regression of lesions and targeted viral clearance, and ApolloBio plans to pursue a regulatory submission in China. • In March 2026, Nykode announced that its randomized clinical trial (Abili-T) in 1L R/M head and neck cancer is on track to deliver meaningful interim results by 2027. • In January 2026, BNT113 received Fast Track Designation from the US FDA for the treatment of HPV16+ HNSCC expressing PD-L1. • PDS Biotechnology indicated in its 2026 corporate presentation that Phase II clinical data for PDS0101 in cervical cancer are expected to be reported in H2 2026. • HPV16 remains the predominant oncogenic type, accounting for ≥60% in anogenital cancer sites and 44-91% in head and neck cancers. • According to the CDC, about 91% of anal cancer cases in the United States are thought to be attributable to HPV infection. • An estimated 26 million Americans have an oral HPV infection at any given time, of whom approximately 2,600 are infected with HPV16. • The total incident cases of HPV16+ Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma (OPSCC) in Japan were nearly 3,900 in 2025. • The HPV16+ Cancers Companies include Merck, Bristol Myers Squibb, Merus, Nykode Therapeutics, Roche, PDS Biotechnology, BioNTech, Cue Biopharma, Transgene, and others. • Promising HPV16+ Cancers Therapies include Petosemtamab (MCLA-158), Versamune HPV (PDS0101), BNT113, Abipapogene suvaplasmid (VB10.16), CUE-101, TG4001, and others. Get a Free sample for the HPV16+ Cancers Market Forecast, Size & Share Analysis Report @ https://www.delveinsight.com/sample-request/human-papillomavirus-16-positive-hpv16-cancers-market?utm_source=openpr&utm_medium=pressrelease&utm_campaign=akpr What are HPV16-Positive Cancers? HPV16+ Cancers are virus-driven epithelial malignancies primarily caused by persistent infection with human papillomavirus type 16, one of the most oncogenic HPV strains responsible for a significant proportion of cervical cancers and head & neck squamous cell carcinomas, particularly oropharyngeal tumors. These cancers arise when viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 disrupt key tumor suppressor pathways, leading to uncontrolled cellular proliferation, genomic instability, and malignant transformation of epithelial cells. Diagnosis begins with clinical suspicion based on symptoms or screening findings, followed by confirmation through biopsy and histopathological examination, which typically reveals squamous cell carcinoma. Once malignancy is established, HPV16 status is determined using molecular and immunohistochemical methods, most commonly HPV DNA genotyping and p16 immunohistochemistry, with HPV E6/E7 mRNA testing sometimes used for more definitive evidence of active viral oncogene expression. HPV16+ Cancers Epidemiology Segmentation in the 7MM • Total Incident Cases of Selected Indications • Total Incident Cases of Selected Indications by HPV Status • Total Incident Cases of HPV16+ Cancers • Gender-specific Incident Cases of HPV16+ Cancers • Stage-specific Incident Cases of HPV16+ Cancers Download the report to understand which factors are driving HPV16+ Cancers epidemiology trends @ https://www.delveinsight.com/report-store/human-papillomavirus-16-positive-hpv16-cancers-market?utm_source=openpr&utm_medium=pressrelease&utm_campaign=akpr HPV16+ Cancers Approved Therapies • Pembrolizumab (KEYTRUDA): Merck KEYTRUDA is an anti-PD-1 therapy that increases the ability of the body's immune system to help detect and fight tumor cells. It is a humanized monoclonal antibody that blocks the interaction between PD-1 and its ligands, PD-L1 and PD-L2, thereby activating T lymphocytes, which may affect both tumor cells and healthy cells. In October 2025, Merck announced that the European Commission approved KEYTRUDA as monotherapy for the treatment of resectable locally advanced head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (LA-HNSCC) as neoadjuvant treatment. • Nivolumab (OPDIVO): Bristol Myers Squibb OPDIVO is a PD-1 inhibitor administered via IV, marketed in the US since 2016 for HNSCC. HPV16+ Cancers Pipeline Analysis • Versamune HPV (PDS0101): PDS Biotechnology PDS0101 is a novel investigational HPV-targeted immunotherapy that stimulates a potent targeted T-cell attack against HPV-positive cancers, given by simple SC injection in combination with other immunotherapies and cancer treatments. It is currently in Phase III development (VERSATILE-003) for HPV16-positive recurrent/metastatic head and neck cancer, having received FDA Fast Track Designation, and has completed a Phase II study in collaboration with the National Cancer Institute. According to the company, intellectual property rights are exclusive until 2042/2043. • BNT113: BioNTech BNT113 is an mRNA cancer vaccine candidate encoding the E6 and E7 oncoproteins frequently found in HPV16+ solid cancers, aiming to trigger a strong and precise immune response in patients with HPV16+ HNSCC. HPV16+ Cancers Companies Merck, Bristol Myers Squibb, Merus, Nykode Therapeutics, Roche, PDS Biotechnology, BioNTech, Cue Biopharma, Transgene, and others Discover more about therapies set to grab major HPV16+ Cancers market share @ https://www.delveinsight.com/sample-request/human-papillomavirus-16-positive-hpv16-cancers-market?utm_source=openpr&utm_medium=pressrelease&utm_campaign=akpr HPV16+ Cancers Market Outlook The HPV16+ Cancers market (2022-2036) is transitioning from a long-standing reliance on surgery, radiation, and platinum-based chemotherapy toward a more immunotherapy and antigen-targeted treatment paradigm. Preventive vaccination has also reshaped the landscape, with prophylactic HPV vaccines such as GARDASIL, GARDASIL 9, and CERVARIX reducing the incidence of HPV16-related cancers by preventing initial infection. The introduction of immune checkpoint inhibitors such as Pembrolizumab and Nivolumab has significantly improved survival outcomes and established immunotherapy as a cornerstone of care. Beyond approved therapies, the pipeline is rapidly expanding with HPV16-specific, mechanism-driven treatments, including Petosemtamab (Merus), PDS0101 (PDS Biotechnology), BNT113 (BioNTech), CUE-101 (Cue Biopharma), and TG4001 (Transgene), primarily focused on targeting HPV16 E6/E7 oncoproteins. Among approved therapies, Pembrolizumab is expected to remain a key revenue driver due to its broad use across cervical and head and neck cancers, while the entry of mid-to-late stage candidates such as BNT113, PDS0101, and CUE-101 is anticipated to intensify competition and accelerate the shift toward HPV16-targeted immunotherapies in the later stages of the forecast period. View five news elements
Time
Published on 2026-07-24. Events referenced span from January 2026 to forecasted developments through 2036.
Location and population
Global market analysis with specific focus on the United States, Japan, and other major markets. Populations affected include patients with HPV16-positive cancers, particularly cervical, head and neck, and oropharyngeal cancers.
Event
Market analysis and forecast report on HPV16-positive cancers, covering epidemiology, current therapies, pipeline developments, and market trends. Includes updates on clinical trials, regulatory designations, and therapeutic approaches.
Scale, impact and risk
HPV16 is the predominant oncogenic type, accounting for ≥60% of anogenital cancers and 44-91% of head and neck cancers. In the US, about 91% of anal cancer cases are attributable to HPV infection. An estimated 26 million Americans have oral HPV infection, with approximately 2,600 infected with HPV16. Japan reported nearly 3,900 incident cases of HPV16+ oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma in 2025.
Response, status and uncertainty
The market is transitioning from traditional treatments to immunotherapy and antigen-targeted approaches. Preventive vaccines have reduced HPV16-related cancer incidence. Pipeline includes HPV16-specific treatments targeting E6/E7 oncoproteins, with several candidates in Phase II/III development. Fast Track Designations have been granted for some therapies. The report provides epidemiology segmentation and market forecast through 2036, with some data expected in H2 2026.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

石药集团现涨超3% SYS 6026注射液用于治疗HPV16或18型相关HSIL的II期临床在中国正式启动 - 新浪网

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报石药集团(CSPC)已在中国启动SYS 6026注射液的II期临床试验,针对与HPV16或18相关的高级鳞状上皮内病变(HSIL)的治疗。这项多中心、随机、双盲、安慰剂对照研究旨在评估该药物的有效性和安全性。继 2024 年 11 月监管部门批准后开始第一阶段试验后,该公司目前正在积极招募和筛选第二阶段的参与者。该公告对石药集团的股价产生了积极影响,该消息导致该股上涨超过 3%。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表于2026-07-24;事件最近发生,2026年启动II期临床试验。
地点与对象
中国;患有 HPV16 或 18 相关的高级鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 的患者。
事件
石药集团(石药集团)宣布在中国启动SYS 6026注射液治疗HPV16或18相关HSIL的II期临床试验。该试验是一项多中心、随机、双盲、安慰剂对照研究,评估疗效和安全性。
规模、影响与风险
没有报告具体病例数或 死亡率 率。该试验重点关注 治疗 对 HPV16/18 相关 HSIL 患者的疗效和安全性。未明确说明风险级别。
应对、状态与不确定性
2024年11月获得中国国家药品监督管理局临床试验批准后启动一期试验。目前二期受试者招募和筛选工作正在积极进行中。来源是完整的原始报告。

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
石药集团现涨超3% SYS 6026注射液用于治疗HPV16或18型相关HSIL的II期临床在中国正式启动 - 新浪网
News Brief
Shijiazhuang Pharmaceutical Group (CSPC) has initiated a Phase II clinical trial in China for SYS 6026 injection, targeting the treatment of high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL) associated with HPV16 or 18. This multicenter, randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled study aims to evaluate the drug's efficacy and safety. Following Phase I trials that began after regulatory approval in November 2024, the company is now actively recruiting and screening participants for Phase II. The announcement has positively impacted CSPC's stock, which rose over 3% on the news.
Original source text (Chinese)

Event
Shijiazhuang Pharmaceutical Group (CSPC) announced the initiation of a Phase II clinical trial in China for SYS 6026 injection to treat HPV16 or 18-related HSIL. The trial is a multicenter, randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled study evaluating efficacy and safety.
Scale, impact and risk
No specific case counts or mortality rates reported. The trial focuses on treatment efficacy and safety for HPV16/18-related HSIL patients. Risk level not explicitly stated.
Response, status and uncertainty
Phase I trial was initiated after receiving clinical trial approval from China's National Medical Products Administration in November 2024. Currently, participant recruitment and screening for Phase II are actively underway. Source is a full original report.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

石药集团:SYS 6026注射液用于治疗HPV16或18型相关宫颈高级别鳞状上皮内病变的II期临床试验在中国正式启动 - QQ News

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报石药集团已在中国启动 SYS 6026 的 II 期临床试验,SYS 6026 是一种针对 HPV16 和 18 相关高度鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 的 mRNA 治疗药物 疫苗。疫苗 通过脂质纳米粒子递送,旨在诱导 T 细胞针对 E6 和 E7 蛋白的反应,可能提供非手术 治疗 选项。继 2024 年 11 月获得 I 期批准后,当前的 II 期研究是一项多中心、随机、双盲、安慰剂对照试验,评估疗效和安全性。参与者招募工作正在积极进行中,尚未报告中期结果或病例数。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表于2026-07-22;事件日期:没有明确说明,但 II 期试验是在 2024 年 11 月 I 期批准后启动的。
地点与对象
中国;目标人群:患有 HPV16 或 18 相关的高级鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 的患者。
事件
石药集团(SYS 6026注射液)在中国启动治疗HPV16或18相关HSIL的II期临床试验。该产品是一种针对 HPV16/18 E6 和 E7 蛋白的治疗性 mRNA 疫苗。以公司官方公告确认。
规模、影响与风险
所提供的证据中未报告。没有提供病例数、死亡人数或明确的风险级别。
应对、状态与不确定性
II 期试验是一项评估疗效和安全性的多中心、随机、双盲、安慰剂对照研究。参与者招募和筛选正在进行中。I 期试验(安全性/耐受性)此前已于 2024 年 11 月获得监管部门批准后启动。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
石药集团:SYS 6026注射液用于治疗HPV16或18型相关宫颈高级别鳞状上皮内病变的II期临床试验在中国正式启动 - QQ News
News Brief
Shijiazhuang Pharmaceutical Group has launched a Phase II clinical trial in China for SYS 6026, an mRNA therapeutic vaccine targeting HPV16 and 18-related high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL). The vaccine, delivered via lipid nanoparticles, aims to induce T-cell responses against E6 and E7 proteins, potentially offering a non-surgical treatment option. Following Phase I approval in November 2024, the current Phase II study is a multi-center, randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled trial assessing efficacy and safety. Recruitment for participants is actively underway, with no interim results or case counts reported yet.
Original source text (Chinese)

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

KP 卫生部门寻求神职人员对人类乳头状瘤病毒 疫苗接种 活动的支持 - 黎明

KP health dept seeks clerics’ support for human papilloma virus vaccination drive - Dawn
新闻简报2026 年 7 月 24 日,开伯尔-普赫图赫瓦省 (KP) 卫生部门与当地神职人员合作,为 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 发起了 疫苗接种 活动。该倡议旨在提高人们对 HPV 的认识并鼓励 疫苗接种,HPV 与多种癌症和生殖器疣有关。然而,所提供的证据并未报告 KP 中任何确诊病例、死亡或与 HPV 相关的暴露。该活动被描述为一场公共卫生活动,但没有提供有关检测、隔离或接触者追踪的详细信息。消息来源是《黎明报》的新闻报道,该活动正在进行中,没有提及任何明确的风险级别或卫生系统影响。

时间

地点与对象
巴基斯坦开伯尔-普赫图赫瓦省 (KP)。
事件
KP 卫生部门寻求神职人员对人类乳头状瘤病毒 疫苗接种 活动的支持。这是一项旨在提高 疫苗接种 率的公共卫生举措,所提供的证据中没有报告确诊病例或死亡病例。
规模、影响与风险
证据中没有报告具体病例、死亡或暴露情况。该事件是 疫苗接种 驱动器,而不是 暴发 或健康危机。证据中没有明确说明风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
该计划仍在进行中,但没有提供有关测试、隔离或接触者追踪的详细信息。来源是新闻报道,该事件被报道为一场公共卫生运动。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

KP health dept seeks clerics’ support for human papilloma virus vaccination drive - Dawn

News Brief
On July 24, 2026, the Khyber Pakhtunkhwa (KP) health department launched a vaccination drive for human papillomavirus (HPV) in collaboration with local clerics. The initiative aims to raise awareness and encourage vaccination against HPV, which is associated with several cancers and genital warts. However, the supplied evidence does not report any confirmed cases, deaths, or exposures related to HPV in KP. The event is described as a public health campaign, with no details on testing, isolation, or contact tracing provided. The source is a news report from Dawn, and the campaign is ongoing without any explicit risk level or health-system impact mentioned.

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报石家庄医药集团(01093.HK)宣布在中国启动 SYS 6026 注射液的 II 期临床试验,这是一种针对 HPV16 或 18 相关的高级鳞状上皮内病变(HSIL)的 治疗。该试验标志着在解决特定 HPV 相关疾病方面迈出了重要一步,但没有披露有关患者人数、试验时间表或中期结果的进一步细节。这一消息发布之际,分析师对该公司股票持积极态度,多家券商维持“买入”评级。这一进展凸显了中国为推进 HPV 相关疾病的治疗选择而不断做出的努力。

发布于 2026-07-22。事件日期:提供的证据中未报告。

目标人群:患有 HPV16 或 18 相关的高级鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 的患者。
事件
石药集团(01093.HK)宣布在中国启动SYS 6026注射液治疗HPV16或18相关HSIL的II期临床试验。经公司确认。

没有报告病例数、死亡人数或明确的风险水平。范围:针对特定 HPV 相关病症的临床试验。

Original title (Chinese)
石药集团(01093.HK):SYS 6026注射液用于治疗HPV16或18型相关宫颈高级别鳞状上皮内病变的II期临床试验在中国正式启动 - Sohu
News Brief
Shijiazhuang Pharmaceutical Group (01093.HK) has announced the launch of a Phase II clinical trial in China for SYS 6026 injection, a treatment targeting high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL) associated with HPV16 or 18. The trial marks a significant step in addressing a specific HPV-related condition, though no further details on patient numbers, trial timelines, or interim results were disclosed. The announcement comes amid positive analyst sentiment toward the company's stock, with multiple brokerages maintaining 'buy' ratings. This development underscores ongoing efforts to advance therapeutic options for HPV-associated diseases in China.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

石药集团(01093.HK):SYS6026注射液用于治疗HPV16或18型相关宫颈高级别鳞状上皮内病变的II期临床试验在中国正式启动 - 东方财富

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报石药集团已在中国启动 SYS6026 的 II 期临床试验,SYS6026 是一种针对 HPV16 或 18 相关高度鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 的治疗性 mRNA 疫苗。疫苗 被归类为 1 类治疗性生物产品,采用脂质纳米颗粒递送来表达 E6 和 E7 蛋白,旨在诱导 T 细胞针对 HPV 感染细胞的反应。HPV16 和 18 是致病性最强的亚型,导致 70% 以上的高级宫颈病变和 宫颈癌。该试验为受影响的女性提供了一种潜在的非手术 治疗 选择,但有关试验进展的更多细节尚未报告。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-07-23;活动日期:2026年7月22日(启动试验公告)。
地点与对象
中国;目标人群:患有 HPV16 或 18 相关的高级鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 的女性。
事件
石药集团(01093.HK)宣布在中国启动针对HPV16或18相关HSIL的治疗性mRNA 疫苗注射液SYS6026的II期临床试验。该产品被归类为 1 类治疗性生物制品。
规模、影响与风险
HPV16和18是致病性最强的HPV亚型;大约 10% 感染这些亚型的女性在 3 年内出现高度宫颈癌前病变。超过 70% 的高级宫颈病变和 宫颈癌 与 HPV16 或 18 相关。疫苗 旨在提供非手术 治疗 选择。
应对、状态与不确定性
II期试验已在中国正式启动。该产品使用脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 递送来表达 E6 和 E7 蛋白,诱导 T 细胞反应以消除 HPV 感染或肿瘤细胞。所提供的证据中没有报告有关试验状态、招募或时间表的进一步细节。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
石药集团(01093.HK):SYS6026注射液用于治疗HPV16或18型相关宫颈高级别鳞状上皮内病变的II期临床试验在中国正式启动 - 东方财富
News Brief
Shijiazhuang Pharmaceutical Group has initiated a Phase II clinical trial in China for SYS6026, a therapeutic mRNA vaccine targeting HPV16 or 18-related high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL). Classified as a Class 1 therapeutic biological product, the vaccine uses lipid nanoparticle delivery to express E6 and E7 proteins, aiming to induce T-cell responses against HPV-infected cells. HPV16 and 18 are the most pathogenic subtypes, responsible for over 70% of high-grade cervical lesions and cervical cancers. This trial represents a potential non-surgical treatment option for affected women, though further details on trial progress remain unreported.
Original source text (Chinese)

China; target population: women with HPV16 or 18-related high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL).

Shijiazhuang Pharmaceutical Group (01093.HK) announced the initiation of a Phase II clinical trial in China for SYS6026 injection, a therapeutic mRNA vaccine targeting HPV16 or 18-related HSIL. The product is classified as a Class 1 therapeutic biological product.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

HPV 和 宫颈癌 意识与对政府信息的信任相关

HPV and Cervical Cancer Awareness Linked to Trust in Government Messaging
新闻简报2026 年 7 月 24 日发表在《JAMA Network Open》上的一项研究表明,美国成年人对 HPV 是 宫颈癌 病因的认识与对政府卫生机构的信任直接相关。通过分析 2022 年 HINTS 调查的数据,研究人员发现 61.4% 的参与者意识到 HPV-宫颈癌 之间的联系。这种意识与对宗教组织的癌症信息和个人癌症病史很少或不信任呈正相关。相反,意识与亚洲种族、较低教育水平、男性以及对政府或医生信息的信任呈负相关。横断面设计限制了因果结论,但研究结果强调了可信来源在有关 HPV 预防和 疫苗接种 的公共卫生信息传递中的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 24 日。该研究分析了 2022 年健康信息国家趋势调查 (HINTS) 的数据。
地点与对象
研究人群由 5,957 名参与者组成,其中 3,914 名参与者回答了 HPV-宫颈癌 认知问题。人口统计:55.8% 为女性,36.5% 为 46-65 岁,65.2% 为白人,9.5% 有个人癌症史。
事件
《JAMA Network Open》上发表的一项横断面研究发现,对 HPV 作为 宫颈癌 病因的认识(61.4% 的参与者)与对政府卫生机构的信任直接相关。该研究评估了美国成年人的意识因素。
规模、影响与风险
61.4% of participants were aware of the HPV-cervical cancer link. Awareness was positively associated with little/no trust in religious cancer information (aOR, 1.88; P=.02) and personal cancer history (aOR, 1.49; P=.02). Inverse associations were observed with Asian race (aOR, 0.31; P<.001), lower education, male sex, and trust in government/physician information.
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法进行因果推断。证据中没有报告具体的公共卫生应对措施或干预措施。来源是肿瘤护士顾问的完整原始报告正文。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

HPV and Cervical Cancer Awareness Linked to Trust in Government Messaging

News Brief
A study published in JAMA Network Open on July 24, 2026, reveals that awareness of HPV as a cause of cervical cancer among US adults is directly linked to trust in governmental health agencies. Analyzing data from the 2022 HINTS survey, researchers found that 61.4% of participants were aware of the HPV-cervical cancer connection. This awareness was positively associated with having little or no trust in cancer information from religious organizations and a personal history of cancer. Conversely, awareness was inversely related to Asian race, lower education levels, male sex, and trust in government or physician information. The cross-sectional design limits causal conclusions, but the findings highlight the importance of trusted sources in public health messaging about HPV prevention and vaccination.
Original source text (English)
Menu SECTIONS News Features CME Conferences Clinical Tools Special Collections Multimedia Cancer Management Fact Sheets Diagnosis & Disease Info Editorial Advisory Board Topics Bladder Cancer Breast Cancer Central Nervous System Cancers Endocrine Cancers Gastrointestinal Cancers General Medicine General Oncology Genitourinary Cancers Gynecologic Cancers Head and Neck Cancers Hematologic Cancers Leukemia Lung Cancer Lymphoma Myeloma Pediatric Cancer Prostate Cancer Renal Cell Carcinoma Sarcomas Skin Cancers Haymarket Medical Network Cancer Therapy AdvisorClinical AdvisorClinical Pain AdvisorDermatology AdvisorEndocrinology AdvisorGastroenterology AdvisorHematology AdvisorInfectious Disease AdvisorMedical BagMPRNeurology AdvisorOncology Nurse AdvisorOphthalmology AdvisorOptometry AdvisorPsychiatry AdvisorPulmonology AdvisorRare Disease AdvisorRenal and Urology NewsRheumatology AdvisorSleep Wake AdvisorThe Cardiology AdvisorVaccine Advisor Search for: Headlines Drugs Conferences Cancer Management Clinical Resources Tools Multimedia CME Part of the News and Features CME Conferences Clinical Tools Special Collections Multimedia Share this article Share on Facebook Share on Twitter Share on LinkedIn Share by Email Print HPV and Cervical Cancer Awareness Linked to Trust in Government Messaging Jessica Nye, PhD | Publish Date July 24, 2026 Most study participants were aware of the link between HPV and cervical cancer (61.4%). Awareness that cervical cancer can be caused by the human papillomavirus (HPV) was directly associated with trust in governmental health agencies, according to results of a study published in JAMA Network Open. Most cervical cancer cases are caused by HPV, which can be prevented with vaccination. Investigators from The University of Texas MD Anderson Cancer Center analyzed data from the 2022 Health Information National Trends Survey (HINTS). This study evaluated the level of awareness that HPV can cause cervical cancer and the factors affecting that awareness among people living in the United States. Of 5957 participants who were asked about their awareness of HPV, 3942 (67.7%) said that they had heard of HPV. Of those, this study included the 3914 participants who responded to the question “Do you think HPV can cause cervical cancer?” The study population comprised 55.8% women, 36.5% were aged 46 to 65 years, 65.2% were White, and 9.5% had a personal cancer history. Most study participants were aware of the link between HPV and cervical cancer (61.4%). Awareness of HPV as a cause of cervical cancer was positively associated with having little or no trust in information about cancer from a religious organization (adjusted odds ratio [aOR], 1.88; P =.02) and having a personal history of cancer (aOR, 1.49; P =.02). “ This cross-sectional study of US adults reported for the first time, to our knowledge, the association of trust in government health agencies, religious organizations, and physicians with the awareness of HPV as a cause of cervical cancer. An inverse relationship was observed between awareness and Asian race (aOR, 0.31; P <.001), education level up to high school or post-high school/some college (aOR, 0.42 and 0.47; both P <.001), male sex (aOR, 0.62; P <.001), some or little/none at all trust in information about cancer from governmental health agencies (aOR range, 0.71 and 0.68; P =.03 and P =.02, respectively), and having only some trust in information about cancer from physicians (aOR, 0.71; P =.03). This study was limited by its cross-sectional design, which did not allow for causal inferences to be made. The study investigators concluded, “This cross-sectional study of US adults reported for the first time, to our knowledge, the association of trust in government health agencies, religious organizations, and physicians with the awareness of HPV as a cause of cervical cancer.” This article originally appeared on Oncology Nurse Advisor References: Oladoyin OO, Cuccaro P, Savas LS, et al. Trust in cancer information sources and awareness of human papillomavirus as a cause of cervical cancer among US adults. Published on July 24, 2026. The study analyzed data from the 2022 Health Information National Trends Survey (HINTS).
Location and population
The study population comprised 5,957 participants, with 3,914 responding to the HPV-cervical cancer awareness question. Demographics: 55.8% women, 36.5% aged 46-65 years, 65.2% White, 9.5% with a personal cancer history.
Event
A cross-sectional study published in JAMA Network Open found that awareness of HPV as a cause of cervical cancer (61.4% of participants) was directly associated with trust in governmental health agencies. The study evaluated awareness factors among US adults.
Scale, impact and risk
61.4% of participants were aware of the HPV-cervical cancer link. Awareness was positively associated with little/no trust in religious cancer information (aOR, 1.88; P=.02) and personal cancer history (aOR, 1.49; P=.02). Inverse associations were observed with Asian race (aOR, 0.31; P<.001), lower education, male sex, and trust in government/physician information.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study was limited by its cross-sectional design, which prevented causal inferences. No specific public health responses or interventions were reported in the evidence. The source is a full original report body from Oncology Nurse Advisor.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

加德满都 宫颈癌 筛查发现 90 名女性 HPV 呈阳性

Cervical cancer screening in Kathmandu finds 90 women positive for HPV
新闻简报A cervical cancer screening program in Kathmandu, Nepal, conducted between March and May 2026, found 90 women positive for human papillomavirus (HPV) among 2,975 participants. Of these, 12 were infected with HPV-16 and 13 with HPV-18, which are high-risk types causing approximately 70% of cervical cancer cases. The remaining 65 had other HPV types. All positive cases underwent colposcopy, with some receiving further procedures like cervical biopsy. Nepal has integrated the HPV vaccine into its routine immunization for girls aged 10. The screening highlights the importance of early detection, as cervical cancer is a leading cause of death among Nepali women, with an estimated four fatalities daily. Early detection significantly improves treatment outcomes.
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-21。事件日期:筛查于2026年3月30日至5月4日进行。
地点与对象
受影响人群:2,975 名接受 宫颈癌 筛查的女性。
事件
已确认事件:加德满都市进行了随机 宫颈癌 筛查,发现 90 名女性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 呈阳性。市卫生部门官员报告。
规模、影响与风险
病例:90 名女性 HPV 检测呈阳性(12 名 HPV-16、13 名 HPV-18、65 名其他 HPV 类型)。据估计,约 70% 的 宫颈癌 病例是由 HPV 16 型和 18 型引起的。宫颈癌 是尼泊尔妇女死亡的一个主要原因,估计每天有四人死亡。
应对、状态与不确定性
回应:对所有 90 名 HPV 阳性女性进行了阴道镜检查;6例行息肉切除术,24例行宫颈活检,11例诊断为宫颈上皮内瘤变(4例高级别,7例低级别)。尼泊尔将 HPV 疫苗 纳入 10 岁女孩的常规免疫接种中。局限性:尼泊尔 宫颈癌 患者的确切人数尚未报告。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Cervical cancer screening in Kathmandu finds 90 women positive for HPV

News Brief
A cervical cancer screening program in Kathmandu, Nepal, conducted between March and May 2026, found 90 women positive for human papillomavirus (HPV) among 2,975 participants. Of these, 12 were infected with HPV-16 and 13 with HPV-18, which are high-risk types causing approximately 70% of cervical cancer cases. The remaining 65 had other HPV types. All positive cases underwent colposcopy, with some receiving further procedures like cervical biopsy. Nepal has integrated the HPV vaccine into its routine immunization for girls aged 10. The screening highlights the importance of early detection, as cervical cancer is a leading cause of death among Nepali women, with an estimated four fatalities daily. Early detection significantly improves treatment outcomes.
Original source text (English)

Login In Now Don't show this again Nepal Cervical cancer screening in Kathmandu finds 90 women positive for HPV Tuesday, 21 Jul 2026 | 12:49 PM MYT share bookmark Related Stories Nepal 1d ago Fuel price surge sends Nepal’s domestic air travel into steep decline Nepal 22 Jul 2026 Nepal moves to make climbing Mount Everest tougher Nepal 19 Jul 2026 Organic farmers show way beyond Nepal’s fertiliser crisis KATHMANDU: Ninety women who underwent random cervical cancer screening carried out by the Kathmandu Metropolitan City have tested positive for human papillomavirus (HPV).According to officials at the municipal health department, the women had no symptoms and were unaware of their condition until the screening.“Of the total positive cases, 12 were infected with HPV-16 and 13 with HPV-18,” said Chandra Bhatta, focal person of the cervical cancer screening programme of the metropolis. “The remaining 65 women were infected with other types of HPV.”Human papillomavirus is a viral infection that spreads through skin-to-skin contact and is a leading cause of cervical cancer—the second-most common cancer in the developing world. Cervical cancer is a major cause of death among Nepali women, with hundreds diagnosed every year.Although the exact number of patients suffering from cervical cancer in Nepal is not known, it is estimated that at least four women die of cervical cancer every day.The screening was carried out between March 30 and May 4, during which 2,975 women were examined. Of them, 2,779 underwent HPV DNA testing and 1,450 underwent genotype testing of the HPV virus. DNA testing detects HPV genetic material in cells collected from the cervix, while genotyping identifies the specific type of HPV present in the cervical cell samples.As the city office had a limited number of testing kits, only 1,329 women were tested specifically for HPV types 16 and 18. HPV types 16 and 18 are estimated to cause about 70 percent of cervical cancer cases.“First, we organise a screening camp for around a month and then perform other testing, including biopsy, mammogram, ultrasound scanning and others, which takes several weeks,” said Bhatta. “Our findings show that many women, who believe they are healthy may be living with various forms of cancer and infected with cancer-causing viruses.”Officials say the finding also shows that women in Nepal are infected not only with HPV types 16 and 18, but also with dozens other HPV types. HPV types 16 and 18 are frequently associated with invasive cervical cancer compared to other types, meaning that they are more carcinogenic than other types.Experts say HPV has more than 200 types, ranging from low-risk to high-risk strains. Around 40 infect the genital tract, and around 14 types are classified as high-risk because they can cause cervical and other cancers. Low-risk types generally cause genital warts or no symptoms at all.Health officials said colposcopy was performed on all 90 women who tested positive for HPV. It is a medical procedure in which a doctor closely examines the cervix, vagina and vulva using a colposcope—a special instrument with a bright light and magnifying lenses.Six women underwent polypectomy to remove uterine polyps, and 24 underwent cervical biopsy.Eleven of the women who tested positive for HPV were diagnosed with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (4 high-grade and 7 low-grade), which refers to precancerous changes in cells lining the cervix.Doctors say cervical intraepithelial neoplasia is not cancer, but if left untreated, high-grade lesions can develop into cervical cancer over time.Nepal has included the HPV vaccine in its routine immunisation programme and provides a single dose to all 10-year-old girls. The HPV vaccine, included in the routine immunisation list, targets types 16 and 18 of the virus.Apart from screening for cervical cancer, the health department of the city office had also carried out breast examinations on 2,961 women, and mammography was performed on 411 of them. Breast biopsy was performed on four women, and of them, two tested positive and are currently receiving treatment.“Most cancer patients in Nepal seek treatment only after symptoms start to emerge,” said Dr Ujjwal Chalise, executive director at the Bhaktapur Cancer Hospital. “Symptoms often do not appear in the initial stages of the disease, and by the time they do, it is often too late, resulting in poor treatment outcomes.”Experts say local governments should ensure that services for basic cancer screening are available in local health facilities, stressing that early detection markedly improves treatment outcomes.- The Kathmandu Post/ANN Follow us on our official WhatsApp channel for breaking news alerts and key updates! HPV E6 共价抑制剂市场、增长和 2036 年预测 - Fact.MR

HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor Market, Growth and Forecast 2036 - Fact.MR
新闻简报The HPV E6 covalent inhibitor market is projected to grow from USD 1.2 billion in 2026 to USD 3.3 billion by 2036, driven by increasing research into treatments for HPV-associated cancers. Small-molecule covalent inhibitors lead the market with a 30.0% share in 2026, followed by cervical cancer indication at 25.0%. Most treatments remain in the preclinical stage (30.0% share), with pharmaceutical companies dominating development (30.0% share). Key challenges include demonstrating direct E6 binding, p53 restoration, and safety before clinical advancement. Regional growth varies, with Germany leading at 14.3% CAGR, followed by Brazil (13.2%) and the United States (12.2%). The market represents a USD 2.1 billion absolute opportunity by 2036, particularly in cervical cancer treatment.

时间

全球市场分析以及区域细分,包括德国、巴西、美国、韩国、英国和日本。目标人群:患有 HPV 相关癌症的患者,特别是 宫颈癌。
事件
Market analysis of HPV E6 covalent inhibitors, a class of drugs in early development for treating HPV-related cancers. The market reached USD 1.1 billion in 2025 and is projected to grow to USD 3.3 billion by 2036 at a 10.6% CAGR.
规模、影响与风险
Market value: USD 1.1 billion (2025), USD 1.2 billion (2026), USD 3.3 billion (2036). CAGR: 10.6% (2026-2036). Key segments: Small-molecule covalent inhibitors (30.0% share in 2026), cervical cancer indication (25.0% share in 2026), preclinical development stage (30.0% share in 2026), pharmaceutical companies as end users (30.0% share in 2026). Absolute market opportunity: USD 2.1 billion by 2036.
应对、状态与不确定性
HPV E6 共价抑制剂市场正处于早期研发阶段,大多数候选药物处于临床前测试阶段。关键要求包括在进入临床试验之前证明与 E6 的直接结合、p53 功能的恢复、肿​​瘤反应和安全性。地区增长各不相同,德国以 14.3% 的复合年增长率领先,其次是巴西 (13.2%)、美国 (12.2%)、韩国 (11.1%)、英国 (10.1%) 和日本 (9.0%)。来源限制:这是一份市场分析报告,而不是临床或公共卫生 暴发 报告。

HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor Market, Growth and Forecast 2036 - Fact.MR

News Brief
The HPV E6 covalent inhibitor market is projected to grow from USD 1.2 billion in 2026 to USD 3.3 billion by 2036, driven by increasing research into treatments for HPV-associated cancers. Small-molecule covalent inhibitors lead the market with a 30.0% share in 2026, followed by cervical cancer indication at 25.0%. Most treatments remain in the preclinical stage (30.0% share), with pharmaceutical companies dominating development (30.0% share). Key challenges include demonstrating direct E6 binding, p53 restoration, and safety before clinical advancement. Regional growth varies, with Germany leading at 14.3% CAGR, followed by Brazil (13.2%) and the United States (12.2%). The market represents a USD 2.1 billion absolute opportunity by 2036, particularly in cervical cancer treatment.
Original source text (English)
Market Value (2025): USD 1.1 Bn - Estimated Value (2026): USD 1.2 Bn - Forecast Value (2036): USD 3.3 Bn - CAGR (2026-2036): 10.6% What is the HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor Market forecast to be worth by 2036? USD 1.2 billion in 2026 to USD 3.3 billion by 2036, at a 10.6% CAGR. - The HPV E6 covalent inhibitor market reached a valuation of USD 1.1 billion in 2025, supported by early-stage oncology research and viral-oncoprotein drug development. - Demand is projected to increase from USD 1.2 billion in 2026 to USD 3.3 billion by 2036. - The market is forecast to record a 10.6% CAGR from 2026 to 2036 as HPV-driven cancer research advances from viral-oncoprotein biology into translational drug development programs. What are the defining numbers behind HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor Market growth? The USD 2.1 billion absolute opportunity to 2036 is led by Small-Molecule Covalent Inhibitors in Molecule Type and Cervical Cancer in Indication. - Demand Drivers in the Market - The treatment must attach directly to E6, since E6 is the protein that blocks p53 in HPV-related tumours. - Researchers must show that binding to E6 helps p53 start working again. They must also track any changes in the tumour after treatment. - Patient selection should consider the HPV type and the location of the tumour. Whether the virus has joined the tumour’s DNA and which treatments the patient has already received also matter. - Strong safety results are required before these treatments move from laboratory testing to the first studies in people. - Key Segments Analyzed - By Molecule Type: Small-Molecule Covalent Inhibitors are expected to hold 30.0% share in 2026, supported by their ability to bind HPV E6 directly and enable durable intracellular target engagement. - By Indication: Cervical Cancer is projected to account for 25.0% share in 2026, owing to its established association with high-risk HPV infection and the need to restore p53 pathway activity. - By Development Stage: Preclinical is anticipated to capture 30.0% share in 2026, driven by ongoing studies of E6 binding, p53 recovery, tumour response, and off-target safety. - By End User: Pharmaceutical Companies are estimated to represent 30.0% share in 2026, attributable to their capabilities in biomarker-led patient selection, toxicology testing, and first-in-human clinical development. - Analyst Opinion at Fact.MR - Shambhu Nath Jha, Senior Analyst at Fact.MR, says “HPV E6 covalent inhibitors are still at an early research stage. At this point, strong proof that the drug binds to E6 matters more than broad claims about HPV-related cancers. Developers must show that the drug reaches E6 inside tumour cells without affecting other targets. They should also show that p53 starts working again and that test results remain clear and consistent. Safety will be another major factor. Before enrolling more patients, sponsors should have a clear plan for how the drug will enter clinical testing.” - Strategic Implications - Before making claims outside the lab, developers must set a clear level of proof that shows the drug binds directly to E6. - Sponsors should support wider testing to check whether the drug also binds to other proteins. This is important because covalent drugs can create safety concerns, and standard binding tests do not give the full picture. - Clinical teams should show that p53 starts working again and that this leads to clear changes in the tumour before enrolling a larger group of patients. - HPV type and tumour location should help decide which patients are most suitable for treatment. These details should not be treated as basic background information. Germany is expected to record a 14.3% CAGR from 2026 to 2036 because specialist oncology research supports early translation. Brazil is projected to post a 13.2% CAGR through 2036 as cervical cancer burden and public-health need keep research attention high. The United States is anticipated to advance at a 12.2% CAGR from 2026 to 2036 owing to trial infrastructure and biotechnology funding. South Korea is estimated to record an 11.1% CAGR by 2036 based on precision-medicine capacity. The United Kingdom is forecast to post a 10.1% CAGR from 2026 to 2036 as genomic laboratory networks support evidence review. Japan is expected to grow at a 9.0% CAGR through 2036 as local evidence review guides adoption. How does the HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor Market break down by segment? Small-Molecule Covalent Inhibitors lead at 30.0% by Molecule Type in 2026. Cervical Cancer leads Indication with 25.0% share in the same year. Which molecule type has the largest share? Small-molecule covalent inhibitors account for 30.0% of the market in 2026. These drugs can enter tumour cells and attach directly to E6. Irreversible E6 binders are also being studied since they can remain attached for a longer time and keep E6 blocked. Another method aims to remove or weaken E6 instead of only stopping its activity. Some studies are also combining E6 inhibitors with immunotherapy or radiation to improve the tumour’s response to treatment. Which cancer type has the largest share? Cervical cancer leads with 25.0% of the market in 2026. Its large share comes from the clear link between long-term high-risk HPV infection and cervical cancer. HPV-positive throat cancer is another area receiving attention in treatment research. Anal cancer also offers a clear group of patients for future studies. Vulvar and penile cancers have smaller patient groups, so research in these areas may require more focused recruitment. The close link between E6 and cervical tumour growth keeps cervical cancer at the centre of early treatment plans. Which development stage has the largest share? Preclinical work holds 30.0% of the market in 2026. Most drug candidates are still being tested in laboratories. Researchers are checking whether they attach to E6, help p53 work again, and remain safe. Phase I studies will mainly look at safety and early signs that the treatment is working. Phase II will examine whether these changes improve patient results. Phase III will require steady results across much larger patient groups. The high share of preclinical work shows that most treatments are still in the early research stage. Why do pharmaceutical companies hold the largest share? Pharmaceutical companies account for 30.0% of the market in 2026. They can manage safety testing, approval work, production, and clinical trials under one system. Biotechnology companies often focus on early research and new drug ideas. Universities study how E6 works and how it affects p53. Cancer centres provide tumour samples and support patient testing. Pharmaceutical companies hold the largest share since they can connect the research, approval, production, and trial stages. What is accelerating HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor Market adoption, and what is holding it back? Adoption is expected to rise when E6 engagement evidence links viral-oncoprotein binding with a measurable downstream effect. Safety uncertainty and limited clinical maturity are expected to restrain broad use. Drivers Impact Analysis - Targeting E6 inside tumour cells: A treatment must show that it can attach to the HPV E6 protein inside tumour cells. It should also cause a clear change in how the tumour works. Germany and the United States may move faster in this area because they have specialist cancer centres that can run these tests and study patient markers. - Cervical cancer control programmes: The WHO 90-70-90 goals bring vaccination, screening, diagnosis, and treatment into one plan. These programmes do not directly increase the use of E6 inhibitors. However, they can help doctors find suitable patients and decide where future treatments may fit into care. - Tests for choosing patients: Researchers must show that blocking E6 helps p53 work again and causes visible changes in the tumour. Patient selection may also depend on the HPV type, where the tumour is found, whether the virus has entered the tumour’s DNA, and which treatments the patient has received before. Simple and reliable testing methods will make these treatments easier to use. - Support for early drug studies: Early research depends on skilled laboratories, cancer trial centres, safety testing, and knowledge of drug approval rules. The United States has many biotechnology companies and cancer research centres, which may help new treatments move into human studies sooner. Progress will depend on turning laboratory results into a clear and complete plan for the first trial in people. Opportunity Impact Analysis - Combination-therapy pathways: HPV-driven tumours are commonly managed through multimodal oncology approaches, creating opportunities to assess E6 inhibitors alongside immunotherapy and radiation. Developers will need to show that these combinations improve treatment response without introducing safety issues that limit clinical use. - Expansion into non-cervical indications: Oropharyngeal Cancer and Anal Cancer provide additional settings where HPV-driven tumour biology may support E6-targeted treatment. Vulvar Cancer and Penile Cancer represent more specialized opportunities that require indication-specific evidence and clearly defined patient-selection criteria before broader development. - Biomarker-guided patient selection: HPV genotype and tumour location are expected to guide clinical-trial design and treatment eligibility. Viral integration, p53 pathway response, and previous treatment history provide additional layers of selection that can help developers identify patients most likely to benefit. - Next-generation treatment modalities: Targeted protein degradation and other emerging approaches may expand the development landscape beyond conventional E6 binding. These modalities offer alternative ways to suppress or remove E6 activity where direct inhibition does not produce a sufficient biological response. Restraints Impact Analysis - Off-target toxicity uncertainty: Covalent mechanisms can create safety concerns when a candidate binds unintended proteins inside the cell. Developers need broad selectivity testing and convincing toxicology evidence before clinical teams can determine whether the expected benefit outweighs the risk. - Unresolved p53 restoration efficacy: E6 inhibition has limited commercial value unless it produces measurable recovery of tumour-suppressor activity. Market progress is expected to remain uneven until laboratory findings translate into consistent biological responses and meaningful patient outcomes. - Incomplete institutional adoption pathway: Hospitals need suitable testing capacity, reimbursement support, treatment-sequencing guidance, and specialist oversight before an E6 inhibitor can enter routine oncology practice. Adoption may remain slow where institutions cannot clearly position the therapy alongside existing treatment options. - Early-stage pipeline concentration: Most development activity remains concentrated in discovery and preclinical research, leaving the market dependent on a limited number of candidates. Broader adoption will require a deeper pipeline, repeatable target-engagement evidence, and successful progression into clinical development. Where is HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor Market demand growing across key countries? A global growth rate represents the weighted average of all markets included in the analysis. The HPV E6 covalent inhibitor market is forecast to grow at a 10.6% CAGR from 2026 to 2036. Germany and the USA follow research- and clinical-infrastructure-led paths, while Brazil is influenced more by disease burden and healthcare access. - South Korea follows a biomarker-led development path. The United Kingdom depends on coordinated evidence review, while Japan is expected to progress more cautiously through local safety and efficacy assessment. - Markets with similar CAGRs can therefore follow different development paths. These differences become relevant when comparing potential entry points across the countries covered. The full report examines these dynamics across North America, Latin America, Western Europe, Eastern Europe, East Asia, South Asia and Pacific, and the Middle East and Africa.
