Germany combines university hospitals, molecular oncology laboratories and Comprehensive Cancer Centers that connect laboratory findings with clinical review. The German Cancer Research Center has also studied the role of the E6 oncoprotein in disrupting p53 activity. The market is expected to record a 14.3% CAGR by 2036 as developers work to prove target engagement and identify the HPV-positive patients most likely to respond. How is Brazil developing HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor demand? 13.2% CAGR, driven by cervical cancer burden and organised HPV screening. Brazil has a substantial cervical cancer treatment need and is expanding screening based on high-risk HPV-DNA testing. This creates a clearer route for identifying patients with persistent oncogenic infections who may later enter targeted-treatment studies. The market is projected to expand at a 13.2% CAGR from 2026 to 2036, although uptake depends on affordable treatment models that work across both the public SUS system and private oncology centres. What supports the USA outlook? 12.2% CAGR, owing to early-stage trial capacity and strong HPV oncology research. The United States has a large network of oncology trial centres and drug developers working across cervical, anal and head-and-neck cancers linked to HPV. This infrastructure supports early human studies and biomarker-based patient recruitment. Demand is anticipated to post a 12.2% CAGR through 2036 as developers establish whether E6 blockade restores p53 activity and produces a measurable tumour response. What underpins South Korea’s growth? 11.1% CAGR, backed by biomarker-led oncology and concentrated hospital infrastructure. South Korea’s major hospitals are well placed to run carefully selected trials that examine HPV genotype, viral integration and tumour location before treatment. This approach suits E6 inhibitors, which may work differently across viral subtypes and cancer sites. The market is estimated to record an 11.1% CAGR by 2036 as companion-testing strategies become more closely linked with clinical development. How is the United Kingdom scaling HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor demand? 10.1% CAGR, led by genomic testing networks and structured NHS evidence review. The United Kingdom has seven Genomic Laboratory Hubs and a nationally managed test directory that links genomic testing with clinical decision-making. This framework offers a clear route for evaluating biomarker-based E6 therapies within specialist cancer pathways. Japan has designated cancer-genomic hospitals and specialist centres capable of supporting first-in-human studies. Clinical programmes are expected to assess cervical, oropharyngeal, anal and other HPV-related tumours separately because viral involvement and drug exposure differ by anatomical site. The market is projected to expand at a 9.0% CAGR through 2036 as Japan-specific evidence defines which tumour groups are suitable for E6-targeted treatment. Who leads the HPV E6 Covalent Inhibitor Market? BioNTech and Precigen lead the closest clinical work around HPV E6 and E7 targets, while INOVIO, ISA Pharmaceuticals and Transgene strengthen therapeutic-vaccine and combination-treatment development. BioNTech develops an mRNA immunotherapy directed at HPV oncoproteins, while Precigen contributes an investigational immunotherapy for HPV-associated cancers. ISA Pharmaceuticals adds synthetic peptide-based treatment research. INOVIO brings DNA-medicine experience, and Transgene contributes viral-vector immunotherapy development. These companies are adjacent HPV-targeted developers rather than confirmed covalent E6 inhibitor providers. Competition is expected to focus on selective E6 engagement, p53 restoration, tumour response, patient selection and off-target safety. Which companies are the key providers? BioNTech SE and Precigen, Inc. are key adjacent providers. Inovio Pharmaceuticals, Inc. and ISA Pharmaceuticals B.V. are also profiled. 暑期接种正当时!关于青少年HPV疫苗接种,大连疾控中心权威解答 - 手机网易网

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报大连市疾病预防控制中心发布青少年HPV 疫苗接种权威指南,确认HPV 疫苗自2025年11月10日起纳入我国国家免疫规划。2011年11月10日及以后出生、现年满13周岁的女童,有资格免费接种两剂二价HPV 疫苗接种。该文章解决了家长常见的担忧,解释了 疫苗接种 在预防 宫颈癌 方面的重要性、疫苗接种 的最佳年龄(9-14 岁)以及 疫苗 提供的长期保护。它还澄清了 HPV 疫苗接种 在国家免疫计划中是强制性的,并为家长提供了有关不同 疫苗 选项的信息。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-07-24。本文讨论了自 2025 年 11 月 10 日起实施的 HPV 疫苗接种 政策,并建议夏季 疫苗接种 进行优化调度。
地点与对象
目标人群是2011年11月10日或之后出生、现年13岁、有资格根据国家免疫规划免费接种HPV 疫苗接种的女孩。
事件
大连市疾病预防控制中心(CDC)发布针对青少年的HPV 疫苗接种权威指南。文章确认HPV 疫苗自2025年11月10日起已纳入国家免疫规划,并对家长普遍关心的疫苗接种问题进行了详细解答。
规模、影响与风险
文章指出,大约80%的女性一生中至少会感染一次HPV。疫苗接种 计划的目标是到 2030 年在 15 岁及以下女孩中实现 90% HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率,这符合世卫组织消除 宫颈癌 的全球战略。据描述,一般人群感染 HPV 的风险较高。
应对、状态与不确定性
大连市疾控中心正在积极宣传HPV 疫苗接种,解决家长关心的问题。文章解释说,疫苗接种在国家免疫规划中是强制性的,具有遵守的法律义务。它还提到,一些地方对非国家计划疫苗提供折扣,例如九价HPV 疫苗的一剂减免。

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
暑期接种正当时!关于青少年HPV疫苗接种,大连疾控中心权威解答 - 手机网易网
News Brief
The Dalian Disease Control and Prevention Center has issued authoritative guidance on HPV vaccination for adolescents, confirming that HPV vaccine has been included in China's national immunization program since November 10, 2025. Girls born on or after November 10, 2011, who are now 13 years old, are eligible for free two-dose bivalent HPV vaccination. The article addresses common parental concerns, explaining the importance of vaccination in preventing cervical cancer, the optimal age for vaccination (9-14 years), and the long-term protection provided by the vaccine. It also clarifies that HPV vaccination is mandatory under the national immunization program and provides information on different vaccine options available to parents.
Original source text (Chinese)

2025年11月10日起,人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗正式纳入国家免疫规划,2011年11月10日以后出生的满13周岁女孩均可享受免费接种2剂次双价HPV疫苗(间隔6个月)。这是国家送给年轻女性的专属健康民生福利,但市疾控中心在实际工作中发现,部分家长仍存在顾虑。针对广大家长们关心的问题,市疾控中心进行了集中解答。一、为什么要接种HPV疫苗?积极响应国家政策,同时保护自身免受疾病威胁。国产二价HPV疫苗正式纳入国家免疫规划疫苗。“居住在中国境内的居民,依法享有接种免疫规划疫苗的权利,履行接种免疫规划疫苗的义务。”也就是说,居民按照规定负有接种免疫规划疫苗的义务,不得以非法定事由拒绝履行,即无故拒绝接种免疫规划疫苗。世界卫生组织于2020年11月17日发布《加速消除宫颈癌全球战略》,提出阶段性目标:到2030年使90%的15岁以下女孩完成HPV疫苗接种,中国同步发布《加速消除宫颈癌行动计划(2023—2030年)》响应战略。高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)持续感染是导致宫颈癌的主要原因,宫颈癌是可预防甚至可以被消除的癌症,为了实现消除宫颈癌的全球战略目标,将HPV疫苗纳入免疫规划疫苗是必须实行的一步重要举措。二、13岁接种HPV疫苗的优势有哪些?世界卫生组织(WHO)指出,9-14岁是HPV疫苗接种的“黄金窗口期”,仅需接种‌2剂次即可产生非劣效于15岁及以上(16-26岁)人群接种3剂次HPV疫苗的免疫效果。HPV主要通过性接触传播,9-14岁的孩子多数未发生性行为,此时期接种可在首次潜在暴露前建立免疫屏障,预防效果接近100%,能有效预防低危型HPV感染可能引发的生殖器疣(双价HPV疫苗无法预防)及高危型HPV持续感染可能引发的宫颈病变、宫颈癌等疾病。这也是建议13周岁女孩接种HPV疫苗并纳入免费接种的核心原因。三、接种HPV疫苗后保护效果有多久?大量研究证实,9-14岁人群接种HPV疫苗能激发更强、更持久的免疫应答。同时根据现有研究数据,在完成双价HPV疫苗全程免疫后,保护效果可持续至少10年以上,且没有证据显示保护力会随时间显著衰减。目前全球最早接种HPV疫苗的人群已随访超过14年,体内抗体依然维持在较高水平。且研究证实,HPV疫苗可诱导机体产生免疫记忆,即在免疫系统识别疫苗包含HPV型别后,可在短期内再次产生大量特异性抗体,以避免HPV的感染及引发的相关疾病,给接种疫苗后相关疾病的持久预防多加了一重免疫保障。四、HPV疫苗的安全性如何?HPV疫苗是基因工程疫苗,仅含由病毒外壳L1蛋白组装形成的“假病毒颗粒”,不含病毒DNA,因此无毒无感染性,不会导致HPV感染,也不会影响孩子成长发育和生殖健康。五、哪些途径会感染HPV病毒?HPV病毒的主要传播途径包括:性接触传播:这是最主要的传播途径,约80%—90%的感染通过此途径发生。密切接触传播:皮肤-皮肤、黏膜-黏膜的直接接触,也可能造成传播。间接接触传播:由于HPV理化稳定性极强,在常温潮湿物体表面可维持感染活性长达数天,接触被HPV污染的内衣、浴巾、浴盆、马桶圈等物品,且在免疫力低下或皮肤黏膜有微小破损时存在理论上的感染可能。母婴垂直传播:感染HPV的母亲在分娩过程中可能将病毒传给新生儿,但这种情况相对罕见,且多数新生儿可自行清除。部分家长可能觉得HPV病毒距离孩子的生活很遥远,其实不然。感染可能性低的传播途径,不意味着绝不可能。HPV在人群中的感染率极高,约80%的女性一生中至少感染过一次HPV。六、接种HPV疫苗对孩子的月经有影响吗?目前研究显示HPV疫苗接种与月经量少、月经不规律等月经不调症状无因果关系,因此没有来月经或月经不规律的女生也是可以接种的。月经期并非HPV疫苗接种的禁忌证,但大部分女性在月经期有不同程度经期不适,建议尽量避开月经期接种。七、免疫规划和非免疫规划HPV疫苗如何选择?免疫规划HPV疫苗为国产二价HPV疫苗,可预防HPV16和18型感染。九价HPV疫苗在覆盖HPV16、18型的基础上,还可覆盖另外5种高危型别和2种低危型别;四价HPV疫苗除HPV16、18型以外还可覆盖2种低危型别。已全程接种过非免疫规划二价、四价、九价HPV疫苗者,视为完成HPV疫苗接种,无需重复接种免疫规划HPV疫苗。家长可以自愿自费选择为孩子接种四价、九价HPV疫苗,提供针对更多型别HPV病毒感染的保护。目前我市部分九价HPV疫苗有减免一剂次的优惠政策,市民可按需选择。八、为什么说暑假是接种HPV疫苗的最佳时间?暑假7—8月完成第一针,次年寒假接种第二针,两段假期无缝衔接,完全不占用在校上课时间,不用临时请假、耽误学业,省心又方便。半岛晨报、39度视频首席记者黄凤桐 Time
Published on 2026-07-24. The article discusses HPV vaccination policies implemented since November 10, 2025, and recommends summer vaccination for optimal scheduling.
Location and population
The target population is girls born on or after November 10, 2011, who are now 13 years old and eligible for free HPV vaccination under the national immunization program.
Event
The Dalian Disease Control and Prevention Center (CDC) has issued authoritative guidance on HPV vaccination for adolescents. The article confirms that HPV vaccine has been included in the national immunization program since November 10, 2025, and provides detailed answers to common parental concerns about vaccination.
Scale, impact and risk
The article states that approximately 80% of women will be infected with HPV at least once in their lifetime. The vaccination program aims to achieve 90% HPV vaccination coverage among girls aged 15 and below by 2030, in line with WHO's global strategy to eliminate cervical cancer. The risk of HPV infection is described as high in the general population.
Response, status and uncertainty
The Dalian CDC is actively promoting HPV vaccination and addressing parental concerns. The article explains that the vaccination is mandatory under the national immunization program, with legal obligations for compliance. It also mentions that some local areas offer discounts on non-national program vaccines, such as a one-dose reduction for the nine-valent HPV vaccine.

医生强调:只要查出HPV阳性,女性一定要多关注这5点! - 手机网易网

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报本文为 HPV 检测呈阳性的女性提供健康指导,强调 HPV 感染很常见,通常无需干预即可清除。它概述了五个关键点:了解 HPV 类型(区分高风险和低风险)、解释 TCT 结果、确定感染是短暂的还是持续的、了解何时需要阴道镜检查和活检以及通过改变生活方式支持免疫健康。文章澄清,HPV 阳性并不等于 宫颈癌,大多数感染会自行消退。它强调了对高危病例(特别是 HPV16/18)进行定期筛查和随访的重要性,同时揭穿了有关 HPV 和不忠的误解。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-07-24。没有提到具体的活动日期。
地点与对象
目标人群:HPV 检测呈阳性的女性。
事件
健康指导文章强调了 HPV 阳性女性的五个要点。不是 暴发 报告或已确认的疾病事件。
规模、影响与风险
未提供 暴发 数据。文章指出,80%的中国女性一生中都会感染HPV,但90%的感染会在2年内消失。风险背景:HPV16/18 阳性带来 宫颈癌 的较高风险(细胞学异常的 CIN3+ 风险分别为 89.9% 和 82.3%)。
应对、状态与不确定性
文章提供了随访指导:HPV16/18阳性患者无论TCT结果如何都应接受阴道镜检查;其他TCT异常的高危型HPV需要阴道镜检查;低危 HPV 需要监测疣。没有主动调查或 治疗 描述。来源限制:已审查完整的原始文章正文。

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
医生强调:只要查出HPV阳性,女性一定要多关注这5点! - 手机网易网
News Brief
This article provides health guidance for women who have tested positive for HPV, emphasizing that HPV infection is common and usually clears without intervention. It outlines five key points: understanding HPV types (distinguishing high-risk from low-risk), interpreting TCT results, determining if infection is transient or persistent, knowing when colposcopy and biopsy are needed, and supporting immune health through lifestyle changes. The article clarifies that HPV positivity does not equal cervical cancer, with most infections resolving spontaneously. It stresses the importance of regular screening and follow-up for high-risk cases, particularly HPV16/18, while debunking misconceptions about HPV and infidelity.

很多女性拿到“HPV阳性”报告单,手都在发抖,脑子里已经开始想最坏的结果——化疗、手术、生命倒计时......但真实情况是:HPV阳性,离宫颈癌还远得很。今天咱们就把这件事彻底说清楚:查出HPV阳性后,你最该关注的是哪几点? 先搞明白:HPV阳性到底意味着什么?HPV叫人乳头瘤病毒,是一种主要通过性接触传播的病毒。它感染的是皮肤和黏膜的上皮细胞。绝大多数有性生活的女性,一生中某个阶段都会感染HPV。但感染≠得病,更≠得癌。根据2025年最新数据,我国80%的女性一生中至少会感染一次HPV。这个数字听起来吓人,但真相是:约90%的HPV感染会在2年内被免疫系统自动清除。超过80%的感染可在6到24个月内被机体自然清除。低危型HPV需要5到6个月消退,高危型HPV需要8到24个月。 也就是说,10个HPV阳性的人里,9个都能自己好。所以,查出HPV阳性,第一件事不是恐慌,是先看清楚三个东西:你感染的是哪一型、TCT是什么结果、有没有持续感染。第一点:看清楚你感染的是哪一型HPV病毒有200多种亚型,根据致癌风险分为两大类:高危型:包括16、18、31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66、68等。其中16和18型最凶险,约70%以上的宫颈癌与这两种类型持续感染有关。 低危型:如6、11型等,主要引起生殖器疣,不致癌。如果是低危型阳性,基本不用担心宫颈癌的问题,注意观察有无疣体就行。如果是高危型但不是16/18,也远没到“天塌了”的程度——该复查复查、该随访随访。但如果是HPV16或18阳性,需要格外重视。2025年《中国子宫颈癌筛查指南(二)》明确指出:HPV16/18阳性,无论TCT结果如何,都建议直接转诊阴道镜检查。国内研究显示,HPV16阳性且细胞学提示HSIL时,CIN3+(高级别癌前病变)的即时风险高达89.9%,HPV18阳性者也达82.3%。但这不等于确诊宫颈癌,只是说风险更高了,需要尽快去做进一步检查。第二点:看清楚TCT是什么结果HPV检测是找“病因”——有没有病毒。TCT(宫颈细胞学检查)是看“结果”——病毒有没有对宫颈细胞造成伤害。两项结合起来,才能看清全貌。拿到报告,分几种情况对照着看:情况一:HPV阳性 + TCT正常(非16/18型)这是最常见的情况。病毒虽然存在,但还没伤害到宫颈细胞。不需要任何治疗,也不需要手术。建议6到12个月后复查HPV+TCT。给免疫力一个清除病毒的时间。 情况二:HPV16/18阳性,无论TCT结果如何直接去妇科做阴道镜检查。不需要等、不需要观察。情况三:HPV阳性(高危型)+ TCT异常不管是不是16/18,只要TCT有异常(如ASC-US、LSIL、HSIL等),都需要做阴道镜检查。情况四:HPV阴性 + TCT正常恭喜你,这次筛查过关了。可以按常规周期3到5年后再查。第三点:搞清楚是“一过性”还是“持续性”感染这是决定风险高低最核心的一点。一过性感染:病毒来了又被免疫系统清走了。约70%到80%的HPV感染属于一过性,会自行清除。持续感染:医学上的定义是间隔6到12个月,两次检测中查出同一种高危型HPV阳性。 只有持续感染,才有进展为癌前病变和癌症的风险。平均而言,持续感染2到3年可能发展为癌前病变,10到12年可能发展为宫颈癌。从HPV感染到宫颈癌变,需要经历CIN1→CIN2→CIN3,通常从感染到癌前病变约5年,从癌前病变到浸润癌需要10到20年。第四点:搞清楚需不需要做阴道镜和活检很多人一听说“要做阴道镜”就吓坏了,觉得“是不是很严重”。其实阴道镜只是一种检查手段,不是治疗。什么情况需要做阴道镜:HPV16或18阳性HPV阳性 + TCT异常(ASC-US及以上)其他高危型HPV持续感染超过1年阴道镜能看到宫颈上的可疑区域,必要时取一小块组织做病理活检——活检结果才是判断有没有癌前病变的“金标准”。 如果活检结果是CIN1(低级别病变),多数情况下可以观察随访,超过60%会自然消退。如果活检结果是CIN2或CIN3(高级别病变),需要根据年龄、生育需求等因素决定治疗方案。在这个阶段干预,完全可以阻断宫颈癌的发生。第五点:做好生活管理,给免疫力“搭把手”目前针对HPV病毒没有特效药。清除病毒主要靠你自己的免疫系统。以下几件事,是你能做的:规律作息,别熬夜。 长期熬夜直接损伤免疫系统。 适度运动。 每天运动30到45分钟,每周5次,持续12周后免疫细胞数量和活性都会明显增强。均衡饮食。 多吃新鲜蔬菜水果、优质蛋白(鱼、虾、瘦肉),补充维生素C、维生素E、β-胡萝卜素和硒等营养素。严格限制高糖、高脂及深加工食品。戒烟。 长期吸烟会使HPV感染者转癌风险增加。固定性伴侣,全程使用安全套。 减少交叉感染和重复感染的机会。还有两件必须知道的事第一,打了HPV疫苗,不等于不用筛查。目前的HPV疫苗都是预防性疫苗,不能覆盖所有高危型别,而且对已经感染的型别没有治疗作用。接种疫苗后仍应定期进行宫颈癌筛查。 第二,HPV阳性不等于伴侣不忠。HPV感染非常普遍,主要通过性接触传播,但也可通过皮肤黏膜接触感染。病毒可在体内潜伏多年,单次检测阳性无法推断感染时间。不要因为一个阳性结果就去猜忌对方——把它当成两个人共同关注健康的起点。说句掏心窝的话HPV阳性,不等于宫颈癌。它更像是身体给你发的一条提醒短信:“你的免疫系统该加把劲了,记得定期检查。”80%的女性一生中都会感染HPV,但90%都能自己清除。 从感染到癌变,中间隔着10到20年的时间——这段时间足够你做无数次筛查、处理无数次癌前病变。 查出HPV阳性后,你最该关注的不是“会不会得癌”,而是看清楚型别、看明白TCT、搞清楚是持续还是一过性、该复查就复查、该做阴道镜就做阴道镜。把这几点盯紧了,HPV阳性几乎不会发展成宫颈癌。真正危险的,不是HPV阳性本身,而是查出阳性后不去管它、不去复查、不去处理。所以,拿到HPV阳性报告单,深呼吸,别慌。看清型别,找医生,该查查,该治治。这件事没那么可怕,可怕的是你被吓住了,什么都不做。 Target population: women who have tested HPV positive.

Health guidance article emphasizing five key points for women with HPV positive results. Not an outbreak report or confirmed disease event.
Scale, impact and risk
No outbreak data provided. Article states 80% of Chinese women will be infected with HPV in their lifetime, but 90% of infections clear within 2 years. Risk context: HPV16/18 positivity carries higher risk for cervical cancer (89.9% and 82.3% risk of CIN3+ with abnormal cytology, respectively).
Response, status and uncertainty
Article provides guidance on follow-up: HPV16/18 positive patients should undergo colposcopy regardless of TCT results; other high-risk HPV with abnormal TCT requires colposcopy; low-risk HPV requires monitoring for warts. No active investigation or treatment described. Source limitation: Full original article body was reviewed.

发现是希望之举:铭记 Maura Gillison 博士对真理的追求 - ASCO Post

Discovery Is an Act of Hope: Remembering Dr. Maura Gillison’s Pursuit of Truth - The ASCO Post
新闻简报Maura Gillison 博士是一位确立了 HPV 与头颈​​癌之间因果关系的科学家先驱,于 2026 年 6 月 21 日去世。她在 2000 年发表的具有里程碑意义的论文颠覆了一个世纪的传统观点,证明 HPV-16 是头颈癌的一个独特亚型的直接致病因子。25 年来,她系统地描述了 HPV 相关癌症的 传播,将性 传播 确定为关键危险因素,并将 HPV 状态确定为口咽癌临床结果的最重要预测因子。她的工作改变了癌症研究和 治疗,导致临床试验中的 HPV 分层并影响全球 疫苗接种 策略。吉里森的遗产通过她指导的研究人员和临床医生以及她的发现拯救的生命得以延续。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-24。Maura Gillison于2026年6月21日去世。
地点与对象
美国和墨西哥;受影响人群:六大洲的研究人员、临床医生和公共卫生倡导者。
事件
确立 HPV 与头颈​​癌之间因果关系的科学家 Maura Gillison 于 2026 年 6 月 21 日去世。她的工作改变了人们对 HPV 相关癌症的理解和 治疗。
规模、影响与风险
她发现 HPV 会导致大部分头颈癌,这一发现将通过扩展的 疫苗接种、完善的分期以及围绕生物学而不是假设设计的 治疗 继续拯救生命。
应对、状态与不确定性
她的遗产被全球研究人员和临床医生继承。该领域继续以她针对 HPV 相关癌症的循证方法为基础,不断研究分子机制和 治疗 降级策略。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Discovery Is an Act of Hope: Remembering Dr. Maura Gillison’s Pursuit of Truth - The ASCO Post

News Brief
Dr. Maura Gillison, a pioneering scientist who established the causal link between HPV and head and neck cancers, passed away on June 21, 2026. Her landmark 2000 paper overturned a century of conventional wisdom, demonstrating that HPV-16 is a direct causal agent in a distinct subset of head and neck cancers. Over 25 years, she systematically characterized the epidemiology of HPV-associated cancers, identified sexual transmission as a key risk factor, and established HPV status as the most important predictor of clinical outcomes in oropharyngeal cancer. Her work transformed cancer research and treatment, leading to HPV stratification in clinical trials and influencing global vaccination strategies. Gillison's legacy endures through the researchers and clinicians she mentored and the lives saved by her discoveries.
Original source text (English)
Maura L. Gillison, MD, PhD Maura Gillison did not chase awards. She chased answers, and especially clinically impactful answers. That distinction between the pursuit of recognition and the pursuit of truth defined everything about her as a scientist, a clinician, and a person. She was direct with compassion, generous to the last, and possessed of a laugh that could fill a room. To the surprise of many who only knew her as a speaker behind a podium, she was also a trained dancer who had not entirely left the dance floor. Dr. Gillison passed away on June 21, 2026, surrounded by her devoted mother, her husband, Dr. David Symer, and her daughters, Sanna and Lia. She died as she had lived: on her own terms, in her childhood home in Ohio. The field she transformed is immeasurably poorer for her loss. A Discovery That Changed Everything Born in Ontario, Canada, and raised across the United States and Mexico, Maura brought a global sensibility to everything she touched. She studied zoology at Duke University before earning her medical degree from the Johns Hopkins University School of Medicine and later her doctoral degree in clinical investigation at Johns Hopkins Bloomberg School of Public Health, a dual mastery that would prove uniquely well-suited to the question that would consume her career: Why were some people with head and neck cancer doing so unexpectedly well? The answer, as she demonstrated in a landmark paper that she published with colleagues in 2000 in the Journal of the National Cancer Institute , was HPV. 1 Specifically, HPV-16, a sexually transmitted virus, is a direct causal agent in a distinct and biologically different subset of head and neck cancers. This finding overturned nearly a century of conventional wisdom attributing all head and neck cancers to tobacco and alcohol. She is widely recognized as the first to establish this causal link conclusively. Her own reflection on the work was characteristic, as she told The ASCO Post in 2019: “Although the 2000 JNCI paper changed my whole career, I was having so much fun that I didn’t realize its implications.” Fun, she always insisted, was not a distraction from good science; it was the engine of it. Building the Evidence, Brick by Brick What followed was 25 years of systematic, relentless science. Seminal papers in The New England Journal of Medicine in 2007 and 2010 characterized the epidemiology of HPV-associated head and neck cancer, identified sexual transmission as a key risk factor, and established the striking difference in prognosis between HPV-positive and HPV-negative disease. 2,3 In 2009, she presented data at the ASCO Annual Meeting demonstrating that HPV status is the single most important predictor of clinical outcome in oropharyngeal cancer, work that prompted the National Cancer Institute to mandate HPV stratification in all HNSCC clinical trials. It was, in the most literal sense, a redrawing of the map. It was a game changer for our field. From the Jeg Coughlin Chair at Ohio State to the halls of MD Anderson, she used every platform to push further: into the molecular mechanisms of HPV-driven malignancy, into treatment de-escalation strategies that might spare patients unnecessary toxicity, and into the global implications of HPV vaccination for cancer prevention. Her science was never abstract. It was always ultimately about the patient and always to address fundamental questions. Honors Earned, Never Sought The accolades accumulated despite her indifference to them: election to the American Society for Clinical Investigation (2010); the Richard and Hinda Rosenthal Memorial Award from AACR (2012); election to the National Academy of Medicine (2013); the Karnofsky Award and Lecture from ASCO (2021). In 2025, she received the VinFuture Grand Prize, an international distinction recognizing discoveries that create tangible, scalable, and positive change that benefits humanity. She accepted these recognitions graciously and went immediately back to work. The Whole Person Those who knew Maura well knew that the scientist was inseparable from the person. She had trained as a dancer in her youth and carried that physicality with her, a precision, a sense of rhythm, an understanding that timing matters. She was intentional in every decision and action, ensuring they were consistent with her moral compass. She was not one to mince words; she told you what she thought, plainly and without performance, and you trusted her the more for it. She possessed a humor that was quick and pointed and occasionally, memorably, irreverent. She was devoted to her family with the same intensity she brought to her work. She mentored generously and without ego, the kind of mentor who asked harder questions of the people she believed in, and whose belief meant something precisely because it was not freely given. Researchers, clinicians, and public health advocates across six continents carry her influence forward in the questions they ask and the rigor with which they pursue answers. Every Day Is a Beautiful Day—Don’t Let It Get Away Maura Gillison understood, perhaps more acutely than most, that time is not infinite and that what you do with it is everything. She would have little patience for grief that became paralysis. Her life’s message, spoken in data and in deed, was that discovery is an act of hope and hope requires showing up. In the spirit she would recognize and probably smile at, in that way she had. To humbly borrow a thought from the song she might have danced to: It’s a beautiful day. Don’t let it get away. For those of us who had the privilege of working alongside her, or learning from her, or simply witnessing what relentless curiosity in service of humanity looks like, the obligation is clear. Carry the work forward. Ask the harder question. Make the day beautiful, make it count. Her discovery that HPV causes a substantial subset of head and neck cancers will continue to save lives—through expanded vaccination, refined staging, and treatments designed around biology rather than assumption—long after those who knew her are gone. That is the measure of a life in science. That is Maura Gillison’s measure. Sue S. Yom, MD, PhD, MAS, FASTRO , i s Professor and Vice Chair, Strategic Advisory Department of Radiation Oncology; Professor, Otolaryngology-Head and Neck Surgery, at the University of California, San Francisco; David Raben, MD , is Senior Executive Advisor with Bicara Therapeutics; A. Dimitrios Colevas, MD, FASCO , is Professor of Medicine at Stanford University School of Medicine; and Carole Fakhry, MD, MPH , is Professor of Otolaryngology Head and Neck Surgery at Johns Hopkins University. REFERENCES 1. Gillison ML, Koch WM, Capone RB, et al. Evidence for a causal association between human papillomavirus and a subset of head and neck cancers. J Natl Cancer Inst 92:709-720, 2000. 2. D’Souza G, Kreimer AR, Viscidi R, et al: Case-control study of human papillomavirus and oropharyngeal cancer. N Engl J Med 356:1944-1956, 2007. 3. Ang KK, Harris J, Wheeler R, et al: Human papillomavirus and survival of patients with oropharyngeal cancer. N Engl J Med 363:24-35, 2010. Originally published in ASCO Connection. © American Society of Clinical Oncology. June 26, 2026. All rights reserved. ASCO Remembers Karnofsky Award Recipient Maura L. Gillison, MD, PhD, Pioneer in HPV-Associated Cancers ASCO joins the oncology community in mourning the loss of Maura L. Gillison, MD, PhD . As a medical oncologist specializing in thoracic/head and neck cancers, and as a molecular epidemiologist, Dr. Gillison definitively characterized the association between human papillomavirus (HPV) and a distinct subset of head and neck cancers starting as a graduate student. Dr. Gillison passed away on June 21, 2026; she was 61. In recognition of her far-reaching contributions to understanding HPV-associated cancers, ASCO presented Dr. Gillison with the David A. Karnofsky Memorial Award, the Society’s highest scientific honor, in 2021. In her award lecture, Dr. Gillison provided an overview of the growing global burden of HPV-associated cancers, chronicled her major research breakthroughs about the natural history of HPV-associated cancers, and discussed her latest research, focused on understanding the effects of HPV integration into the host genome including focal genomic instability that may promote cancer development. She emphasized the ongoing challenge of screening and prevention, calling it “the greatest unmet need” in oropharyngeal cancer. A longtime ASCO member, Dr. Gillison served as an associate editor of the Journal of Clinical Oncology (JCO). Her past volunteer service includes a term on the JCO Editorial Board and track leadership positions on the Annual Meeting Education Program Committee and Annual Meeting Scientific Program Committee, among other roles. To read more about the life of Dr. Maura Gillison and see further remembrances from friends and colleagues, visit ASCOPost.com . Reprinted with permission from ASCO Connection , June 26, 2026
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 2026-07-24. Maura Gillison passed away on June 21, 2026.
Location and population
United States and Mexico; affected population: researchers, clinicians, and public health advocates across six continents.
Event
Maura Gillison, a scientist who established the causal link between HPV and head and neck cancers, passed away on June 21, 2026. Her work transformed the understanding and treatment of HPV-associated cancers.
Scale, impact and risk
Her discovery that HPV causes a substantial subset of head and neck cancers will continue to save lives through expanded vaccination, refined staging, and treatments designed around biology rather than assumption.
Response, status and uncertainty
Her legacy is carried forward by researchers and clinicians globally. The field continues to build on her evidence-based approach to HPV-associated cancers, with ongoing research into molecular mechanisms and treatment de-escalation strategies.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

不论男女,身上若长出5种“瘊子”,或已感染HPV病毒!别不当回事 - 手机网易网

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报2026 年 7 月 21 日发表的一篇中国健康文章澄清,五种常见的皮肤疣——寻常疣、扁平疣、丝状疣、跖疣和生殖器疣——通常是由低危 HPV 感染引起的,而不仅仅是与 ZX​​QTERMCQXZ 相关的高危类型引起。这篇文章通过对一名河南 62 岁女性的案例研究,揭穿了 HPV 疣仅与不良卫生习惯或性活动有关的神话,并指出它们可以影响任何人,无论年龄或性别。虽然大多数皮肤疣是良性且非癌性的,但作者警告不要自我 治疗 以防止扩散或感染。建议包括针对有症状的疣进行医疗咨询,以及针对符合条件的个人采取皮肤保护和 HPV 疫苗接种 等预防措施。该作品旨在减少耻辱并促进早期医疗关注。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-07-21,包含一个未指定的最近时期的案例。
地点与对象
中国,一名来自河南省的 62 岁女性的具体病例。受影响的一般人群包括各个年龄段的男性和女性。
事件
一篇健康意识文章讨论了五种类型的皮肤疣与 HPV 感染的关联,强调这些疣是由低危 HPV 类型引起的,并不完全与 宫颈癌 或性 传播 有关。
规模、影响与风险
未提供具体病例数或 暴发 数据。文章将常见的皮肤疣(寻常疣、扁平疣、丝状疣、足底疣)描述为良性且非癌性的,除非引起不适或扩散,否则对健康的影响很小。如果治疗不当,癌症的风险被认为是低的,但扩散和继发感染的风险是中等的。
应对、状态与不确定性
本文建议 诊断 和专业人士 治疗(例如冷冻、激光)针对疣的症状或美容问题进行医疗咨询。它建议采取预防措施,例如避免皮肤损伤和提高免疫力。没有主动的 暴发 或调查报告;不确定性源于公众对 HPV 传播 的误解和耻辱。

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
不论男女,身上若长出5种“瘊子”,或已感染HPV病毒!别不当回事 - 手机网易网
News Brief
A Chinese health article published on July 21, 2026, clarifies that five common skin warts—verruca vulgaris, flat warts, filiform warts, plantar warts, and genital warts—are often caused by low-risk HPV infections, not exclusively by high-risk types linked to cervical cancer. Using a case study of a 62-year-old woman from Henan, the article debunks myths that HPV warts are solely related to poor hygiene or sexual activity, noting they can affect anyone regardless of age or gender. While most skin warts are benign and non-cancerous, the author warns against self-treatment to prevent spread or infection. Recommendations include medical consultation for symptomatic warts and preventive measures like skin protection and HPV vaccination for eligible individuals. The piece aims to reduce stigma and promote early medical attention.

岁的李阿姨,进门就撸起袖子伸胳膊,说自己这大半年手背上冒了五六个硬邦邦的小疙瘩,表面糙糙的,不疼也不痒。一起跳广场舞的老姐妹都劝她,这就是年纪大了长的 “老年瘊子”,谁身上没几个,不用瞎操心。可李阿姨心里总不踏实,尤其是最近脖子上也长出好几个细细的小肉揪,带孙子的时候总怕蹭到孩子脸上。拗不过心里的疙瘩,她还是抽了一上午时间来医院检查。结果皮肤科医生看完直接告诉她,这些所谓的 “瘊子”,本质上都是HPV 病毒感染引起的皮肤疣。李阿姨当时就愣了,连着问了好几遍:HPV 不是那种和妇科病、宫颈癌有关的病毒吗?我都绝经十几年了,平时出门回家都洗手,家里毛巾也都是分开用的,怎么会染上这个?有这种想法的人不在少数。很多人对 HPV 的认知,都停留在 “女性专属”“和私生活有关”,但这其实是最大的认知误区。HPV 全称是人乳头瘤病毒,目前已知的亚型有上百种,根据致病特点分为皮肤型和黏膜型,也分高危型和低危型。我们常说的和宫颈癌相关的,是黏膜高危型的 HPV,而皮肤上长的各类瘊子,大多是皮肤低危型的 HPV 感染引起的。这种病毒不分男女,不分年龄,十几岁的孩子到七八十岁的老人,都有可能接触感染。很多人一听到感染 HPV,就下意识觉得是 “个人卫生不好”,甚至往私生活上联想,这又是一个常见的误解。皮肤型 HPV 的传播途径非常普遍,日常很多不起眼的小事,都可能让病毒钻空子。比如平时做家务、干农活不小心磨破了手,皮肤出现肉眼看不见的微小破损;比如洗澡的时候喜欢用搓澡巾使劲搓,把皮肤角质层搓坏了;再比如共用了家人的毛巾、拖鞋,或是在公共浴室、泳池旁边光脚走路,都有可能接触到病毒。尤其是中老年人,随着年龄增长免疫力逐渐下降,皮肤的屏障修复能力也不如年轻人,接触病毒后更容易长出肉眼可见的疣体。这和个人品行、卫生习惯没有必然联系,就是很常见的皮肤病毒感染,完全不用因此有心理负担。最常见的是寻常疣,也就是大家嘴里常说的 “瘊子”。它大多长在手上、手指缝或者胳膊上,是硬硬的、表面粗糙的丘疹,小的像针尖,大的能长到黄豆大小,颜色和肤色相近,或是偏深的褐色。平时经常做家务、手部容易磨出小伤口的人,最容易被这种病毒盯上。还有一种很容易和老年斑、脂肪粒混淆的,是扁平疣。它偏爱长在脸上、手背和前臂这些暴露在外的皮肤上,是扁扁平平的小丘疹,表面比较光滑,呈肤色或者淡褐色,常常一长就是一小片。很多人长在脸颊、额头上,以为是年纪大了长的斑,随手抠一抠,结果病毒顺着手指沾到别的地方,越长越多。中老年人脖子上最常冒出来的小肉揪,大多是丝状疣。它总喜欢长在脖子、眼睑、下巴这些皮肤薄嫩的地方,样子是细细软软的丝状凸起,像个小肉坠一样垂在皮肤上,有的是肤色,有的偏棕褐色。不少人觉得这是 “皮肤老化的正常现象”,甚至会用手揪、用细线勒,想自己弄掉,却不知道这样很容易把病毒扩散到周围皮肤,还可能引发发炎感染。长在脚底板上的跖疣,是最容易被认错的一种。很多人脚底下长了硬疙瘩,走路压着疼,就默认是鸡眼,随便买鸡眼膏来贴,结果越贴越严重。跖疣表面看起来粗糙发硬,轻轻削开表层能看到细小的黑点,那是皮下的出血点。它是 HPV 通过脚底的微小破损感染造成的,经常光脚走公共浴室、泳池地面,或者鞋子磨脚总破皮的人,很容易中招。还有一种需要格外警惕的,是尖锐湿疣。和前面几种皮肤疣不同,它是黏膜型 HPV 感染引起的,大多出现在私密部位和肛周,主要通过亲密接触传播,属于性传播疾病的范畴。如果私密部位长出菜花状、乳头状的赘生物,一定要及时去正规医院就诊,别因为不好意思拖延。不少人查出是 HPV 感染引起的瘊子,当场就慌了,生怕会癌变,这又是一个认知上的偏差。绝大多数皮肤疣都是低危型 HPV 引起的,属于良性的皮肤赘生物,不会发生癌变,对身体健康的直接影响很小。真正有致癌风险的,是长期持续感染黏膜高危型 HPV,比如 16、18 型,而且这个过程通常需要十几年的时间,定期筛查就能有效防控。但也不能因为是良性的就完全放任不管。一方面,疣体会自身接种传染,越长越多,长在脸上、脖子上会影响外观。另一方面,长在脚底、眼睑这些特殊位置的疣体,会影响走路、视物,给日常生活带来不便。如果反复用手抠抓刺激,还可能造成皮肤破损、继发细菌感染,反而添了新麻烦。很多人长了瘊子,第一反应是自己抠掉,或是找偏方、乱涂药膏,这是最不推荐的做法。用手抠或者用不干净的工具修剪,很容易把病毒带到其他完好的皮肤上,长出更多新的疣体,还可能留下疤痕、造成感染。如果疣体数量少、没有任何不舒服的感觉,也可以先观察,一部分免疫力较好的人,疣体可能会在几个月到一年内慢慢自行消退。如果疣体越长越多,或是长在脸上、脖子上影响外观,长在脚底影响走路,最好去正规医院的皮肤科处理。临床常用的冷冻、激光等方式,都是成熟安全的处理方法,遵医嘱规范治疗即可。想要减少感染和复发的概率,日常要做好几件小事。首先是保护好皮肤屏障,洗澡别用搓澡巾使劲搓皮肤,做家务、干农活的时候可以戴上手套,减少皮肤出现微小破损的机会。其次是注意个人卫生,不跟别人共用毛巾、搓澡巾、拖鞋,去公共浴室、泳池尽量穿自己的拖鞋,不要光脚踩地面。最重要的是提升自身免疫力,规律作息,均衡饮食,适度活动,免疫力好的人,即使接触了病毒,也不容易被感染,就算感染了也更容易自行清除。另外,很多人觉得 HPV 疫苗只有女性才能打,其实男性接种同样可以保护自己,减少皮肤疣和相关疾病的风险,符合接种条件的人群都可以根据自身情况选择。身上长了小疙瘩别不当回事,也别自己瞎琢磨瞎处理,先分清情况,必要时找专业医生看看。小毛病重视起来,才能避免拖成大麻烦。 特别声明:以上内容(如有图片或视频亦包括在内)为自媒体平台“网易号”用户上传并发布,本平台仅提供信息存储服务。 Notice: The content above (including the pictures and videos if any) is uploaded and posted by a user of NetEase Hao, which is a social media platform and only provides information storage services. China, with a specific case of a 62-year-old woman from Henan province. General population affected includes both men and women across age groups.
Event
A health awareness article discussing the association of five types of skin warts with HPV infection, emphasizing that these warts are caused by low-risk HPV types and are not exclusively linked to cervical cancer or sexual transmission.
Scale, impact and risk
No specific case counts or outbreak data provided. The article describes common skin warts (verruca vulgaris, flat warts, filiform warts, plantar warts) as benign and non-cancerous, with minimal health impact unless they cause discomfort or spread. Risk is framed as low for cancer but moderate for spread and secondary infection if improperly treated.
Response, status and uncertainty
The article advises medical consultation for diagnosis and professional treatment (e.g., freezing, laser) for symptomatic or cosmetically concerning warts. It recommends preventive measures like avoiding skin damage and improving immunity. No active outbreak or investigation is reported; the uncertainty stems from public misconceptions about HPV transmission and stigma.

新研究:HPV 疫苗接种 有助于预防 宫颈癌 死亡 - PhRMA

New study: HPV vaccination helps prevent cervical cancer deaths - PhRMA
新闻简报《柳叶刀》上发表的一项新研究提供了强有力的现实证据,证明 HPV 疫苗接种 正在英格兰拯救生命。在 12 至 13 岁接种疫苗的女孩在 30 岁之前死于 宫颈癌 的风险几乎为零,而在较大年龄接种疫苗的女性的死亡人数也明显低于预期。研究发现,到 2024 年,HPV 疫苗接种 在英格兰预防了大约 200 例 宫颈癌 死亡,与未接种疫苗的预期相比,30-34 岁的女性因 宫颈癌 死亡的相对风险降低了 63%。这些发现表明消除 宫颈癌 现在是可以实现的,但成功取决于维持高 疫苗接种 率和支持性政策。该研究强调了 HPV 疫苗接种 对癌症预防的变革性影响。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026 年 7 月 21 日。事件日期:未明确说明,但研究涵盖 2024 年之前的数据。
地点与对象
女孩在 12-13 岁接种疫苗,女性在 30-34 岁接种疫苗。
事件
《柳叶刀》上发表的一项新研究提供了强有力的现实证据,证明 HPV 疫苗接种 正在预防 宫颈癌 死亡。研究发现,12-13 岁接种疫苗的女孩在 30 岁之前死于 宫颈癌 的风险几乎为零,而年龄较大时接种疫苗的女性的 宫颈癌 死亡人数也比未接种 疫苗接种 的预期要少。
规模、影响与风险
到 2024 年,HPV 疫苗接种 在英格兰预防了大约 200 例 疫苗接种 死亡。在 30-34 岁的女性中,疫苗接种 导致 疫苗接种 死亡的相对风险比没有 疫苗接种 的预期低 63%。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究基于人群水平疾病 监测。研究人员表示,消除 宫颈癌 现在是一个可以实现的目标,但进展取决于通过支持覆盖和获取 疫苗 的政策来维持及时和高的 疫苗接种 率。研究结果表明,HPV 疫苗接种 正在改变癌症预防的可能性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

New study: HPV vaccination helps prevent cervical cancer deaths - PhRMA

News Brief
A new study published in The Lancet offers strong real-world evidence that HPV vaccination is saving lives in England. Girls vaccinated at ages 12–13 had a near-zero risk of dying from cervical cancer before age 30, while women vaccinated at older ages also showed significantly fewer deaths than expected. The research found that HPV vaccination prevented approximately 200 cervical cancer deaths in England through 2024, with women ages 30–34 experiencing a 63% lower relative risk of death from cervical cancer compared to unvaccinated expectations. These findings indicate that eliminating cervical cancer is now achievable, but success depends on maintaining high vaccination rates and supportive policies. The study underscores the transformative impact of HPV vaccination on cancer prevention.
Original source text (English)
Home Blog New study: HPV vaccination helps prevent cervical cancer deaths Blog Carolyn Ha July 21, 2026 New study: HPV vaccination helps prevent cervical cancer deaths A new study published in The Lancet offers some of the strongest real-world evidence to date that vaccination against human papillomavirus (HPV) is saving lives. What you should know: Research conducted in England found that girls vaccinated against HPV at ages 12–13 had a near-zero risk of dying from cervical cancer before age 30. And women vaccinated at older ages also had fewer cervical cancer deaths than expected without vaccination. Why it matters: HPV vaccination prevents the infections that cause nearly all cases of cervical cancer , making vaccination a critical intervention in combating this cancer. More than a decade of evidence demonstrates HPV vaccines are highly effective at preventing HPV infections, precancerous lesions and cervical cancer. The research further shows: HPV vaccination prevented approximately 200 cervical cancer deaths in England through 2024. Among women ages 30–34, already well beyond the target age when they were vaccinated, HPV vaccination was associated with a 63% lower relative risk of death from cervical cancer than would have been expected without vaccination. The bigger picture: Researchers say these findings indicate that eliminating cervical cancer is now an achievable goal. But that progress depends on maintaining timely and high vaccination rates with policies that support coverage and access to vaccines. The bottom line: The new findings, drawing on population-level disease surveillance, show that HPV vaccination is changing what is possible in cancer prevention. Policymakers should support policies that preserve access and help ensure families can get recommended vaccines without unnecessary barriers. Learn more at PhRMA.org/Vaccines . Topics: Vaccines , Cancer Related Resources Blog America's cancer research progress is real and worth protecting May 28, 2026 Blog May 26, 2026 Ahead of ASCO: Cancer innovation is promising, but policy choices matter Blog May 21, 2026 New research reinforces the importance of hepatitis B vaccination at birth Blog April 24, 2026 World Immunization Week: Putting preventable diseases in the past
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: July 21, 2026. Event date: Not explicitly stated, but the study covers data through 2024.
Location and population
Girls vaccinated at ages 12–13 and women ages 30–34.
Event
A new study published in The Lancet provides strong real-world evidence that HPV vaccination is preventing cervical cancer deaths. The study found that girls vaccinated at ages 12–13 had a near-zero risk of dying from cervical cancer before age 30, and women vaccinated at older ages also had fewer cervical cancer deaths than expected without vaccination.
Scale, impact and risk
HPV vaccination prevented approximately 200 cervical cancer deaths in England through 2024. Among women ages 30–34, vaccination was associated with a 63% lower relative risk of death from cervical cancer than would have been expected without vaccination.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study is based on population-level disease surveillance. Researchers indicate that eliminating cervical cancer is now an achievable goal, but progress depends on maintaining timely and high vaccination rates with policies that support coverage and access to vaccines. The findings show that HPV vaccination is changing what is possible in cancer prevention.

沃森生物:子公司双价HPV疫苗获坦桑尼亚上市许可 - 东方财富

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报沃森生物科技于2026年7月23日宣布,其子公司玉溪泽润生物科技的二价HPV 疫苗已在坦桑尼亚获得上市许可。用于预防高危型HPV 16型和18型(包括宫颈癌)引起的疾病的疫苗获得了坦桑尼亚药品和医疗器械管理局的“药品注册证书”。这一监管里程碑代表着 疫苗 进入坦桑尼亚市场,但没有披露有关公共卫生影响或分销计划的详细信息。此次批准凸显了该公司将 HPV 预防产品扩展到国际市场。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表于2026-07-23;近日发生事件,拥有坦桑尼亚监管机构颁发的疫苗注册证书。
地点与对象
坦桑尼亚;疫苗由沃森生物科技旗下玉溪泽润生物科技有限公司生产。
事件
沃森生物科技子公司玉溪泽润生物科技有限公司获得坦桑尼亚药品和医疗器械管理局颁发的“药品注册证”,批准其二价HPV 疫苗在坦桑尼亚上市。
规模、影响与风险
二价 HPV 疫苗 主要用于预防由 高危型HPV 16 型和 18 型(例如 宫颈癌)引起的疾病。证据中没有报告具体的病例数、死亡数或风险水平。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗已获得坦桑尼亚官方监管批准,表明监管流程已完成。证据中没有提供有关正在进行的调查、测试或公共卫生应对措施的信息。

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
沃森生物:子公司双价HPV疫苗获坦桑尼亚上市许可 - 东方财富
News Brief
Watson Biotechnology announced on July 23, 2026, that its subsidiary Yuxi Zerun Biotechnology has received marketing approval for its bivalent HPV vaccine in Tanzania. The vaccine, designed to prevent diseases caused by high-risk HPV types 16 and 18, including cervical cancer, obtained a 'Drug Registration Certificate' from the Tanzanian Medicines and Medical Devices Administration. This regulatory milestone represents the vaccine's entry into the Tanzanian market, though no details on public health impact or distribution plans were disclosed. The approval underscores the company's expansion into international markets for its HPV prevention products.

石药集团:SYS6026注射液治疗HPV相关疾病II期临床试验在中国正式启动 - 东方财富

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报2026年7月22日,石药集团宣布在中国启动针对HPV 16或18型引起的宫颈高级鳞状上皮内病变(HSIL)的治疗注射液SYS6026的中国II期临床试验。该试验标志着开发HPV相关癌前病变靶向治疗的重要一步。虽然该公告确认了试验的开始,但有关患者入组、中期结果或潜在时间表的详细信息仍未披露。这一进展强调了解决 HPV 相关疾病的持续努力,尽管现有预防措施 疫苗,但这些疾病仍然是全球主要的健康问题。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-22。事件日期:2026-07-22(IIa期试验启动)。
地点与对象
目标人群:患有 HPV 16 或 18 相关宫颈高级鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 的患者。
事件
确认事件:SYS6026注射液治疗HPV相关疾病的II期临床试验在中国启动。石家庄医药集团通过香港联交所公告报道。
规模、影响与风险
没有报告病例/死亡/暴露计数。范围:HPV 16/18 相关宫颈 HSIL 的治疗开发。没有明确的风险声明证据。
应对、状态与不确定性
试验启动已确认。没有提供有关调查、测试、隔离或接触者追踪的详细信息。来源限制:完整的原始文章正文未经审查(联合摘要)。

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
石药集团:SYS6026注射液治疗HPV相关疾病II期临床试验在中国正式启动 - 东方财富
News Brief
On July 22, 2026, Shijiazhuang Pharmaceutical Group announced the initiation of a Phase II clinical trial in China for SYS6026 injection, a treatment targeting high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL) of the cervix caused by HPV types 16 or 18. The trial marks a significant step in developing targeted therapies for HPV-related precancerous conditions. While the announcement confirms the trial's start, details regarding patient enrollment, interim results, or potential timelines remain undisclosed. The development underscores ongoing efforts to address HPV-associated diseases, which remain a major global health concern despite existing preventive vaccines.
Original source text (Chinese)

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,北京经开区适龄女孩可免费接种 - thepaper.cn

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报中国已将HPV 疫苗接种纳入国家免疫规划,自2025年11月10日起生效,北京经济技术开发区现向符合条件的女孩免费提供疫苗接种。该政策标志着对 2011 年 11 月 10 日之后出生且年满 13 岁的女孩从自愿 疫苗接种 到强制性 疫苗接种 的重大转变。HPV 疫苗接种 被认为是针对 宫颈癌 的最具成本效益的长期干预措施,超过 80% 的女性一生中至少经历过一次 HPV 感染。二价 HPV 疫苗(针对 HPV16/18)分两次注射,间隔六个月,研究显示 9-14 岁女孩的抗体血清转化率为 100%。到 2027 年,针对特定年龄段的 9 价 疫苗 还可享受额外优惠政策。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表于2026-07-22;政策自2025年11月10日起实施。
地点与对象
北京经济技术开发区(北京亦庄);2011年11月10日之后出生、年满13岁及以上的符合条件的女孩。
事件
确认的政策变化:HPV 疫苗接种 纳入国家免疫规划,对符合条件的女孩从自愿接种转为免费强制接种 疫苗接种。
规模、影响与风险
没有报告具体病例数;政策旨在降低 宫颈癌 风险;80% 以上的女性一生中至少感染一次 HPV;疫苗接种被描述为最具成本效益的长期干预。
应对、状态与不确定性
政策实施正在进行中;疫苗接种 可通过学校课程或在当地诊所预约获得;2026 年至 2027 年间,针对 9 价 HPV 疫苗 的 9-26 岁(女性)和 18-26 岁(男性)提供具体优惠政策(优惠政策);来源限制:部分文章正文。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,北京经开区适龄女孩可免费接种 - thepaper.cn
News Brief
China has incorporated HPV vaccination into its national immunization program, effective since November 10, 2025, with Beijing Economic-Technological Development Area now offering free vaccinations to eligible girls. The policy marks a significant shift from voluntary to mandatory vaccination for girls born after November 10, 2011, who are 13 years or older. HPV vaccination is recognized as the most cost-effective long-term intervention against cervical cancer, with over 80% of women experiencing at least one HPV infection in their lifetime. The bivalent HPV vaccine (targeting HPV16/18) is administered in two doses six months apart, with studies showing 100% antibody seroconversion rates in 9-14 year-old girls. Additional preferential policies are available for the 9-valent vaccine for specific age groups through 2027.
Original source text (Chinese)
澎湃号·政务 > HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,北京经开区适龄女孩可免费接种 北京亦庄 北京经济技术开发区管理委员会 2026-07-22 09:00 北京 自2025年11月10日起,国家将HPV疫苗纳入免疫规划,这一举措更被列为“2026年卫生健康系统为民服务十件实事”之一。此次政策的核心变化是: HPV疫苗接种从以往的自愿接种,正式变为国家为每一个符合要求的女孩免费接种。 我们整理了以下基本知识,请您阅知,积极配合HPV疫苗接种,为孩子的健康保驾护航,共同助力实现消除宫颈癌的目标。 快问快答 带您快速了解新政 什么是免疫规划疫苗? 免疫规划疫苗是指政府免费向公民提供,公民应当依照规定接种的疫苗,居住在中国境内的居民,依法享有接种免疫规划疫苗的权利,履行接种免疫规划疫苗的义务。 什么是HPV? 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是女性生殖道及皮肤黏膜常见病原体,主要通过感染人的皮肤和黏膜引起上皮增生性病变,80%以上女性一生至少感染过1次HPV。目前已发现数百种型别的HPV,分为高危和低危型,高危HPV感染不仅是所有宫颈癌的原因,也是部分阴道、阴茎、肛门以及头颈部癌症的病因。 为什么要接种HPV疫苗? HPV疫苗接种是降低宫颈癌患病风险最经济、最有效的长期干预措施。及时接种HPV疫苗能够预防高危型HPV的持续感染,从而有效降低患宫颈癌等疾病的风险。 此次HPV纳免的接种对象是哪些人群? 2011年11月10日以后出生的满13周岁女孩。 此次纳免的疫苗是哪种? 双价HPV疫苗,针对HPV16/18两种亚型,接种2剂次,间隔6个月。 疫苗保护效果怎么样? 研究表明9-14岁女性2剂次接种后HPV16/18抗体阳转率均达100%。 疫苗安全吗? 目前上市的HPV疫苗均为基于HPV病毒样颗粒(VLPs)为抗原的疫苗,由于VLPs不含有病毒DNA,所以不具感染性和致癌性,保障了疫苗的安全性。 为什么选择这个年龄段? 研究表明,9至14岁女孩接种HPV疫苗产生的抗体水平,是15岁以上人群的2倍以上,而且保护效果可持续至少10年以上。因此,在13周岁接种HPV疫苗可以获得更高的抗体水平和更持久的保护效果。 适龄女生如何预约接种? 适龄女生可根据所在学校安排,参加校内集中接种。也可通过拨打就近的预防接种门诊电话、现场登记、“京通”小程序、“首都疫苗服务”APP等方式进行预约,预防接种门诊信息可在北京市疾病预防控制局网站或北京市疾病预防控制中心官网查询。 北京经开区接种门诊信息一览 进口九价HPV疫苗优惠政策 适龄人群:9—26岁女性、18—26岁男性。 优惠规则:在2026年7月1日至2027年12月31日期间在京接种首针,并在2028年7月31日前在同一接种单位完成全程接种的适龄人群,可享受优惠。 温馨提示:具体优惠政策请拨打门诊电话详询。 来源:开发区社会事业局微信公众号 特别声明 本文为澎湃号作者或机构在澎湃新闻上传并发布,仅代表该作者或机构观点,不代表澎湃新闻的观点或立场,澎湃新闻仅提供信息发布平台。申请澎湃号请用电脑访问https://renzheng.thepaper.cn。

大部分女性都躲不过的病毒,反复感染易癌变,两个高危年龄段尤其要注意...... - Sohu

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报这篇来自搜狐的文章,发表于2026年7月21日,为女性提供有关人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染的健康指导。它强调了HPV感染的两个高危年龄组:20岁和45岁左右,由于免疫力变化和生活因素。文章解释说,虽然大多数 HPV 感染都会被免疫系统清除,但持续的高危感染可能会导致 宫颈癌。它强调了对有性活动史或宫颈疾病的女性定期筛查的重要性,并推荐疫苗接种进行保护。该文章还解决了常见的误解,例如认为绝经后妇女不需要筛查,并强调早期宫颈变化可能是无症状的。
查看新闻五要素
时间
Published on 2026-07-21. No specific event date is mentioned in the evidence.
地点与对象
中国,重点关注中国女性,特别是两个高危年龄组的女性:20岁左右和45岁左右。
事件
一篇讨论 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染风险、癌症进展和女性预防措施的健康意识文章。本文并未报告特定的 暴发 或已确认的事件,而是提供一般指导。
规模、影响与风险
文章提到,HPV感染在女性中很常见,有两个感染高峰年龄(20岁和45岁)。它指出,如果存在持续感染,高危型HPV 类型可能会导致 宫颈癌,但没有提供具体的病例数或死亡人数。它强调了定期筛查和疫苗接种的重要性。
应对、状态与不确定性
本文提供了有关 HPV 筛查、疫苗接种 和预防的一般指导。它阐明大多数 HPV 感染可以被免疫系统清除,但持续的高危感染可能导致癌症。它建议对有性活动史或宫颈疾病史的女性进行定期筛查,并指出只有那些没有宫颈病史或性活动史的女性才能在 65 岁时停止筛查。来源为搜狐全文,光明王转载。

Original title (Chinese)
大部分女性都躲不过的病毒,反复感染易癌变,两个高危年龄段尤其要注意...... - Sohu
News Brief
This article from Sohu, published on July 21, 2026, provides health guidance for women regarding human papillomavirus (HPV) infection. It highlights two high-risk age groups for HPV infection: around 20 and 45 years old, due to immunity changes and life factors. The article explains that while most HPV infections are cleared by the immune system, persistent high-risk infections can lead to cervical cancer. It emphasizes the importance of regular screening for women with a history of sexual activity or cervical disease, and recommends vaccination for protection. The article also addresses common misconceptions, such as the belief that postmenopausal women do not need screening, stressing that early cervical changes may be asymptomatic.

需要!感染过HPV仍建议适龄接种,可防护未感染亚型;男性接种同样有益。 2 快60岁还需要做HPV筛查吗? 目前国家规定的宫颈癌筛查的上限是64岁,所以建议60岁也要做;如果常年筛查都没异常,64岁后就不用再查,不放心也能继续做。 3 HPV疫苗该怎么选? 不少女性执着非要等九价,其实大可不必。 二价主打16、18高危型,能预防七八成宫颈癌;四价在此基础上,还可预防6、11型引发的尖锐湿疣;九价防护亚型更多,但不用盲目等候。 有二价、四价疫苗就可以尽早接种,疫苗搭配定期筛查,能有效远离宫颈癌。 对于女性来说,爱自己是一辈子的必修课,尤其是50岁以后,身体的各项机能开始走下坡路,更要把自己的健康放在心上! 转自:养生堂 来源:北京时间返回搜狐,查看更多 平台声明:该文观点仅代表作者本人,搜狐号系信息发布平台,搜狐仅提供信息存储空间服务。 首赞 +1 点赞失败 阅读 (22万) by Taboolaby TaboolaSponsored LinksSponsored LinksPromoted LinksPromoted Links你可能会喜欢Why Are Women Still Wearing These Outdated Fashion Trends?womenmagUndo40 Best Dinner RecipesCorrie CooksUndoJay-Z Fighting Godmother of Man Claiming to Be His Son Over 6-Figure DebtTMZUndoTom Cruise and Katie Holmes' Daughter Suri Grabs Part in Modern Shakespeare AdaptationTMZUndo王楚钦独揽4分立功,周启豪输18岁小将,梁靖崑登乒乓世界封面,山东魏桥2连胜!Undo2024年,蛇蝎美女叶诚尘被注射死刑,前男友透露她生前诡异习惯_张波_孩子_陈美琳Undo 推荐阅读 吃“春药”后,是什么感觉?医生说的2个案例,告诉你真实感受 乔虎子 · 06-29 21:15 3 80%的女性都曾感染过HPV!身上长了这个东西的,要特别注意 朝元的小世界 · 昨天 04:38 0 同房时多接触女性的隐秘好处,89%的男生一辈子没意识到 历来爱生活 · 今天 00:09 1 中共中央批准,开除王峻党籍 新浪财经 · 昨天 09:18 14Outdated Style Trends Older Women Still WearwomenmagUndo 一个妓女从业10年后的职场领悟:见过太多的男人,比心理专家还懂得人性 小鸭子嘎嘎 · 今天 00:00 3 南京,一个13岁女孩,捡到一个金项链,送到了派出所。失主赶来,刚要开口道谢,眼神却... 壮壮科普 · 今天 06:13 3 终于不再隐瞒!张柏芝小儿子身份曝光,和谢贤的绯闻早已真相大白 星娱记 · 07-22 13:43 14 女生走路时,为什么屁股会左右摇摆? View five news elements
Time
Published on 2026-07-21. No specific event date is mentioned in the evidence.
Location and population
China, with emphasis on Chinese women, particularly those in two high-risk age groups: around 20 years old and around 45 years old.
Event
A health awareness article discussing human papillomavirus (HPV) infection risks, cancer progression, and prevention measures for women. The article is not reporting a specific outbreak or confirmed event but rather providing general guidance.
Scale, impact and risk
The article mentions that HPV infection is common in women, with two peak infection ages (20 and 45). It states that high-risk HPV types can lead to cervical cancer if there is persistent infection, but does not provide specific case numbers or death counts. It emphasizes the importance of regular screening and vaccination.
Response, status and uncertainty
The article provides general guidance on HPV screening, vaccination, and prevention. It clarifies that most HPV infections are cleared by the immune system, but persistent high-risk infections can lead to cancer. It recommends regular screening for women with a history of sexual activity or cervical disease, and notes that screening can be stopped at age 65 only for those without cervical history or sexual activity. The source is a full article from Sohu, republished from Guangming Wang.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

乌克兰免费分发 38,000 多剂 Gardasil-9,用于预防 HPV 疫苗接种 - Межа。Новини України。

News article (English original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报乌克兰启动了免费 HPV 疫苗接种 计划,向区域中心分发了超过 38,000 剂 Gardasil-9。疫苗 针对 12-13 岁的女孩,与 2026 年 1 月生效的更新后的国家免疫日历保持一致。Gardasil-9 可针对 9 种 HPV 类型提供保护,包括高风险 16 型和 18 型,它们是 宫颈癌 的主要原因。疫苗 在分销前经过了质量控制,并通过与 Merck Sharp & Dohme 签订的两年协议进行采购。该举措旨在扩大公众预防的机会,并支持乌克兰的全国 宫颈癌 预防计划,预计到 2026 年底增加剂量。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 24 日;疫苗接种计划自2026年1月1日起生效;首批货物于 2026 年 7 月抵达。
地点与对象
乌克兰;针对12-13岁女孩。
事件
据 Ukrinform 报道,乌克兰正在分发超过 38,000 剂 Gardasil-9 疫苗,用于治疗免费的 HPV 疫苗接种。
规模、影响与风险
已交付 38,000 剂;预防 9 种 HPV 类型,包括高危型 16 型和 18 型,它们是 宫颈癌 的主要原因;全国 宫颈癌 预防计划的一部分。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗在分发前经过国家实验室质量控制;通过与 Merck Sharp & Dohme 签订的为期两年的管理访问协议进行采购;预计到 2026 年底剩余剂量;管理分销的区域中心;全国疫苗供应保持稳定。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (English original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (English)
Ukraine distributes over 38,000 doses of Gardasil-9 for free HPV vaccination - Межа. Новини України.
News Brief
Ukraine has initiated a free HPV vaccination program, distributing over 38,000 doses of Gardasil-9 to regional centers. The vaccine targets girls aged 12–13, aligning with the updated National Immunization Calendar effective January 2026. Gardasil-9 provides protection against nine HPV types, including high-risk types 16 and 18, which are primary causes of cervical cancer. The vaccine underwent quality control before distribution and was procured through a two-year agreement with Merck Sharp & Dohme. This initiative aims to expand public access to prevention and support Ukraine's nationwide cervical cancer prevention program, with additional doses expected by the end of 2026.

repositories, experts question legal basis Ukraine distributes over 38,000 doses of Gardasil-9 for free HPV vaccination Friday, 24 July 2026, 04:54 A major shipment of Gardasil-9 has arrived and is now moving to regional centers. Free HPV shots for 12–13-year-old girls will follow the updated schedule. As reported by the online media Ukrinform. More than 38,000 doses of the nine-valent Gardasil-9 vaccine against human papillomavirus (HPV) are being delivered to regions across Ukraine. The Ministry of Health of Ukraine reports that the vaccine arrived this month and is now being distributed across regions for free vaccination of girls aged 12–13, in line with the updated National Immunization Calendar. Delivery, Quality Control, and Procurement Terms Before distribution to the regions, Gardasil-9 vaccine underwent state laboratory quality control. To procure the drug in 2025, the Ministry of Health signed a two-year managed-access agreement with the American company Merck Sharp & Dohme (MSD). The remaining doses are expected by the end of 2026. The nine-valent vaccine purchased for Ukraine provides the widest protection among vaccines for HPV prevention to date. It protects against nine types of the virus, including the high-risk types 16 and 18, which are the main causes of cervical cancer. From January 1, 2026, the updated National Immunization Calendar takes effect, under which the state will provide HPV vaccination. The vaccination will be free of charge. This approach expands the public’s access to prevention and supports the nationwide program to prevent cervical cancer. More interesting materials: Large study shows 9-valent HPV vaccination reduces HPV-related cancer risk in males by about 46%, supporting timely vaccination and catch-up doses to prevent anal, penile, and throat cancers. Ukraine finances vaccination against 11 infectious diseases and provides emergency rabies immunoprophylaxis. National vaccine supply remains stable, while regional centers manage local distribution and availability updates. Gardasil nine-valent HPV vaccine has arrived in Ukraine, offering protection against nine HPV types linked to cervical cancer. Free vaccination for girls aged 12-13 starts January 2026. AI companies tighten access to frontier models, hindering cybersecurity researchers Friday, 24 July 2026, 04:52 New export controls and gatekeeping by AI vendors are slowing defensive research, forcing experts to rely on local and open models. According to Techcrunch Over several months, AI giants have been developing tested programs and strict guardrails to limit the use of their models by malicious actors. However, these limits are currently hindering the work of legitimate network defenders, as well as offensive cybersecurity professionals. In June, the U.S. government imposed export restrictions on the widely anticipated Mythos and Fable models from Anthropic. Reports say the restrictions were aimed at preventing the use for creating and carrying out harmful cyberattacks, including attempts to bypass their security barriers. Regardless of whether the incident was truly driven by fear of an ‘out-of-control release,’ the fact remains that Anthropic has repeatedly positioned Mythos as the so-called ‘cyber-endangerment object’ that is provided after thorough verification with strict constraints. (Subsequently, export restrictions on Fable 5 and Mythos 5 were lifted: Fable 5 returned to general access on July 1; Mythos 5 was restored only for selected American organizations under government scrutiny.) Such access controls are not unique to Mythos. Anthropic and OpenAI offer cybersecurity researchers programs through which they can apply for verified access with fewer restrictions: OpenAI – Trusted Access for Cyber, Anthropic – Cyber Verification Program. Impact on the research community and justified limitations Such access regimes are widely criticized by researchers whose work involves discovering unknown vulnerabilities and finding ways to exploit them before criminals do. I’m not entirely comfortable with these giant companies making arbitrary decisions about what is safe in the field of security, and what isn’t. – Mark Dowd The mentioned expert from a well-known group of security solutions noted that his work might influence how security is perceived and the development of appropriate approaches, but he is not alone in this understanding. Various offensive cybersecurity specialists spoke about how they use AI tools and how they operate with their limitations. Requesting the use of the model with the aim of attempting to exploit a flaw is a key step in confirming that it is a real vulnerability worth fixing. If the barrier refuses to respond, defenders become less effective. – Chris Enley He added that the same tool can be both offensive and defensive – and their roles cannot be fully separated. Likewise, it is compared to a hammer: you cannot build a house without a hammer, but the tool can be used as a weapon. It’s like a hammer, he continued. You can’t build a house without a hammer. – Chris Enley To bypass such blocks, some specialists turn to open models without strict restrictions and run them locally. Paolo Stagno from CrowdFense noted that AI companies “practically place customers in the role of children who need to be supervised” through their verification programs and restrictions. AI companies effectively place customers as children who need to be supervised. – Paolo Stagno Such approaches have led some researchers to use frontier models solely for reverse-engineering analysis to avoid data leakage risks into cloud services while probing vulnerabilities. Those who work with such models emphasize that denial of access or restrictions reduce the effectiveness of their work. I still want to own discovering the vulnerability and using it as a weapon myself, and that won’t change, even if all restrictions were lifted tomorrow. – Giuseppe Cali Other industry participants argue that restrictions push toward using open local models or Chinese open-source solutions such as GLM, which can be downloaded and run without checks or restrictions. Practically, it means a lot of time is spent negotiating with the model rather than focusing on the core security program. – Chris Thompson Chris Thompson also noted that when using frontier models the quality of results can be inconsistent, even within the more ‘soft’ offerings from Anthropic and OpenAI. This pushes researchers to seek a balance between obtaining useful information and avoiding unpredictable model outputs. Rather than analyzing the vulnerability and justifying its exploitability, the researcher looks for why they obtain incoherent results or why the models overly sanitize the output. – Chris Thompson As a result, the research community increasingly turns to Chinese open models or local versions without strict checks, which experts say may reduce oversight of tool usage in the United States and increase data leakage risks. A massive storm is coming: defenders may lose the race in artificial intelligence due to restrictions and access controls. – Chris Thompson In the long run, the research community calls frontier labs to open their programs, provide responsible access, and hold those who misuse tools accountable. Otherwise, defenders may concede the edge in fighting new threats that are rapidly emerging in today’s digital environment. In conclusion, they emphasize that a balance is needed between open safety verification and responsible use of high-tech tools. Preserving the ability of research power with minimal necessary restrictions is a critical condition for maintaining cybersecurity at a high level in the face of increasing attack tempo. We recommend paying attention to: The US ordered Anthropic to limit foreign access to its most powerful AI models over national security concerns; Anthropic complied but is suing, warning the rule could halt frontier deployments. Compare vx-underground’s 30 TB archive with VirusTotal’s reported 31 PB of malware samples, visualized as stacks of 1 TB drives to show storage scale and research implications. The US directed Anthropic to block foreign access to Fable 5 and Mythos 5 over national security, prompting Anthropic to disable the models worldwide and sparking industry debate

Location and population
Ukraine; targeting girls aged 12–13 years old.
Event
Ukraine is distributing over 38,000 doses of Gardasil-9 vaccine for free HPV vaccination, as reported by Ukrinform.
Scale, impact and risk
38,000 doses delivered; protects against nine HPV types, including high-risk types 16 and 18, which are the main causes of cervical cancer; part of a nationwide cervical cancer prevention program.
Response, status and uncertainty
Vaccine underwent state laboratory quality control before distribution; procured through a two-year managed-access agreement with Merck Sharp & Dohme; remaining doses expected by end of 2026; regional centers managing distribution; national vaccine supply remains stable.

Over 52 Lakh Girls Receive Free HPV Vaccine Under Nationwide Campaign For Preventing Cervical Cancer: Centre - ETV Bharat

Over 52 Lakh Girls Receive Free HPV Vaccine Under Nationwide Campaign For Preventing Cervical Cancer: Centre - ETV Bharat
新闻简报India's Ministry of Health and Family Welfare has reported that over 52 lakh girls have been vaccinated against Human Papillomavirus (HPV) under a nationwide campaign launched on February 28, 2026. The initiative, targeting approximately 1.2 crore eligible girls aged 14 years and above across all 36 States and Union Territories, aims to prevent cervical cancer by providing free HPV vaccines through public healthcare facilities. As of July 15, 2026, 52,10,302 girls had received the vaccine. The campaign is monitored via the U-WIN portal and includes extensive training of healthcare personnel and awareness activities. This vaccination drive is part of a comprehensive national program that also incorporates screening, early diagnosis, and treatment services to combat cervical cancer.
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026 年 7 月 21 日;活动时间:2026年2月28日(活动启动)至2026年7月15日(最新报告数据)。
地点与对象
印度全国36个邦和联邦领土的运动;目标人群:约1.2亿符合条件的14岁及以上女孩。
事件
确认卫生和家庭福利部发起的全国性 HPV 疫苗接种 运动,以预防 宫颈癌;截至 2026 年 7 月 15 日,已有 52,10,302 名女孩接种了疫苗。
规模、影响与风险
52.1万女孩已接种疫苗;活动目标为1.2千万女孩;风险未明确说明;该倡议旨在通过预防减少宫颈癌负担。
应对、状态与不确定性
通过U-WIN门户监控活动的实时数据;政府开展宣传活动和人员培训;综合方法包括筛选、诊断 和 治疗;来源限制:已审查完整的原始文章。

Replying to an unstarred question by BJP MP Dr K Laxman and Congress MP S Niranjan Reddy, Minister of State for Health and Family Welfare Anupriya Patel said that 52,10,302 girls had received the HPV vaccine across the country as of July 15, 2026. The vaccination campaign is being monitored through the U-WIN portal, the Ministry of Health and Family Welfare’s digital vaccination platform, which captures real-time coverage data. The minister said the Centre is also undertaking a large-scale awareness campaign to improve vaccine acceptance and address hesitancy. Information, Education and Communication (IEC) activities include radio jingles, posters, banners, myth-busting digital content and social media campaigns, with special emphasis on reaching girls in rural, tribal and urban slum areas.Highlighting the government’s broader strategy to combat cervical cancer, Patel said the HPV vaccination drive is part of a comprehensive national programme that also includes screening, early diagnosis through Ayushman Arogya Mandirs, and treatment at Day Care Cancer Centres (DCCCs). “非常严重的问题”:德里高等法院指出 HPV 疫苗 推广活动存在安全问题 - 印度快报

‘Very serious issue’: Delhi High Court flags safety issues in HPV vaccine rollout drive - The Indian Express
新闻简报德里高等法院对默克公司的 HPV 疫苗 Gardasil 的安全性提出了严重关切,该产品是根据印度全民免疫计划推出的。法院强调了之前临床试验中的违规行为和不良事件,包括 2009 年至 2010 年古吉拉特邦和安得拉邦 疫苗接种 活动期间报告的死亡事件,该活动于 2013 年停止。尽管存在这些问题,政府仍于 2026 年 2 月启动了当前的活动,为 14 岁女孩提供单剂疫苗。法院已要求提供有关不良事件的数据,并要求卫生官员提供简报,下一次听证会定于 7 月 29 日进行。请愿者认为,鉴于 疫苗 的安全问题历史,透明度至关重要。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 22 日;该活动涉及 2009-2010 年 HPV 疫苗 推出和 2026 年 疫苗接种 驱动器。
地点与对象
印度,特别是德里高等法院;受影响人群包括符合全民免疫计划资格的 14 岁女孩。
事件
德里高等法院指出了默克目前在全民免疫计划下推出的 HPV 疫苗 Gardasil 的安全问题,并援引了之前的不良事件和临床试验中的违规行为。
规模、影响与风险
2026 疫苗接种 驱动器免费提供单剂四价 HPV 疫苗,以预防 宫颈癌;此前于 2009 年至 2010 年推出的计划导致了死亡报告,并因严重违规行为于 2013 年停止。
应对、状态与不确定性
法院已要求卫生和家庭福利部提供情况通报,向 ICMR 和 CDSCO 发出通知,并寻求对公益诉讼的回应;据报道,有关不良事件的数据尚未公开,该案将于 7 月 29 日再次审理。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

‘Very serious issue’: Delhi High Court flags safety issues in HPV vaccine rollout drive - The Indian Express

News Brief
The Delhi High Court has raised serious concerns about the safety of Merck's HPV vaccine Gardasil, which is being rolled out under India's Universal Immunisation Programme. The court highlighted previous irregularities in clinical trials and adverse events, including deaths reported during a 2009-2010 vaccination drive in Gujarat and Andhra Pradesh, which led to a halt in 2013. Despite these issues, the government launched the current drive in February 2026, offering a single dose to 14-year-old girls. The court has demanded data on adverse events and requested a briefing from health officials, with the next hearing scheduled for July 29. The petitioners argue that transparency is crucial given the vaccine's history of safety concerns.
Original source text (English)

The government in February rolled out the vaccination drive, which offers a single dose of quadrivalent HPV free of cost to eligible 14-year-old girls to prevent cervical cancer. Gardasil 9 is a vaccine that protects against nine strains of HPV. The Parliamentary Standing Committee Report in 2013 had found “grave irregularities in the conduct of clinical trials, leading to a halt in the rollout. It issued notice to the ministry, Indian Council of Medical Research (ICMR) and Central Drugs Standard Control Organisation (CDSCO), and has sought their responses to the issues raised in the public interest litigation (PIL). Cervical cancer screening in Kathmandu finds 90 women positive for HPV - The Kathmandu Post

News Brief
A cervical cancer screening program in Kathmandu, Nepal, has identified 90 women testing positive for human papillomavirus (HPV). The screening, conducted in the Kathmandu Valley, highlights ongoing public health efforts to detect HPV infections, which are linked to cervical cancer. While the exact scale of the outbreak or subsequent health impacts remains unspecified in the report, the findings underscore the importance of regular screening in early detection and prevention. No details on treatment protocols or follow-up measures were provided in the available evidence.

官方劝告卢迪亚纳居民确保所有女孩按时接种 HPV 疫苗 - 论坛报

Official exhorts Ludhiana residents to ensure all girls get HPV vaccine on time - The Tribune
新闻简报在印度卢迪亚纳,卫生官员正在加紧努力,确保所有 14 岁女孩都接种 HPV 疫苗,他们称这是针对 宫颈癌 的最佳防御措施。地区免疫官员 Harpreet Singh 博士和民事外科医生 Ramandeep Kaur 博士向家长、教师和社区领袖发出呼吁,宣传 疫苗接种 并反击错误信息。该活动于 2 月 28 日开始,已为 1,400 名女孩接种了疫苗。官员们强调了 疫苗 的安全性和有效性,并指出在潜在的 HPV 暴露之前服用它可以提供最大程度的保护。他们强调,宫颈癌 在很大程度上可以通过 疫苗接种 进行预防,并且超过 150 个国家已成功将 HPV 疫苗 纳入其国家免疫计划。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-07-22。HPV 疫苗接种 活动于 2 月 28 日发起并正在进行中。
地点与对象
印度卢迪亚纳区。目标人群为14岁女孩。
事件
地区免疫官员 Harpreet Singh 博士和民事外科医生 Ramandeep Kaur 博士敦促居民确保所有符合条件的女孩接受 HPV 疫苗,以预防 宫颈癌。该活动被描述为官方健康倡议。
规模、影响与风险
截至报告时,已有 1,400 名女孩接种了疫苗。卫生官员表示,该倡议旨在保护少女免受 宫颈癌 的侵害,宫颈癌 被认为是妇女面临的最严重的健康威胁之一。通过及时使用疫苗接种,可以显着降低患宫颈癌的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗接种 活动正在卢迪亚纳的政府卫生机构中开展。官方正在积极鼓励疫苗接种,并呼吁各社区领袖传播准确的信息。对 疫苗 的轻微反应被认为通常是温和且短暂的。官员们正在积极反击社交媒体上流传的错误信息。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Official exhorts Ludhiana residents to ensure all girls get HPV vaccine on time - The Tribune

News Brief
In Ludhiana, India, health officials are intensifying efforts to ensure all 14-year-old girls receive the HPV vaccine, which they describe as the best defense against cervical cancer. District Immunisation Officer Dr Harpreet Singh and Civil Surgeon Dr Ramandeep Kaur have launched an appeal to parents, teachers, and community leaders to promote vaccination and counter misinformation. The campaign, which began on February 28, has already vaccinated 1,400 girls. Officials emphasize the vaccine's safety and effectiveness, noting that it provides maximum protection when administered before potential HPV exposure. They highlight that cervical cancer is largely preventable through vaccination and that over 150 countries have successfully integrated HPV vaccines into their national immunization programs.
Original source text (English)
Official exhorts Ludhiana residents to ensure all girls get HPV vaccine on time Ludhiana district immunisation officer assures all parents that vaccine has been extensively tested and is completely safe, says it is the best defence against cervical cancer District Immunisation Officer Dr Harpreet Singh has urged locals to ensure that all girls aged 14 get human papilloma virus (HPV) vaccine, pointing out that cervical cancer is one of the few types of the fatal disease that can largely be prevented through timely vaccination. HPV vaccination campaign, launched on February 28, is underway across government health institutions in the district. Till now 1,400 girls have been vaccinated and department has urged parents to get their daughters vaccinated at the earliest. Health officials said the initiative is aimed at protecting adolescent girls from cervical cancer — one of the most serious health threats faced by women. Civil Surgeon Dr Ramandeep Kaur appealed to parents, teachers, schools, healthcare workers, Panchayati Raj Institutions, social and religious leaders and the public to spread accurate information on the vaccine and encourage all eligible girls to get the vaccine. Dr Harpreet said that nearly all cases of cervical cancer are caused by persistent infection with high-risk HPV, which is a very common virus that can infect both women and men. While most HPV infections clear naturally, some persist for years and may eventually develop into cervical cancer. Dr Harpreet Singh added that the HPV vaccine is scientifically proven, safe and highly effective in protecting against the HPV types responsible for the majority of cervical cancer cases. The vaccine provides maximum protection when administered at the recommended age (after completion of 14 years and before the 15th birthday) before exposure to the virus, significantly reducing the risk of developing cervical cancer later in life. He said more than 150 countries and territories have already included the HPV vaccine in their national immunisation programmes. Countries such as Australia, UK, US, Canada, New Zealand, Rwanda, Bhutan, Malaysia, Singapore the UAE, and several European nations have reported substantial reductions in HPV infections and precancerous cervical lesions following HPV vaccination. The World Health Organization (WHO) has also recognised HPV vaccination as one of the most effective strategies for eliminating cervical cancer as a public health problem. Dr Harpreet said girls who do not receive the HPV vaccine remain at risk of acquiring high-risk HPV infection later in life. Persistent infection can lead to abnormal changes in the cervix, which, if left untreated, may progress to cervical cancer. Since cervical cancer usually does not produce noticeable symptoms in its early stages, vaccination is the most effective preventive measure. He emphasised that the safety and effectiveness of HPV vaccines have been established through extensive scientific research worldwide. Mild reactions, such as pain, redness, swelling at the injection site or a low-grade fever, may occur in some individuals but they usually resolve on their own within a short period. Dr Harpreet urged parents not to believe rumours or misinformation circulating on social media and seek information from qualified doctors and government health institutions. He urged all sections of the society to play an active role in ensuring that every eligible girl benefits from HPV vaccination. He said a well-informed decision can protect “our daughters from cervical cancer in the future”, and appealed to all parents to contact their nearest government hospital, community health centre, primary health centre or healthcare worker for information regarding HPV vaccination
View five news elements

Event
District Immunisation Officer Dr Harpreet Singh and Civil Surgeon Dr Ramandeep Kaur are urging residents to ensure all eligible girls receive the HPV vaccine to prevent cervical cancer. The campaign is described as an official health initiative.
Scale, impact and risk
As of reporting, 1,400 girls have been vaccinated. Health officials state the initiative aims to protect adolescent girls from cervical cancer, which is described as one of the most serious health threats faced by women. The risk of developing cervical cancer is significantly reduced through timely vaccination.
Response, status and uncertainty
The vaccination campaign is underway across government health institutions in Ludhiana. Officials are actively encouraging vaccination and appealing to various community leaders to spread accurate information. Mild reactions to the vaccine are acknowledged as generally mild and short-lived. Officials are actively countering misinformation circulating on social media.

B routine priorityPublic-health news

HPV vaccination cannot be made mandatory, parents must decide: Karnataka Health Minister - The New Indian Express

News Brief
Karnataka Health Minister U.T. Khader has clarified that the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine cannot be made mandatory in the state, emphasizing that the decision to vaccinate adolescent girls ultimately rests with their parents. Speaking in Mangaluru, Khader noted that while the state's HPV vaccination campaign has been largely successful, some areas, particularly in the coastal region, have seen lower acceptance rates. In response, the Health Department is intensifying awareness campaigns to address misconceptions and encourage vaccination. The minister also addressed other matters, including the proposed extension of free medical treatment for road accident victims and clarification of his previous remarks on dealing with rowdy elements. The state government is working to streamline the implementation of the PM-Rahat scheme to ensure timely cashless treatment for accident victims.
Original source text (English)
KarnatakaHPV vaccination cannot be made mandatory, parents must decide: Karnataka Health Minister U.T. Khader says the state will step up awareness campaigns to boost vaccine uptake and clarifies his remarks on dealing with rowdy elements. MANGALURU: Health and Family Welfare Minister U.T. Khader on Wednesday said the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine cannot be made mandatory, asserting that the decision to vaccinate adolescent girls ultimately rests with their parents. Addressing reporters in Mangaluru, Khader said the state's HPV vaccination campaign has largely been successful, although a few pockets, including parts of the coastal region, have witnessed lower acceptance. He said the Health Department is intensifying awareness campaigns to address misconceptions and encourage vaccination. "We can create awareness and educate people about the benefits of the vaccine, but we cannot make it compulsory. It is for the parents to decide whether their daughters should receive the vaccine," he said. Khader also announced that the state government has proposed extending free medical treatment of up to Rs 2.5 lakh for road accident victims for the first seven days after an accident. The proposed scheme will be integrated with the Centre's PM-Rahat initiative, under which the Centre provides financial assistance of Rs 1.5 lakh. The Karnataka government plans to contribute an additional Rs 1 lakh, taking the total assistance to Rs 2.5 lakh. He said the PM-Rahat scheme has not been implemented effectively in Karnataka due to certain operational issues, and the government is working to streamline its implementation so that accident victims receive timely cashless treatment. Responding to a question on his recent remarks suggesting that the police should "shoot" rowdy elements—a statement that drew criticism—Khader clarified that his comments had been misconstrued. "I never said the police should shoot anyone. What I meant was that the police should go a step further in dealing with habitual offenders and create a psychological fear among rowdy elements," he maintained. The New Indian Express www.newindianexpress.com

疾控专家建议年满13周岁女孩应按规定接种国家免疫规划HPV疫苗 - Sohu

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报中国已正式将HPV 疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,针对2011年11月10日后出生的13岁及以上女童。该政策自2025年11月10日起生效,既是一项健康权利,也是一项法律义务。二价 HPV 疫苗 预计可预防约 80% 的 宫颈癌 病例。卫生当局强调,13 岁是 疫苗接种 的最佳年龄,因为它在潜在接触之前提供最高的抗体水平和保护。该举措旨在解决中国不断上升的 宫颈癌 发病率问题,到 2022 年,中国的 宫颈癌 病例占全球病例的 22.75%,报告新增确诊病例 150,600 例。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
疾控专家建议年满13周岁女孩应按规定接种国家免疫规划HPV疫苗 - Sohu
News Brief
China has officially integrated the HPV vaccine into its national immunization program, targeting girls aged 13 and older born after November 10, 2011. The policy, effective from November 10, 2025, is presented as both a health right and a legal obligation. The bivalent HPV vaccine is expected to prevent approximately 80% of cervical cancer cases. Health authorities emphasize that 13 is the optimal age for vaccination, as it provides the highest antibody levels and protection before potential exposure. This initiative addresses China's rising cervical cancer rates, which accounted for 22.75% of global cases in 2022, with 150,600 new diagnoses reported.

顶部 首页 首页 雾 / 26° 隐私 疾控专家建议年满13周岁女孩应按规定接种国家免疫规划HPV疫苗 央广网 央广网哈尔滨7月23日消息(记者马俊玮)“2025年10月,国家疾控局联合七部门印发通知,明确自2025年11月10日起,将HPV疫苗正式纳入国家免疫规划,目标人群为2011年11月10日后出生年满13周岁的女孩。”黑龙江省疾病预防控制局副局长张冰欣在7月23日召开的发布会上说,按规定接种国家免疫规划HPV疫苗既是自身健康保障权利,也是应当依法履行的义务。 黑龙江省疾病预防控制局副局长张冰欣介绍相关情况(央广网发 纪勇 摄) HPV(即人乳头瘤病毒)已知亚型超200种,分为高危型与低危型。其中,高危型HPV持续感染是诱发宫颈癌的核心原因,低危型则主要引发生殖器疣等良性病变。近年来,我国宫颈癌发病率持续攀升且呈明显年轻化趋势,已成为威胁女性身心健康的重大公共卫生问题。数据显示,2022年我国宫颈癌新发病例15.06万人,占全球总量22.75%。 张冰欣介绍,接种HPV疫苗是阻断病毒感染、预防宫颈癌的最有效保护屏障,也是最经济、最高效的预防手段。本次纳入国家免疫规划的双价HPV疫苗,能够预防约80%的宫颈癌发生。13周岁是综合科学论证后的最佳接种年龄。从感染规律来看,HPV主要通过性行为传播,13周岁接种可在感染高峰来临前建立稳固免疫屏障,实现最优防护效果。从免疫效果来看,9至14岁女孩接种疫苗后产生的抗体水平是15岁以上接种产生抗体水平的2倍以上。13周岁接种,是兼顾保护效果、成本效益的最佳年龄段。 Free web novels for online reading. 评论 全部 还没有人评论过,快来抢首评 抢首评 推荐阅读 7.10日:德不配位必有灾殃!央视表态仅6天,韩红再迎噩耗,孙浩曝内幕 旅卉聊历史 2026.7.09 257.6万+ 80%的女性都曾感染过HPV!身上长了这个东西的,要特别注意 朝元的小世... 昨天04:38 5312 HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,接种点迎来暑期高峰 三湘都市报 前天15:43 2264 Last call to fall in love with summer. 沃森生物:子公司收到双价HPV疫苗坦桑尼亚药品注册证书 - Sohu

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报沃森生物宣布,子公司玉溪泽润生物科技有限公司的二价HPV 疫苗(WALRINVAX)获得坦桑尼亚药品和医疗器械管理局药品注册证。此次批准允许 疫苗 的小瓶产品在坦桑尼亚上市,这将有助于预防 高危型HPV 16 型和 18 型引起的疾病。该公告标志着扩大 疫苗 在该地区的可及性迈出了重要一步,尽管具体的推出细节或公共卫生影响数据尚未披露。

时间
发表于2026-07-23;事件最近发生(证据中未指定确切日期)。
地点与对象
坦桑尼亚;证据中未具体说明受影响的人口。
事件
沃森生物科技子公司玉溪泽润生物科技有限公司的二价HPV 疫苗(商品名“WALRINVAX”)获得坦桑尼亚药品和医疗器械管理局颁发的药品注册证,获得了在坦桑尼亚的小瓶上市销售许可。
规模、影响与风险
没有报告病例、死亡或具体风险水平;该事件是 疫苗 分发的监管批准。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗获准在坦桑尼亚上市;所提供的证据中没有报告有关调查、测试或实施状况的进一步细节。

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
沃森生物:子公司收到双价HPV疫苗坦桑尼亚药品注册证书 - Sohu
News Brief
Watson Biotechnology announced that its subsidiary, Yuxi Zerun Biotechnology Technology Co., Ltd., has obtained a Drug Registration Certificate from Tanzania's Medicines and Medical Devices Administration for its bivalent HPV vaccine (WALRINVAX). This approval permits the vaccine's vial presentation to be marketed in Tanzania, where it will help prevent diseases caused by high-risk HPV types 16 and 18. The announcement marks a significant step in expanding the vaccine's accessibility in the region, though specific rollout details or public health impact data were not disclosed.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

沃森生物:关于子公司收到双价HPV疫苗坦桑尼亚药品注册证书的公告 - 东方财富

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报沃森生物科技于2026年7月23日宣布,其子公司玉溪泽润生物科技的二价HPV 疫苗已获得坦桑尼亚药品和医疗器械管理局的药品注册证。疫苗 由毕赤酵母生产,被批准用于小瓶销售,用于预防 HPV16 和 HPV18(包括 宫颈癌)引起的疾病。这一监管里程碑是在 疫苗 于 2024 年 8 月获得世界卫生组织资格预审之后实现的,这为国际分销铺平了道路。坦桑尼亚的批准使 疫苗 能够在该国上市,满足 HPV 相关疾病预防的重大公共卫生需求。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-23。事件日期:2024-08(世卫组织预认证),2026-07-23(注册证书签发)。
地点与对象
目标人群:有 HPV16 和 HPV18 感染风险的个体,特别是 宫颈癌 预防。
事件
已确认的监管事件。沃森生物科技子公司玉溪泽润生物科技的二价HPV 疫苗(产自毕赤酵母)获得坦桑尼亚药品和医疗器械管理局药品注册证。疫苗 被批准用于小瓶装,用于预防 HPV16 和 HPV18(包括 宫颈癌)引起的疾病。
规模、影响与风险
没有病例数、死亡或暴露报告。该事件标志着监管部门批准 疫苗 在坦桑尼亚的分销。证据中未说明风险级别。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗已于2024年8月获得WHO预认证,表明国际监管机构已接受。坦桑尼亚注册是国家层面的市场准入批准。没有提及正在进行的调查或未解决的信息。来源限制:已审查完整的原始文章正文。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
沃森生物:关于子公司收到双价HPV疫苗坦桑尼亚药品注册证书的公告 - 东方财富
News Brief
Watson Biotechnology announced on July 23, 2026, that its subsidiary Yuxi Zerun Biotechnology has received a drug registration certificate from Tanzania's Medicines and Medical Devices Administration for its bivalent HPV vaccine. The vaccine, produced in Pichia pastoris, is approved for vial presentation and indicated for preventing diseases caused by HPV16 and HPV18, including cervical cancer. This regulatory milestone follows the vaccine's World Health Organization prequalification in August 2024, which paved the way for international distribution. The Tanzanian approval enables the vaccine's commercial availability in the country, addressing a significant public health need for HPV-related disease prevention.
Original source text (Chinese)

View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 2026-07-23. Event date: 2024-08 (WHO pre-certification), 2026-07-23 (registration certificate issuance).
Location and population
Target population: Individuals at risk of HPV16 and HPV18 infections, particularly for cervical cancer prevention.
Event
Confirmed regulatory event. Watson Biotechnology's subsidiary, Yuxi Zerun Biotechnology, received a drug registration certificate from Tanzania's Medicines and Medical Devices Administration for its bivalent HPV vaccine (produced in Pichia pastoris). The vaccine is approved for vial presentation and indicated for preventing diseases caused by HPV16 and HPV18, including cervical cancer.
Scale, impact and risk
No case counts, deaths, or exposures reported. The event signifies regulatory approval for vaccine distribution in Tanzania. Risk level not stated in evidence.
Response, status and uncertainty
The vaccine has already obtained WHO prequalification in August 2024, indicating international regulatory acceptance. The Tanzanian registration is a national-level approval for market access. No ongoing investigation or unresolved information mentioned. Source limitation: Full original article body was reviewed.
